RENDER MODEL (WHAT THE USER SEES ON SIGNUP)

Render order:

Global Master (Document 1 Global)

Country/Region Addendum

Optional: localized Privacy Notice link + Risk Disclosures link (separate docs, not required inside the contract text)

Conflict rule: Addendum overrides Global Master only for that jurisdiction.

Acceptance record should store:

globalDocVersion, addendumId, addendumVersion

countrySelected, regionDerived

timestamp, ip/session/device fingerprint (as permitted)

hash of rendered HTML/PDF text (tamper evidence)

ALGORITHM (PSEUDOCODE) — SELECT + RENDER + LOG ACCEPTANCE
INPUTS:
  userCountry (ISO2 from signup dropdown)
  userRegion (derived from ISO2 if needed)
  docs:
    globalMasterDoc("DOC1_GLOBAL", version)
    addendumDocs[addendumId](version)
    addendumMap (JSON above)

FUNCTION pickAddendum(userCountry, userRegion):
  if userCountry in addendumMap.countryAddendum:
      return addendumMap.countryAddendum[userCountry]
  else if userRegion in addendumMap.regionFallback:
      return addendumMap.regionFallback[userRegion]
  else:
      return addendumMap.defaultAddendum

FUNCTION renderAgreement(userCountry, userRegion):
  addendumId = pickAddendum(userCountry, userRegion)
  globalText  = load("DOC1_GLOBAL", "1.0")
  addendumText = load(addendumId, "1.0")

  rendered = concatenate(
      globalText,
      "\n\n---\n\n",
      "COUNTRY/REGION ADDENDUM (Auto-selected): " + addendumId,
      "\n\n",
      addendumText
  )

  return { rendered, addendumId, globalVersion:"1.0", addendumVersion:"1.0" }

ON SIGNUP SCREEN:
  when user selects country:
     addendumId = pickAddendum(country, derivedRegion(country))
     show label: "Terms loaded for: {country} / {addendumId}"
     render preview (scroll box)
  on click "I Accept":
     payload = renderAgreement(country, derivedRegion(country))
     hash = sha256(payload.rendered)
     store acceptance:
        userId, country, region, globalVersion, addendumId, addendumVersion,
        hash, acceptedAt, ip, userAgent, sessionId
     proceed to create account

IMPLEMENTATION ADVICE: OPTION A VS OPTION B (PRACTICAL)

Option A (Global + Addendum) is usually best for maintainability: one master contract + localized deltas.

Option B (full doc per country) is simpler to render (one doc) but becomes operationally heavy (dozens of near-duplicate documents, higher legal update risk).

Given your scale, Option A is the better long-term system.

If you want the next step after this: I can produce a clean “document fragment pack” format (IDs, versions, and markdown/HTML blocks) so your signup renderer can load these from DB or filesystem with deterministic hashing and audit logging.

DO AND PRODUCE THE DOCUMENT AS WILL BE LOADED WORD FOR WORD AND CODE BLOCK BY CODE BLOCK 
Thought for 3m 3s

Below is the exact “Option A pack” you can load word-for-word in your signup renderer.

Global Master (DOC1_GLOBAL) is always loaded.

One Addendum is auto-selected based on the trader’s country selection (with region fallback and a “ROW” default).

Includes your required change: 21 days response window, sealed bypass/instant-start loopholes, and updated remedies: 40% (employment) / 4% (AUM for 12 months).

FILE: /legal/doc1/DOC1_GLOBAL_v1.0.txt
TRADEQUIP PLATFORM PARTICIPATION + OPPORTUNITY RIGHTS AGREEMENT (GLOBAL MASTER)
Version: 1.0
Effective Date: The date/time you click “I Accept” (electronic acceptance)

IMPORTANT NOTICE
This Agreement is a legally binding contract. It contains:
• Opportunity Rights (Right of First Opportunity/Refusal, notice, and non-circumvention)
• Standstill rules that prohibit “starting now and papering later”
• Fees/liquidated damages for circumvention (40% of first-year cash comp or 4% of allocated AUM for 12 months)
• Data processing and cross-border transfer terms

1) PARTIES
This Agreement is between:
(A) TradeQuip Platform Operator: {{TRADEQUIP_LEGAL_NAME}} (together with its affiliates, the “TradeQuip Group”), and
(B) You, the individual accepting these terms (“Trader”, “you”).

2) STRUCTURE (GLOBAL MASTER + COUNTRY/REGION ADDENDUM)
2.1 Global Master Applies to All Traders.
This Global Master applies to all Platform users.

2.2 Country/Region Addendum Also Applies.
When you select your country at signup, the Platform automatically loads a Country/Region Addendum (the “Addendum”).
The Addendum forms part of this Agreement and may modify or override this Global Master for that jurisdiction.

2.3 Precedence.
If there is a conflict between this Global Master and your Addendum, your Addendum controls to the extent necessary for your country/region.

3) PURPOSE AND OPPORTUNITY STATEMENT
3.1 Platform Purpose.
TradeQuip operates a trader discovery, growth, vetting, and selection platform (the “Platform”) designed to identify trading talent and provide pathways to professional opportunities, including employment, placement, and/or capital allocation.

3.2 Opportunity Creation and Exchange.
You acknowledge:
(a) TradeQuip invests substantial resources in building the Platform, evaluation logic, surveillance and integrity controls, ranking systems, and partner networks;
(b) your participation may create or expand professional opportunities you may not otherwise access; and
(c) in exchange for access to this opportunity pathway, you agree to the Opportunity Rights, notice obligations, and anti-circumvention provisions in this Agreement.

3.3 No Guarantee.
Participation does not guarantee selection, employment, compensation, funding, capital allocation, or any particular outcome.

4) DEFINITIONS (CORE)
4.1 “Addendum” means the Country/Region Addendum loaded for your selected country at signup.

4.2 “Affiliate” means an entity that controls, is controlled by, or is under common control with TradeQuip.

4.3 “Confidential Information” means all non-public information about TradeQuip or the Platform, including selection logic, scoring/ranking criteria, surveillance methods, risk rules, partner lists, introductions, and any non-public reports or communications.

4.4 “Industry” or “Trading & Capital Management Industry” includes: proprietary trading, hedge funds, asset management, managed accounts, family offices, bank markets desks, broker-dealers, funds/vehicles, and any role involving trading discretion, portfolio management authority, execution authority, strategy research intended for trading P&L, or management of third-party capital.

4.5 “Opportunity” means any offer, invitation, interview, negotiation, term sheet, letter, arrangement, or engagement (employment, contractor, advisor, PM/trader role, funded seat, AUM allocation, seeding, team/pod build, or fund formation) in the Industry.

4.6 “Platform-Linked Opportunity” means an Opportunity that arises from or is connected to your Platform participation, including (without limitation) your ranking, visibility, referrals, TradeQuip introductions, partner discovery through the Platform, or use of TradeQuip evaluation outputs.

4.7 “Partner Firm” means any third party with whom TradeQuip has or is negotiating a relationship for hiring, placement, capital programs, or professional opportunities.

4.8 “Restricted Bypass Conduct” includes any attempt to evade this Agreement, including: routing through intermediaries; using an affiliate/entity you control; labeling an Industry role as “consulting” to conceal trading/capital authority; beginning work before paperwork; receiving credentials/system access; side letters; delayed notice; or any action intended to avoid TradeQuip’s Opportunity Rights.

4.9 “Notice” means a written notice delivered per Section 8, containing the information in Schedule N (Notice Form).

5) PLATFORM ACCESS, ACCOUNT INTEGRITY, AND ACCEPTABLE USE
5.1 Accurate Information.
You must provide accurate and current account information and keep it updated.

5.2 One Person / Integrity.
You must not create multiple accounts to manipulate eligibility, rankings, or evaluation outcomes unless TradeQuip authorizes in writing.

5.3 Prohibited Acts.
You must not: scrape/reverse engineer the Platform; manipulate metrics; use bots to simulate activity; share credentials; or disclose TradeQuip Confidential Information.

6) CONFIDENTIALITY, TRADE SECRETS, AND PLATFORM IP
6.1 Confidentiality.
You will protect Confidential Information and will not use or disclose it except as necessary for legitimate Platform participation.

6.2 Survival.
Confidentiality and trade secret protections survive termination indefinitely to the maximum extent permitted by law.

6.3 No Publicity Without Consent.
You may not use TradeQuip’s name, Partner Firm names, or “Selected” status in marketing without written consent, except where required by law.

7) STATUS LEVELS THAT TRIGGER OPPORTUNITY RIGHTS
Opportunity Rights apply based on your status:

7.1 “General Member” (default).
You have confidentiality/integrity obligations but no exclusivity unless and until you become an Opportunity Candidate or Selected.

7.2 “Opportunity Candidate”.
TradeQuip notifies you in writing (in-app and/or email) that you are eligible for interviews/introductions/placement/capital evaluation.

7.3 “Selected Group”.
TradeQuip notifies you in writing (in-app and/or email) that you are shortlisted/selected for hire, placement, or capital allocation processes.

8) OPPORTUNITY RIGHTS (RIGHT OF FIRST OPPORTUNITY/REFUSAL + NOTICE + ANTI-CIRCUMVENTION)
8.1 Right of First Opportunity (ROFO) — Opportunity Candidate Window.
If you become an Opportunity Candidate, you grant TradeQuip a Right of First Opportunity for a period of six (6) months starting on the date of TradeQuip’s Candidate notice (the “Candidate Window”).
During the Candidate Window:
(a) TradeQuip has the first right to present you to Partner Firms and propose TradeQuip Group roles/capital programs; and
(b) you must route Industry Opportunities through the Notice process (Section 9) before accepting, commencing, or negotiating substantive terms.

8.2 Right of First Refusal (ROFR) — Selected Group.
If you become Selected, you grant TradeQuip a Right of First Refusal with respect to:
(a) any TradeQuip Group offer made to you; and
(b) any Platform-Linked Opportunity offered by a third party.
ROFR is exercised through the Notice process (Section 9).

8.3 Capital Control (Fundraising / Capital Management) — Selected Group.
If you become Selected, then for three (3) years from the date you become Selected (the “Capital Control Period”), you must provide Notice before you:
(a) solicit or raise third-party capital for trading/investment products;
(b) form or operate a fund/vehicle/managed account program; or
(c) accept any discretionary management of third-party capital,
and you must allow TradeQuip the ROFR/ROFO opportunity to employ/place/endorse you or decline in writing.
If your Addendum requires a shorter term or different structure, your Addendum controls.

8.4 Construction to Maximize Enforceability.
These Opportunity Rights are intended as commercial opportunity rights, notice, and anti-circumvention protections. If any part is construed as an unlawful restraint in your jurisdiction, it automatically converts to the maximum enforceable form (including confidentiality, non-circumvention, non-solicitation, and Platform-Linked ROFR/notice) without invalidating the remainder.

9) NOTICE PROCESS (21 DAYS) + SEALED “IMMEDIATE START” LOOPHOLES
9.1 When Notice Is Required.
If you are in a Candidate Window or Selected status, or if the matter is a Platform-Linked Opportunity, you must provide Notice before you:
(a) accept, sign, commence, or begin onboarding for any Industry Opportunity;
(b) negotiate substantive terms (comp, AUM, start date, duties, exclusivity, revenue split, carry);
(c) announce fundraising or commence capital solicitation; or
(d) commence trading or managing capital for a third party.

9.2 Notice Must Be Complete.
A Notice is “complete” only if it includes all items in Schedule N (Notice Form). The response clock does not start until Notice is complete.

9.3 Standstill During Response Period.
From delivery of a complete Notice until the earlier of (i) TradeQuip’s written response, or (ii) expiration of the response process, you must not:
• accept/sign the Opportunity,
• commence work/trading/capital management,
• receive credentials, system access, or onboarding materials beyond scheduling,
• enter side letters, “trial” arrangements, shadow roles, or indirect roles,
• route through an affiliate/entity/intermediary.

9.4 TradeQuip Response Period: 21 Calendar Days.
TradeQuip has twenty-one (21) calendar days after confirmed receipt of a complete Notice to:
(a) match or present a substantially equivalent alternative (employment/capital/placement),
(b) approve the third-party Opportunity in writing,
(c) decline in writing, or
(d) request missing information (which tolls the response clock until provided).

9.5 Final Notice and Cooling-Off (No “Silent Expiry” Loophole).
If TradeQuip does not respond within the 21-day period, you must send a “Final Notice” (email + in-app submission) stating the response period has elapsed.
You must then wait an additional seven (7) calendar days before accepting or commencing the Opportunity.
This Final Notice and cooling-off requirement is an essential anti-circumvention control.

9.6 No “New Terms” After Notice.
If you proceed after approval/decline, you may proceed only on the terms disclosed in your Notice. Materially improved terms require a new Notice.

10) NON-CIRCUMVENTION AND NON-INTERFERENCE
10.1 No Circumvention.
You must not engage in Restricted Bypass Conduct or otherwise evade TradeQuip’s Opportunity Rights.

10.2 Platform-Linked Counterparties.
If you engage a Platform-Linked counterparty outside the Notice/ROFR process, it is presumed circumvention unless you prove otherwise.

10.3 Non-Interference.
For twelve (12) months after your last Candidate/Selected status event, you will not intentionally interfere with TradeQuip’s Partner Firm relationships connected to your Platform pathway.

11) FEES / LIQUIDATED DAMAGES / REMEDIES (UPDATED)
11.1 Employment/Engagement Circumvention Fee.
If you breach Sections 8–10 and accept/commence an Industry Opportunity (employment/engagement) without full compliance, you agree to pay TradeQuip a placement-equivalent fee equal to:
FORTY PERCENT (40%) of your first-year total cash compensation.
“First-year total cash compensation” means base salary + guaranteed bonus + sign-on bonus + guaranteed allowances paid or guaranteed within the first 12 months of start.

11.2 Capital Program / AUM Circumvention Fee.
If you breach Sections 8–10 and commence discretionary management of allocated third-party capital without compliance, you agree to pay:
FOUR PERCENT (4%) of the allocated AUM for twelve (12) months,
payable monthly as 1/12 of 4% based on gross AUM allocated to you or to a vehicle you manage/control.

11.3 Who May Pay.
TradeQuip may seek payment from you and, where lawful, from the counterparty and/or any entity you control that receives the economics.

11.4 Not a Penalty; Court Reduction.
The parties agree damages are difficult to estimate and these amounts are a reasonable pre-estimate of loss.
If a court finds any amount is an unenforceable penalty, the amount automatically reduces to the maximum enforceable amount and the remainder of the Agreement remains effective.

11.5 Injunctive Relief / Specific Performance.
Breach may cause irreparable harm. TradeQuip may seek injunctive relief where available, in addition to monetary remedies.

12) DATA PROTECTION AND PRIVACY (GLOBAL BASELINE)
12.1 Processing.
TradeQuip processes personal data and performance data for account creation, platform delivery, security, fraud prevention, evaluation, ranking, selection, compliance, partner placement, dispute handling, and improvement.

12.2 Cross-Border Transfers.
TradeQuip may transfer data cross-border and will implement safeguards where required by applicable law (e.g., contractual clauses, equivalent transfer tools, or local hosting).

12.3 Addendum Controls.
Your Addendum may add jurisdiction-specific privacy terms.

13) GOVERNING LAW / DISPUTE RESOLUTION
Governing law, forum, arbitration (if any), and mandatory local protections are defined in your Addendum.

14) SEVERABILITY / REFORMATION / MAXIMUM ENFORCEABLE CONSTRUCTION
14.1 Severability.
If any provision is invalid, the remainder remains enforceable.

14.2 Reformation.
Where permitted, any overbroad restriction will be narrowed to the minimum scope necessary to be enforceable.

14.3 Maximum Enforceable Construction.
If any duration, scope, or remedy is disallowed, it automatically reduces to the maximum enforceable version.

15) ELECTRONIC ACCEPTANCE
By clicking “I Accept,” you agree to electronic contracting and acknowledge this Agreement is enforceable as a signed writing to the maximum extent permitted by law.

SCHEDULE N — NOTICE FORM (REQUIRED CONTENT)
A Notice is complete only if it includes:
1) Your legal name + Platform username + email
2) Counterparty legal name, address, website, and contact person
3) Role description and duties (including any trading/capital authority)
4) Compensation terms (base/bonus/sign-on/guarantees) and/or AUM terms (allocation size, risk limits, draw, fee split)
5) Start date / deadlines / exclusivity requested
6) All documents received (offer letter, term sheet, recruiter communications)
7) How the counterparty learned of you and whether Platform-linked
8) Any side arrangements, trial roles, or “consulting” labels
9) Your intended response timeline
END OF GLOBAL MASTER

COUNTRY ADDENDUM — UNITED STATES (ADD-US)
Version: 1.0
Applies if you select: United States (US)

1) GOVERNING LAW / FORUM
1.1 Governing law is the law of the U.S. state of your primary residence as selected/recorded at signup, unless the Platform specifies a different state and such choice is enforceable.
1.2 Venue is the competent state or federal courts for your state of residence unless mandatory rules require otherwise.

2) STATE-LAW DEPENDENT RESTRICTION RULE
2.1 If any part of the Global Master is deemed a “non-compete” or otherwise prohibited by your state (including, for example, states that broadly prohibit non-competes), then:
(a) the Agreement will NOT be interpreted to prohibit you from lawful employment generally; and
(b) Opportunity Rights apply only to Platform-Linked Opportunities and TradeQuip-introduced counterparties, and operate as notice + anti-circumvention + confidentiality + non-solicitation to the maximum lawful extent.

2.2 Any standstill is limited to preventing “start-now-paper-later” circumvention for Platform-Linked Opportunities and does not bar independent employment where such restrictions are unlawful.

3) FEES / LIQUIDATED DAMAGES CONSTRUCTION
3.1 The fees in Section 11 are intended as reasonable liquidated damages for circumvention of Platform-Linked Opportunities.
3.2 If a court requires reduction, amounts reduce to the maximum enforceable level without invalidating the remainder.

4) PRIVACY
4.1 TradeQuip processes personal data as described in the Global Master. State privacy laws may apply depending on your location and TradeQuip’s processing footprint.

END OF ADD-US

COUNTRY ADDENDUM — CHINA (MAINLAND) (ADD-CN)
Version: 1.0
Applies if you select: China (CN)

1) GOVERNING LAW / FORUM
1.1 Governing law is the laws of the People’s Republic of China.
1.2 Venue is the competent PRC courts or PRC arbitration as mandated by applicable law.

2) PRC COMPLIANCE AND FEATURE LIMITATION
2.1 TradeQuip may limit, localize, or require additional PRC-specific terms/consents for certain Platform features, data flows, or integrations to maintain compliance.
2.2 If additional PRC terms are required, TradeQuip will present them in-app and they will form part of this Agreement.

3) ENFORCEABILITY CONSTRUCTION
3.1 Opportunity Rights are enforced to the maximum extent permitted and construed primarily as notice + anti-circumvention for Platform-Linked Opportunities and introductions, plus confidentiality.

4) PRIVACY
4.1 TradeQuip will implement PRC-compliant safeguards for covered processing and cross-border transfers, including localization where required.

END OF ADD-CN

RENDER/ASSEMBLY ALGORITHM (IMPLEMENTATION SPEC)
FILE: /legal/doc1/renderer/selection-and-render.algorithm.v1.txt
TRADEQUIP TERMS RENDERER — SELECTION + RENDER + ACCEPTANCE LOGGING
Version: 1.0

INPUTS
- userCountryIso2: string (ISO 3166-1 alpha-2 from signup dropdown)
- addendumMap: /legal/doc1/maps/addendum-map.v1.json
- regionRules: /legal/doc1/maps/region-rules.v1.json
- globalDoc: /legal/doc1/DOC1_GLOBAL_v1.0.txt

STEP 1: SELECT ADDENDUM (COUNTRY OVERRIDE)
If userCountryIso2 exists in addendumMap.countryAddenda:
  addendum = addendumMap.countryAddenda[userCountryIso2]
  return addendum

STEP 2: SELECT ADDENDUM (REGION FALLBACK)
For each rule in regionRules.regionRulesInOrder:
  if userCountryIso2 in rule.iso2:
    addendum = addendumMap.regionAddenda[rule.regionKey]
    return addendum

STEP 3: DEFAULT
Return addendumMap.defaultAddendum (ADD-ROW)

STEP 4: RENDER (CONCATENATION ORDER)
Render string = [
  globalDocText,
  "\n\n---\n\n",
  "AUTO-SELECTED ADDENDUM:\n",
  "Addendum ID: " + addendum.id + "\n",
  "Addendum Version: " + addendum.version + "\n",
  "Country Selected: " + userCountryIso2 + "\n",
  "\n",
  addendumText
].join("")

STEP 5: ACCEPTANCE RECORD (MUST STORE)
On “I Accept”, persist:
- userId (or pre-user signup token)
- userCountryIso2
- selectedAddendumId, selectedAddendumVersion
- globalDocId/version
- renderedTextHash (SHA-256 of exact rendered string bytes)
- acceptedAt (UTC timestamp)
- sessionId (if collected)
- ipAddress + userAgent (if collected and lawful in jurisdiction)
- UI language at acceptance (if applicable)

STEP 6: DISPLAY REQUIREMENTS
- Show a “Terms loaded for: {country} / {addendumId}” label
- Require scroll-to-end OR explicit checkbox + clickwrap (implementation choice)
- Provide a “Download/Print” control if feasible
END

Thought for 2m 12s

I reviewed the attached signup-flow plan and implementation notes. 

Pasted-To-implement-this-dynami…

 The core design is correct (country selection as the “single source of truth” driving both phone validation and legal assembly). The main compliance hardening I’m applying before we proceed is:

Assemble terms on the backend (canonical), not the frontend, so the server controls the exact bytes shown and hashed.

Issue a signed “terms token” (HMAC) tied to {country, addendumId/version, globalVersion, combinedSha256, issuedAt} and require it on signup.

Recompute and verify the hash server-side at signup time, so a client cannot swap text, country, or addendum.

Persist a tamper-evident acceptance ledger (hash-chained rows) in legalAcceptances.

Below is a copy/paste-ready implementation for your stack (Express + TypeScript + Drizzle + better-sqlite3 + React/wouter). All code is inline and organized by file path.

1) Backend: Legal terms engine + routes + audit ledger
1.1 shared/schema.ts — add legalAcceptances table
// FILE: /shared/schema.ts
// Add to your existing schema exports.

import { sqliteTable, text, integer } from "drizzle-orm/sqlite-core";

// ... your existing tables ...

export const legalAcceptances = sqliteTable("legalAcceptances", {
  id: integer("id").primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),

  // User linkage
  userId: integer("user_id"), // FK to users.id (enforce in app logic)
  emailAtAcceptance: text("email_at_acceptance"),

  // Jurisdiction context
  countryIso2: text("country_iso2").notNull(),
  regionKey: text("region_key"), // optional

  // Document identifiers + hashes
  globalDocId: text("global_doc_id").notNull(), // e.g. DOC1_GLOBAL
  globalDocVersion: text("global_doc_version").notNull(), // e.g. 1.0
  globalDocSha256: text("global_doc_sha256").notNull(),

  addendumId: text("addendum_id").notNull(), // e.g. ADD-KE
  addendumVersion: text("addendum_version").notNull(),
  addendumSha256: text("addendum_sha256").notNull(),

  combinedSha256: text("combined_sha256").notNull(),
  combinedText: text("combined_text").notNull(), // store the exact rendered snapshot

  // Acceptance context
  acceptedAt: integer("accepted_at", { mode: "timestamp_ms" }).notNull(),
  ipAddress: text("ip_address"),
  userAgent: text("user_agent"),
  sessionId: text("session_id"),

  // Token presented at acceptance
  termsToken: text("terms_token").notNull(),

  // Tamper-evident ledger chain (global)
  ledgerSeq: integer("ledger_seq").notNull(),
  prevLedgerHash: text("prev_ledger_hash").notNull(),
  ledgerHash: text("ledger_hash").notNull(),
});

1.3 server/legal/cryptoUtils.ts — sha256 + stable stringify + HMAC token
// FILE: /server/legal/cryptoUtils.ts
import crypto from "crypto";

export function sha256Hex(input: string): string {
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(input, "utf8").digest("hex");
}

// Deterministic stringify for hashing ledger payloads
export function stableStringify(value: unknown): string {
  if (value === null || typeof value !== "object") return JSON.stringify(value);

  if (Array.isArray(value)) {
    return "[" + value.map((v) => stableStringify(v)).join(",") + "]";
  }

  const obj = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  const keys = Object.keys(obj).sort();
  return (
    "{" +
    keys
      .map((k) => JSON.stringify(k) + ":" + stableStringify(obj[k]))
      .join(",") +
    "}"
  );
}

export function base64UrlEncode(str: string): string {
  return Buffer.from(str, "utf8")
    .toString("base64")
    .replaceAll("+", "-")
    .replaceAll("/", "_")
    .replaceAll("=", "");
}

export function base64UrlDecode(str: string): string {
  const padLen = (4 - (str.length % 4)) % 4;
  const padded = str + "=".repeat(padLen);
  const b64 = padded.replaceAll("-", "+").replaceAll("_", "/");
  return Buffer.from(b64, "base64").toString("utf8");
}

export function hmacSha256Hex(secret: string, payload: string): string {
  return crypto.createHmac("sha256", secret).update(payload, "utf8").digest("hex");
}

1.4 server/legal/termsEngine.ts — resolve addendum, assemble, hash, tokenize
// FILE: /server/legal/termsEngine.ts
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import {
  DOC1_GLOBAL,
  DEFAULT_ADDENDUM,
  COUNTRY_ADDENDA,
  REGION_ADDENDA,
  REGION_RULES_IN_ORDER,
  DocRef,
} from "./doc1Pack";
import { base64UrlEncode, base64UrlDecode, hmacSha256Hex, sha256Hex } from "./cryptoUtils";

type ResolveResult = { addendum: DocRef; regionKey?: string | null };

export type AssembledTerms = {
  global: { id: string; version: string; sha256: string };
  addendum: { id: string; version: string; sha256: string; regionKey?: string | null };
  combined: { sha256: string; text: string };
  meta: { countryIso2: string; regionKey?: string | null; issuedAtMs: number };
  token: string;
};

const CACHE = new Map<string, AssembledTerms>();

function normalizeIso2(iso2: string): string {
  const up = (iso2 || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!/^[A-Z]{2}$/.test(up)) throw new Error("Invalid country ISO2.");
  return up;
}

function resolveRegionKey(iso2: string): string | null {
  for (const rule of REGION_RULES_IN_ORDER) {
    if (rule.iso2.includes(iso2)) return rule.regionKey;
  }
  return null;
}

function resolveAddendum(iso2: string): ResolveResult {
  if (COUNTRY_ADDENDA[iso2]) return { addendum: COUNTRY_ADDENDA[iso2], regionKey: null };

  const regionKey = resolveRegionKey(iso2);
  if (regionKey && REGION_ADDENDA[regionKey]) return { addendum: REGION_ADDENDA[regionKey], regionKey };

  return { addendum: DEFAULT_ADDENDUM, regionKey: null };
}

function readTextFile(relPath: string): string {
  const abs = path.resolve(process.cwd(), relPath);
  return fs.readFileSync(abs, "utf8");
}

function requireSecret(): string {
  const secret = process.env.LEGAL_TERMS_HMAC_SECRET;
  if (!secret || secret.length < 32) {
    throw new Error("LEGAL_TERMS_HMAC_SECRET missing/too short (min 32 chars).");
  }
  return secret;
}

export function assembleDoc1Terms(countryIso2Raw: string): AssembledTerms {
  const countryIso2 = normalizeIso2(countryIso2Raw);
  const { addendum, regionKey } = resolveAddendum(countryIso2);

  const cacheKey = `${countryIso2}|${DOC1_GLOBAL.id}@${DOC1_GLOBAL.version}|${addendum.id}@${addendum.version}`;
  const cached = CACHE.get(cacheKey);
  if (cached) return cached;

  const globalText = readTextFile(DOC1_GLOBAL.path);
  const addendumText = readTextFile(addendum.path);

  const globalSha = sha256Hex(globalText);
  const addendumSha = sha256Hex(addendumText);

  const combinedText =
    globalText +
    "\n\n---\n\n" +
    "AUTO-SELECTED ADDENDUM:\n" +
    `Addendum ID: ${addendum.id}\n` +
    `Addendum Version: ${addendum.version}\n` +
    `Country Selected: ${countryIso2}\n` +
    (regionKey ? `Region Key: ${regionKey}\n` : "") +
    "\n" +
    addendumText;

  const combinedSha = sha256Hex(combinedText);

  const issuedAtMs = Date.now();
  const payloadObj = {
    v: 1,
    countryIso2,
    regionKey: regionKey ?? null,
    global: { id: DOC1_GLOBAL.id, version: DOC1_GLOBAL.version, sha256: globalSha },
    addendum: { id: addendum.id, version: addendum.version, sha256: addendumSha },
    combinedSha256: combinedSha,
    issuedAtMs,
  };

  const payloadJson = JSON.stringify(payloadObj);
  const payloadB64 = base64UrlEncode(payloadJson);
  const sig = hmacSha256Hex(requireSecret(), payloadB64);
  const token = `${payloadB64}.${sig}`;

  const assembled: AssembledTerms = {
    global: { id: DOC1_GLOBAL.id, version: DOC1_GLOBAL.version, sha256: globalSha },
    addendum: { id: addendum.id, version: addendum.version, sha256: addendumSha, regionKey: regionKey ?? null },
    combined: { sha256: combinedSha, text: combinedText },
    meta: { countryIso2, regionKey: regionKey ?? null, issuedAtMs },
    token,
  };

  CACHE.set(cacheKey, assembled);
  return assembled;
}

export type VerifiedToken = {
  ok: true;
  payload: {
    countryIso2: string;
    regionKey: string | null;
    global: { id: string; version: string; sha256: string };
    addendum: { id: string; version: string; sha256: string };
    combinedSha256: string;
    issuedAtMs: number;
  };
} | { ok: false; reason: string };

export function verifyTermsToken(token: string, maxAgeMs = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000): VerifiedToken {
  try {
    const parts = (token || "").split(".");
    if (parts.length !== 2) return { ok: false, reason: "Bad token format." };
    const [payloadB64, sig] = parts;
    const expected = hmacSha256Hex(requireSecret(), payloadB64);
    if (!cryptoSafeEqualHex(sig, expected)) return { ok: false, reason: "Bad token signature." };

    const payloadJson = base64UrlDecode(payloadB64);
    const payload = JSON.parse(payloadJson);

    if (!payload?.countryIso2 || !payload?.combinedSha256 || !payload?.issuedAtMs) {
      return { ok: false, reason: "Token payload missing fields." };
    }

    const age = Date.now() - Number(payload.issuedAtMs);
    if (age < 0 || age > maxAgeMs) return { ok: false, reason: "Token expired." };

    return { ok: true, payload };
  } catch (e: any) {
    return { ok: false, reason: `Token verify error: ${e?.message || "unknown"}` };
  }
}

function cryptoSafeEqualHex(a: string, b: string): boolean {
  try {
    const ba = Buffer.from(a, "hex");
    const bb = Buffer.from(b, "hex");
    if (ba.length !== bb.length) return false;
    // timingSafeEqual throws if lengths differ (handled above)
    // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-var-requires
    const crypto = require("crypto");
    return crypto.timingSafeEqual(ba, bb);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

1.5 server/routes/legal.ts — endpoint to fetch assembled terms (canonical)
// FILE: /server/routes/legal.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import { assembleDoc1Terms } from "../legal/termsEngine";

export const legalRouter = Router();

// GET /api/legal/doc1?country=KE
legalRouter.get("/doc1", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const country = String(req.query.country || "");
    const assembled = assembleDoc1Terms(country);

    // Return canonical rendered text + hashes + token
    return res.json({
      ok: true,
      global: assembled.global,
      addendum: assembled.addendum,
      combinedSha256: assembled.combined.sha256,
      issuedAtMs: assembled.meta.issuedAtMs,
      countryIso2: assembled.meta.countryIso2,
      regionKey: assembled.meta.regionKey,
      token: assembled.token,
      text: assembled.combined.text,
    });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Failed to assemble terms." });
  }
});

1.6 server/legal/legalAcceptanceService.ts — insert acceptance + hash-chained ledger
// FILE: /server/legal/legalAcceptanceService.ts
import { db } from "../db";
import { legalAcceptances } from "../../shared/schema";
import { desc } from "drizzle-orm";
import { sha256Hex, stableStringify } from "./cryptoUtils";

type InsertAcceptanceArgs = {
  userId: number;
  emailAtAcceptance: string | null;
  countryIso2: string;
  regionKey: string | null;

  global: { id: string; version: string; sha256: string };
  addendum: { id: string; version: string; sha256: string };
  combined: { sha256: string; text: string };

  termsToken: string;

  acceptedAtMs: number;
  ipAddress?: string | null;
  userAgent?: string | null;
  sessionId?: string | null;
};

export function insertLegalAcceptanceWithLedger(args: InsertAcceptanceArgs) {
  return db.transaction((tx) => {
    const last = tx
      .select({
        ledgerSeq: legalAcceptances.ledgerSeq,
        ledgerHash: legalAcceptances.ledgerHash,
      })
      .from(legalAcceptances)
      .orderBy(desc(legalAcceptances.ledgerSeq))
      .limit(1)
      .all();

    const prevSeq = last.length ? Number(last[0].ledgerSeq) : 0;
    const prevHash = last.length ? String(last[0].ledgerHash) : "GENESIS";
    const nextSeq = prevSeq + 1;

    const payloadForLedger = {
      ledgerSeq: nextSeq,
      prevLedgerHash: prevHash,

      userId: args.userId,
      emailAtAcceptance: args.emailAtAcceptance,
      countryIso2: args.countryIso2,
      regionKey: args.regionKey,

      global: args.global,
      addendum: args.addendum,
      combinedSha256: args.combined.sha256,

      acceptedAtMs: args.acceptedAtMs,
      ipAddress: args.ipAddress ?? null,
      userAgent: args.userAgent ?? null,
      sessionId: args.sessionId ?? null,
      termsToken: args.termsToken,
    };

    const ledgerPayloadStr = stableStringify(payloadForLedger);
    const ledgerHash = sha256Hex(`${prevHash}|${ledgerPayloadStr}`);

    tx.insert(legalAcceptances).values({
      userId: args.userId,
      emailAtAcceptance: args.emailAtAcceptance ?? null,

      countryIso2: args.countryIso2,
      regionKey: args.regionKey ?? null,

      globalDocId: args.global.id,
      globalDocVersion: args.global.version,
      globalDocSha256: args.global.sha256,

      addendumId: args.addendum.id,
      addendumVersion: args.addendum.version,
      addendumSha256: args.addendum.sha256,

      combinedSha256: args.combined.sha256,
      combinedText: args.combined.text,

      acceptedAt: new Date(args.acceptedAtMs),
      ipAddress: args.ipAddress ?? null,
      userAgent: args.userAgent ?? null,
      sessionId: args.sessionId ?? null,

      termsToken: args.termsToken,

      ledgerSeq: nextSeq,
      prevLedgerHash: prevHash,
      ledgerHash,
    }).run();

    return { ledgerSeq: nextSeq, prevLedgerHash: prevHash, ledgerHash };
  });
}

1.7 Signup route: verify token + hash, then create user, then log acceptance

Wherever your signup endpoint is, apply this pattern. If you have POST /api/auth/signup, use the below.

// FILE: /server/routes/auth.signup.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import { assembleDoc1Terms, verifyTermsToken } from "../legal/termsEngine";
import { insertLegalAcceptanceWithLedger } from "../legal/legalAcceptanceService";
import { db } from "../db";
import { users } from "../../shared/schema"; // adjust import if your users table name differs

export const authSignupRouter = Router();

/**
 * Expected payload:
 * {
 *   email, username, password, countryIso2, phoneE164,
 *   legal: { token, combinedSha256 }
 * }
 */
authSignupRouter.post("/signup", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const { email, username, password, countryIso2, phoneE164, legal } = req.body || {};
    if (!email || !username || !password) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "Missing required fields." });
    if (!countryIso2) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "Missing country." });
    if (!legal?.token || !legal?.combinedSha256) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "Missing legal acceptance." });

    // 1) Verify token signature + expiry
    const vt = verifyTermsToken(String(legal.token));
    if (!vt.ok) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: `Invalid terms token: ${vt.reason}` });

    // 2) Re-assemble canonical terms for the claimed country
    const assembled = assembleDoc1Terms(String(countryIso2));

    // 3) Ensure token payload matches assembled terms
    const tokenCountry = String((vt as any).payload.countryIso2);
    const tokenCombined = String((vt as any).payload.combinedSha256);

    if (tokenCountry !== assembled.meta.countryIso2) {
      return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "Terms token country mismatch." });
    }

    if (tokenCombined !== assembled.combined.sha256) {
      return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "Terms token does not match current terms." });
    }

    // 4) Ensure client-reported hash matches canonical hash
    if (String(legal.combinedSha256) !== assembled.combined.sha256) {
      return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "Client hash mismatch; reload terms and retry." });
    }

    // 5) Create user (simplified: replace with your actual password hashing and user creation logic)
    // IMPORTANT: hash password properly in your existing auth system.
    const created = db
      .insert(users)
      .values({
        email,
        username,
        passwordHash: password, // <-- replace: store a real hash
        countryIso: assembled.meta.countryIso2,
        phone: phoneE164 ?? null,
        createdAt: new Date(),
      } as any)
      .returning({ id: users.id })
      .get();

    const userId = Number(created?.id);
    if (!userId) return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: "User creation failed." });

    // 6) Audit: insert legal acceptance snapshot + ledger chain
    const ipAddress = (req.headers["x-forwarded-for"] as string)?.split(",")[0]?.trim() || req.socket.remoteAddress || null;
    const userAgent = String(req.headers["user-agent"] || "");
    const sessionId = String(req.headers["x-session-id"] || ""); // optional if you have one

    const ledger = insertLegalAcceptanceWithLedger({
      userId,
      emailAtAcceptance: email,
      countryIso2: assembled.meta.countryIso2,
      regionKey: assembled.meta.regionKey ?? null,
      global: assembled.global,
      addendum: { id: assembled.addendum.id, version: assembled.addendum.version, sha256: assembled.addendum.sha256 },
      combined: { sha256: assembled.combined.sha256, text: assembled.combined.text },
      termsToken: assembled.token,
      acceptedAtMs: Date.now(),
      ipAddress,
      userAgent,
      sessionId: sessionId || null,
    });

    return res.json({ ok: true, userId, legalLedger: ledger });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(500).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Signup failed." });
  }
});


Note: Replace passwordHash: password with your actual password hashing mechanism.

2) Frontend: Signup embed + dynamic terms modal + scroll gating
2.1 client/src/pages/SignupPage.tsx — country selection triggers legal fetch

This is a drop-in page (adjust route wiring to your wouter setup).

// FILE: /client/src/pages/SignupPage.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useRef, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useLocation } from "wouter";

type LegalDocResponse = {
  ok: boolean;
  global?: { id: string; version: string; sha256: string };
  addendum?: { id: string; version: string; sha256: string; regionKey?: string | null };
  combinedSha256?: string;
  issuedAtMs?: number;
  countryIso2?: string;
  regionKey?: string | null;
  token?: string;
  text?: string;
  error?: string;
};

export default function SignupPage() {
  const [, setLocation] = useLocation();

  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [username, setUsername] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [countryIso2, setCountryIso2] = useState("");
  const [phoneE164, setPhoneE164] = useState("");

  const [legal, setLegal] = useState<LegalDocResponse | null>(null);
  const [legalLoading, setLegalLoading] = useState(false);
  const [termsOpen, setTermsOpen] = useState(false);
  const [scrolledToEnd, setScrolledToEnd] = useState(false);
  const [accepted, setAccepted] = useState(false);

  // Reset acceptance if country changes
  useEffect(() => {
    setAccepted(false);
    setScrolledToEnd(false);
  }, [countryIso2]);

  // Fetch terms whenever country changes
  useEffect(() => {
    async function run() {
      if (!countryIso2) {
        setLegal(null);
        return;
      }
      setLegalLoading(true);
      try {
        const res = await fetch(`/api/legal/doc1?country=${encodeURIComponent(countryIso2)}`);
        const data: LegalDocResponse = await res.json();
        setLegal(data.ok ? data : data);
      } catch (e: any) {
        setLegal({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Failed to load terms." });
      } finally {
        setLegalLoading(false);
      }
    }
    run();
  }, [countryIso2]);

  const canSubmit = useMemo(() => {
    return (
      !!email &&
      !!username &&
      !!password &&
      !!countryIso2 &&
      !!phoneE164 &&
      !!legal?.ok &&
      !!legal?.token &&
      !!legal?.combinedSha256 &&
      accepted
    );
  }, [email, username, password, countryIso2, phoneE164, legal, accepted]);

  async function onSubmit(e: React.FormEvent) {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (!canSubmit || !legal?.ok) return;

    const payload = {
      email,
      username,
      password,
      countryIso2,
      phoneE164,
      legal: {
        token: legal.token,
        combinedSha256: legal.combinedSha256,
      },
    };

    const res = await fetch("/api/auth/signup", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(payload),
    });

    const data = await res.json();
    if (!data?.ok) {
      alert(data?.error || "Signup failed.");
      return;
    }
    setLocation("/"); // or login page
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ maxWidth: 560, margin: "24px auto", padding: 16 }}>
      <h1 style={{ fontSize: 22, fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 8 }}>Create Trader Account</h1>

      <form onSubmit={onSubmit} style={{ display: "grid", gap: 12 }}>
        <label>
          Email
          <input value={email} onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)} style={inputStyle} />
        </label>

        <label>
          Username
          <input value={username} onChange={(e) => setUsername(e.target.value)} style={inputStyle} />
        </label>

        <label>
          Password
          <input type="password" value={password} onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)} style={inputStyle} />
        </label>

        <label>
          Country of Residence (ISO2)
          <input
            value={countryIso2}
            onChange={(e) => setCountryIso2(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
            placeholder="e.g., KE, US, IN"
            style={inputStyle}
          />
          <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.7, marginTop: 4 }}>
            This selection controls both phone validation rules and the legal addendum loaded.
          </div>
        </label>

        <label>
          Phone (E.164)
          <input value={phoneE164} onChange={(e) => setPhoneE164(e.target.value)} placeholder="+2547..." style={inputStyle} />
        </label>

        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 12, alignItems: "center" }}>
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={() => setTermsOpen(true)}
            disabled={!countryIso2 || legalLoading || !legal?.ok}
            style={buttonStyle}
          >
            {legalLoading ? "Loading Terms..." : "View Terms & Conditions"}
          </button>

          <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.85 }}>
            {legal?.ok ? (
              <>
                Loaded: <strong>{legal.addendum?.id}</strong> (v{legal.addendum?.version})
              </>
            ) : (
              countryIso2 && <span style={{ color: "#b00020" }}>{legal?.error || "Terms not loaded."}</span>
            )}
          </div>
        </div>

        <label style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10, alignItems: "center" }}>
          <input
            type="checkbox"
            checked={accepted}
            onChange={(e) => setAccepted(e.target.checked)}
            disabled={!legal?.ok || !scrolledToEnd}
          />
          <span>
            I accept the Terms & Conditions
            {!scrolledToEnd && <span style={{ marginLeft: 6, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.7 }}>(scroll to the end in the Terms modal)</span>}
          </span>
        </label>

        <button type="submit" disabled={!canSubmit} style={{ ...buttonStyle, opacity: canSubmit ? 1 : 0.6 }}>
          Create Account
        </button>

        <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>
          Already have an account? <Link href="/login">Log in</Link>
        </div>
      </form>

      <TermsModal
        open={termsOpen}
        onClose={() => setTermsOpen(false)}
        title="TradeQuip Terms & Conditions"
        text={legal?.ok ? (legal.text || "") : ""}
        footerMeta={legal?.ok ? `Document hash: ${legal.combinedSha256}` : ""}
        onScrolledToEnd={() => setScrolledToEnd(true)}
      />
    </div>
  );
}

function TermsModal(props: {
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
  title: string;
  text: string;
  footerMeta?: string;
  onScrolledToEnd: () => void;
}) {
  const { open, onClose, title, text, footerMeta, onScrolledToEnd } = props;
  const scrollRef = useRef<HTMLDivElement | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!open) return;
    // reset scroll position on open
    const el = scrollRef.current;
    if (el) el.scrollTop = 0;
  }, [open, text]);

  function onScroll() {
    const el = scrollRef.current;
    if (!el) return;
    const atBottom = el.scrollTop + el.clientHeight >= el.scrollHeight - 8;
    if (atBottom) onScrolledToEnd();
  }

  if (!open) return null;

  return (
    <div style={modalOverlay} onMouseDown={onClose}>
      <div style={modalCard} onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 12, alignItems: "center" }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 700 }}>{title}</div>
          <button onClick={onClose} style={buttonStyle}>Close</button>
        </div>

        <div
          ref={scrollRef}
          onScroll={onScroll}
          style={{
            marginTop: 12,
            border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.15)",
            borderRadius: 8,
            height: 420,
            overflow: "auto",
            padding: 12,
            whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
            fontSize: 12,
            lineHeight: 1.4,
          }}
        >
          {text || "No terms loaded."}
        </div>

        <div style={{ marginTop: 10, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>
          {footerMeta || ""}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

const inputStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
  width: "100%",
  padding: "10px 12px",
  borderRadius: 8,
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.2)",
  marginTop: 6,
};

const buttonStyle: React.CSSProperties = {
  padding: "10px 12px",
  borderRadius: 8,
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.2)",
  background: "white",
  cursor: "pointer",
};

const modalOverlay: React.CSSProperties = {
  position: "fixed",
  inset: 0,
  background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.35)",
  display: "grid",
  placeItems: "center",
  padding: 16,
  zIndex: 1000,
};

const modalCard: React.CSSProperties = {
  width: "min(900px, 96vw)",
  background: "white",
  borderRadius: 12,
  padding: 14,
  boxShadow: "0 12px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.25)",
};

4) What I applied from your attached plan (and what I tightened)

From your design note, I retained:

Country selection as the driver for both phone and legal engines.

Assembling Global + Addendum in real time at country selection.

Persisting a hash and metadata at acceptance. 

Pasted-To-implement-this-dynami…

And I tightened:

Canonical assembly moved to backend + tokenization (prevents client spoofing).

Recompute-and-verify at signup (prevents “terms mismatch”).

Tamper-evident ledger (hash chain) for hedge-fund-grade auditability.

If you want the next increment immediately after this: I can add an Admin endpoint + UI mini-tab that lists legal acceptances per user, validates the ledger chain end-to-end, and exports a CSV/JSON with hashes and document versions for compliance reviews.

Thought for 1m 43s

Below is the next step: a System Config minitab (“Legal Docs”) that lets admins load, preview, replace, and roll back the Global Master and Country/Region Addenda—and a backend that:

Stores documents in DB (so you can replace without redeploying files)

Selects the correct active doc per country/region at signup

Preserves hashing + HMAC tokenization + acceptance ledger

Adds a separate tamper-evident audit chain for legal document changes (who changed what, when, from/to hashes)

Everything below is copy/paste file-by-file.

0) What changes conceptually

Docs move from filesystem-only to DB-first (with optional bootstrap import from your existing text files).

Signup terms are assembled from active pointers:

DOC1 / GLOBAL_MASTER / DEFAULT / GLOBAL

DOC1 / ADDENDUM / COUNTRY / {ISO2} else REGION / {REGION_KEY} else DEFAULT / ROW

Admin minitab can:

View active doc + all prior versions

Create a new version and activate it in one action

Roll back by switching the pointer

Preview what a country will receive (“assemble preview”)

1) Database schema additions
1.1 /shared/schema.ts — add legal doc tables + change-audit chain
// FILE: /shared/schema.ts
import { sqliteTable, text, integer, uniqueIndex } from "drizzle-orm/sqlite-core";

// -----------------------------
// Legal Documents (DB-first)
// -----------------------------
export const legalDocuments = sqliteTable(
  "legalDocuments",
  {
    id: integer("id").primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),

    // Document Set (DOC1, DOC2, etc.)
    docSet: text("doc_set").notNull(), // e.g. "DOC1"
    docType: text("doc_type").notNull(), // "GLOBAL_MASTER" | "ADDENDUM"
    jurisdictionType: text("jurisdiction_type").notNull(), // "DEFAULT" | "COUNTRY" | "REGION"
    jurisdictionKey: text("jurisdiction_key").notNull(), // e.g. "GLOBAL", "ROW", "US", "WEST_EUROPE"

    version: text("version").notNull(), // e.g. "1.0", "1.1"
    sha256: text("sha256").notNull(),

    // Exact bytes shown to users/admins
    content: text("content").notNull(),

    notes: text("notes"),
    createdAt: integer("created_at", { mode: "timestamp_ms" }).notNull(),
    createdByAdminUserId: integer("created_by_admin_user_id"),
  },
  (t) => ({
    // Prevent accidental duplicates of the exact same version for the same target
    uqTargetVersion: uniqueIndex("ux_legalDocuments_target_version").on(
      t.docSet,
      t.docType,
      t.jurisdictionType,
      t.jurisdictionKey,
      t.version
    ),
    uqTargetSha: uniqueIndex("ux_legalDocuments_target_sha").on(
      t.docSet,
      t.docType,
      t.jurisdictionType,
      t.jurisdictionKey,
      t.sha256
    ),
  })
);

// Active pointer per target (exactly one active doc per target)
export const legalDocPointers = sqliteTable(
  "legalDocPointers",
  {
    id: integer("id").primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),

    docSet: text("doc_set").notNull(),
    docType: text("doc_type").notNull(),
    jurisdictionType: text("jurisdiction_type").notNull(),
    jurisdictionKey: text("jurisdiction_key").notNull(),

    activeDocumentId: integer("active_document_id").notNull(),

    updatedAt: integer("updated_at", { mode: "timestamp_ms" }).notNull(),
    updatedByAdminUserId: integer("updated_by_admin_user_id"),
  },
  (t) => ({
    uqPointerTarget: uniqueIndex("ux_legalDocPointers_target").on(
      t.docSet,
      t.docType,
      t.jurisdictionType,
      t.jurisdictionKey
    ),
  })
);

// Tamper-evident audit chain for legal doc changes (separate from acceptance ledger)
export const legalDocChangeAudit = sqliteTable("legalDocChangeAudit", {
  id: integer("id").primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),

  seq: integer("seq").notNull(),
  prevHash: text("prev_hash").notNull(),
  eventHash: text("event_hash").notNull(),

  adminUserId: integer("admin_user_id"),
  action: text("action").notNull(), // "CREATE_VERSION" | "SET_ACTIVE" | "REPLACE_ACTIVE" | "ROLLBACK"

  docSet: text("doc_set").notNull(),
  docType: text("doc_type").type<string>().notNull(),
  jurisdictionType: text("jurisdiction_type").notNull(),
  jurisdictionKey: text("jurisdiction_key").notNull(),

  oldActiveDocumentId: integer("old_active_document_id"),
  newActiveDocumentId: integer("new_active_document_id"),

  note: text("note"),
  createdAt: integer("created_at", { mode: "timestamp_ms" }).notNull(),
});

2.1 /server/middleware/requireAdmin.ts — minimal placeholder gating
// FILE: /server/middleware/requireAdmin.ts
import type { Request, Response, NextFunction } from "express";

export function requireAdmin(req: Request, res: Response, next: NextFunction) {
  // Option A: header token (useful until you wire real auth roles)
  const token = String(req.headers["x-admin-token"] || "");
  const expected = process.env.ADMIN_API_TOKEN;

  if (expected && token && token === expected) return next();

  // Option B: role-based (if your app attaches req.user)
  const anyReq = req as any;
  const user = anyReq.user;
  if (user?.role === "ADMIN" || user?.isAdmin === true) return next();

  return res.status(403).json({ ok: false, error: "Admin access required." });
}

2.2 /server/legal/cryptoUtils.ts (reuse from prior step)
// FILE: /server/legal/cryptoUtils.ts
import crypto from "crypto";

export function sha256Hex(input: string): string {
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(input, "utf8").digest("hex");
}

export function stableStringify(value: unknown): string {
  if (value === null || typeof value !== "object") return JSON.stringify(value);
  if (Array.isArray(value)) return "[" + value.map((v) => stableStringify(v)).join(",") + "]";
  const obj = value as Record<string, unknown>;
  const keys = Object.keys(obj).sort();
  return "{" + keys.map((k) => JSON.stringify(k) + ":" + stableStringify(obj[k])).join(",") + "}";
}

export function base64UrlEncode(str: string): string {
  return Buffer.from(str, "utf8")
    .toString("base64")
    .replaceAll("+", "-")
    .replaceAll("/", "_")
    .replaceAll("=", "");
}

export function base64UrlDecode(str: string): string {
  const padLen = (4 - (str.length % 4)) % 4;
  const padded = str + "=".repeat(padLen);
  const b64 = padded.replaceAll("-", "+").replaceAll("_", "/");
  return Buffer.from(b64, "base64").toString("utf8");
}

export function hmacSha256Hex(secret: string, payload: string): string {
  return crypto.createHmac("sha256", secret).update(payload, "utf8").digest("hex");
}

export function timingSafeEqualHex(a: string, b: string): boolean {
  try {
    const ba = Buffer.from(a, "hex");
    const bb = Buffer.from(b, "hex");
    if (ba.length !== bb.length) return false;
    return crypto.timingSafeEqual(ba, bb);
  } catch {
    return false;
  }
}

2.4 /server/legal/legalDocsRepo.ts — DB helpers for docs + pointers
// FILE: /server/legal/legalDocsRepo.ts
import { and, desc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../db";
import { legalDocuments, legalDocPointers } from "../../shared/schema";
import { sha256Hex } from "./cryptoUtils";

export type DocTarget = {
  docSet: string; // "DOC1"
  docType: "GLOBAL_MASTER" | "ADDENDUM";
  jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT" | "COUNTRY" | "REGION";
  jurisdictionKey: string; // "GLOBAL" | "ROW" | "US" | "WEST_EUROPE"
};

export type LegalDocumentRow = {
  id: number;
  docSet: string;
  docType: string;
  jurisdictionType: string;
  jurisdictionKey: string;
  version: string;
  sha256: string;
  content: string;
  notes: string | null;
  createdAt: number;
  createdByAdminUserId: number | null;
};

export function computeDocSha(content: string): string {
  return sha256Hex(content);
}

export function getPointer(target: DocTarget) {
  return db
    .select()
    .from(legalDocPointers)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(legalDocPointers.docSet, target.docSet),
        eq(legalDocPointers.docType, target.docType),
        eq(legalDocPointers.jurisdictionType, target.jurisdictionType),
        eq(legalDocPointers.jurisdictionKey, target.jurisdictionKey)
      )
    )
    .get();
}

export function getActiveDoc(target: DocTarget) {
  const pointer = getPointer(target);
  if (!pointer) return { pointer: null, doc: null };

  const doc = db
    .select()
    .from(legalDocuments)
    .where(eq(legalDocuments.id, pointer.activeDocumentId))
    .get();

  return { pointer, doc };
}

export function listVersions(target: DocTarget) {
  return db
    .select()
    .from(legalDocuments)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(legalDocuments.docSet, target.docSet),
        eq(legalDocuments.docType, target.docType),
        eq(legalDocuments.jurisdictionType, target.jurisdictionType),
        eq(legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey, target.jurisdictionKey)
      )
    )
    .orderBy(desc(legalDocuments.createdAt))
    .all();
}

export function createDocumentVersion(args: {
  target: DocTarget;
  version: string;
  content: string;
  notes?: string | null;
  adminUserId?: number | null;
}) {
  const sha = computeDocSha(args.content);
  const now = Date.now();

  db.insert(legalDocuments)
    .values({
      docSet: args.target.docSet,
      docType: args.target.docType,
      jurisdictionType: args.target.jurisdictionType,
      jurisdictionKey: args.target.jurisdictionKey,
      version: args.version,
      sha256: sha,
      content: args.content,
      notes: args.notes ?? null,
      createdAt: new Date(now),
      createdByAdminUserId: args.adminUserId ?? null,
    })
    .run();

  // Fetch inserted by (target + sha)
  const row = db
    .select()
    .from(legalDocuments)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(legalDocuments.docSet, args.target.docSet),
        eq(legalDocuments.docType, args.target.docType),
        eq(legalDocuments.jurisdictionType, args.target.jurisdictionType),
        eq(legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey, args.target.jurisdictionKey),
        eq(legalDocuments.sha256, sha)
      )
    )
    .orderBy(desc(legalDocuments.createdAt))
    .limit(1)
    .get();

  if (!row) throw new Error("Failed to fetch inserted legal document.");
  return row;
}

export function upsertPointer(args: {
  target: DocTarget;
  activeDocumentId: number;
  adminUserId?: number | null;
}) {
  const now = Date.now();

  const existing = getPointer(args.target);
  if (!existing) {
    db.insert(legalDocPointers)
      .values({
        docSet: args.target.docSet,
        docType: args.target.docType,
        jurisdictionType: args.target.jurisdictionType,
        jurisdictionKey: args.target.jurisdictionKey,
        activeDocumentId: args.activeDocumentId,
        updatedAt: new Date(now),
        updatedByAdminUserId: args.adminUserId ?? null,
      })
      .run();
    return;
  }

  db.update(legalDocPointers)
    .set({
      activeDocumentId: args.activeDocumentId,
      updatedAt: new Date(now),
      updatedByAdminUserId: args.adminUserId ?? null,
    })
    .where(eq(legalDocPointers.id, existing.id))
    .run();
}

2.5 /server/legal/legalDocChangeAuditService.ts — tamper-evident audit chain for doc changes
// FILE: /server/legal/legalDocChangeAuditService.ts
import { desc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../db";
import { legalDocChangeAudit } from "../../shared/schema";
import { sha256Hex, stableStringify } from "./cryptoUtils";

export function appendLegalDocChangeAudit(args: {
  adminUserId?: number | null;
  action: "CREATE_VERSION" | "SET_ACTIVE" | "REPLACE_ACTIVE" | "ROLLBACK";
  docSet: string;
  docType: string;
  jurisdictionType: string;
  jurisdictionKey: string;
  oldActiveDocumentId?: number | null;
  newActiveDocumentId?: number | null;
  note?: string | null;
}) {
  return db.transaction((tx) => {
    const last = tx
      .select({ seq: legalDocChangeAudit.seq, eventHash: legalDocChangeAudit.eventHash })
      .from(legalDocChangeAudit)
      .orderBy(desc(legalDocChangeAudit.seq))
      .limit(1)
      .all();

    const prevSeq = last.length ? Number(last[0].seq) : 0;
    const prevHash = last.length ? String(last[0].eventHash) : "GENESIS";
    const nextSeq = prevSeq + 1;

    const payload = {
      seq: nextSeq,
      prevHash,

      adminUserId: args.adminUserId ?? null,
      action: args.action,

      docSet: args.docSet,
      docType: args.docType,
      jurisdictionType: args.jurisdictionType,
      jurisdictionKey: args.jurisdictionKey,

      oldActiveDocumentId: args.oldActiveDocumentId ?? null,
      newActiveDocumentId: args.newActiveDocumentId ?? null,

      note: args.note ?? null,
      createdAtMs: Date.now(),
    };

    const payloadStr = stableStringify(payload);
    const eventHash = sha256Hex(`${prevHash}|${payloadStr}`);

    tx.insert(legalDocChangeAudit)
      .values({
        seq: nextSeq,
        prevHash,
        eventHash,
        adminUserId: args.adminUserId ?? null,
        action: args.action,
        docSet: args.docSet,
        docType: args.docType,
        jurisdictionType: args.jurisdictionType,
        jurisdictionKey: args.jurisdictionKey,
        oldActiveDocumentId: args.oldActiveDocumentId ?? null,
        newActiveDocumentId: args.newActiveDocumentId ?? null,
        note: args.note ?? null,
        createdAt: new Date(payload.createdAtMs),
      })
      .run();

    return { seq: nextSeq, prevHash, eventHash };
  });
}

2.6 /server/legal/termsEngineDb.ts — assemble DOC1 using active pointers (DB-first)
// FILE: /server/legal/termsEngineDb.ts
import { REGION_RULES_IN_ORDER } from "./regionRules";
import { base64UrlEncode, base64UrlDecode, hmacSha256Hex, sha256Hex, timingSafeEqualHex } from "./cryptoUtils";
import { getActiveDoc } from "./legalDocsRepo";

export type AssembledTerms = {
  global: { id: string; version: string; sha256: string; documentId: number };
  addendum: { id: string; version: string; sha256: string; documentId: number; regionKey: string | null };
  combined: { sha256: string; text: string };
  meta: { docSet: "DOC1"; countryIso2: string; regionKey: string | null; issuedAtMs: number };
  token: string;
};

function normalizeIso2(iso2: string): string {
  const up = (iso2 || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!/^[A-Z]{2}$/.test(up)) throw new Error("Invalid country ISO2.");
  return up;
}

function resolveRegionKey(iso2: string): string | null {
  for (const rule of REGION_RULES_IN_ORDER) {
    if (rule.iso2.includes(iso2)) return rule.regionKey;
  }
  return null;
}

function requireSecret(): string {
  const secret = process.env.LEGAL_TERMS_HMAC_SECRET;
  if (!secret || secret.length < 32) throw new Error("LEGAL_TERMS_HMAC_SECRET missing/too short (min 32 chars).");
  return secret;
}

export function assembleDoc1Terms(countryIso2Raw: string): AssembledTerms {
  const countryIso2 = normalizeIso2(countryIso2Raw);
  const regionKey = resolveRegionKey(countryIso2);

  // GLOBAL MASTER (DEFAULT/GLOBAL)
  const globalTarget = {
    docSet: "DOC1",
    docType: "GLOBAL_MASTER" as const,
    jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT" as const,
    jurisdictionKey: "GLOBAL",
  };

  const globalActive = getActiveDoc(globalTarget);
  if (!globalActive.doc) throw new Error("Missing active DOC1 global master (DEFAULT/GLOBAL).");

  // ADDENDUM selection priority:
  // 1) COUNTRY/{ISO2}
  // 2) REGION/{regionKey}
  // 3) DEFAULT/ROW
  const addCountryTarget = {
    docSet: "DOC1",
    docType: "ADDENDUM" as const,
    jurisdictionType: "COUNTRY" as const,
    jurisdictionKey: countryIso2,
  };
  const addRegionTarget = regionKey
    ? {
        docSet: "DOC1",
        docType: "ADDENDUM" as const,
        jurisdictionType: "REGION" as const,
        jurisdictionKey: regionKey,
      }
    : null;

  const addDefaultTarget = {
    docSet: "DOC1",
    docType: "ADDENDUM" as const,
    jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT" as const,
    jurisdictionKey: "ROW",
  };

  const addCountry = getActiveDoc(addCountryTarget);
  const addRegion = addRegionTarget ? getActiveDoc(addRegionTarget) : { doc: null as any, pointer: null as any };
  const addDefault = getActiveDoc(addDefaultTarget);

  const addDoc = addCountry.doc ?? addRegion.doc ?? addDefault.doc;
  const addRegionUsed = addCountry.doc ? null : addRegion.doc ? regionKey : null;

  if (!addDoc) throw new Error("Missing active DOC1 addendum (COUNTRY/REGION/DEFAULT).");

  const globalText = String(globalActive.doc.content);
  const addendumText = String(addDoc.content);

  // Strong invariant: stored sha must match actual content
  const globalSha = sha256Hex(globalText);
  const addSha = sha256Hex(addendumText);
  if (globalSha !== String(globalActive.doc.sha256)) throw new Error("Global master sha mismatch (DB content vs sha).");
  if (addSha !== String(addDoc.sha256)) throw new Error("Addendum sha mismatch (DB content vs sha).");

  const combinedText =
    globalText +
    "\n\n---\n\n" +
    "AUTO-SELECTED ADDENDUM:\n" +
    `Addendum ID: ${addDoc.jurisdictionType === "COUNTRY" ? `ADD-${countryIso2}` : String(addDoc.jurisdictionKey)}\n` +
    `Addendum Version: ${String(addDoc.version)}\n` +
    `Country Selected: ${countryIso2}\n` +
    (addRegionUsed ? `Region Key: ${addRegionUsed}\n` : "") +
    "\n" +
    addendumText;

  const combinedSha = sha256Hex(combinedText);
  const issuedAtMs = Date.now();

  const payloadObj = {
    v: 1,
    docSet: "DOC1",
    countryIso2,
    regionKey: addRegionUsed,
    global: { id: "DOC1_GLOBAL", version: String(globalActive.doc.version), sha256: globalSha, documentId: Number(globalActive.doc.id) },
    addendum: { id: "DOC1_ADDENDUM", version: String(addDoc.version), sha256: addSha, documentId: Number(addDoc.id) },
    combinedSha256: combinedSha,
    issuedAtMs,
  };

  const payloadJson = JSON.stringify(payloadObj);
  const payloadB64 = base64UrlEncode(payloadJson);
  const sig = hmacSha256Hex(requireSecret(), payloadB64);
  const token = `${payloadB64}.${sig}`;

  return {
    global: { id: "DOC1_GLOBAL", version: String(globalActive.doc.version), sha256: globalSha, documentId: Number(globalActive.doc.id) },
    addendum: { id: "DOC1_ADDENDUM", version: String(addDoc.version), sha256: addSha, documentId: Number(addDoc.id), regionKey: addRegionUsed },
    combined: { sha256: combinedSha, text: combinedText },
    meta: { docSet: "DOC1", countryIso2, regionKey: addRegionUsed, issuedAtMs },
    token,
  };
}

export function verifyTermsToken(token: string, maxAgeMs = 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000) {
  try {
    const parts = String(token || "").split(".");
    if (parts.length !== 2) return { ok: false as const, reason: "Bad token format." };
    const [payloadB64, sig] = parts;

    const expected = hmacSha256Hex(requireSecret(), payloadB64);
    if (!timingSafeEqualHex(sig, expected)) return { ok: false as const, reason: "Bad token signature." };

    const payloadJson = base64UrlDecode(payloadB64);
    const payload = JSON.parse(payloadJson);

    const age = Date.now() - Number(payload?.issuedAtMs || 0);
    if (age < 0 || age > maxAgeMs) return { ok: false as const, reason: "Token expired." };

    return { ok: true as const, payload };
  } catch (e: any) {
    return { ok: false as const, reason: e?.message || "Token verify error." };
  }
}

2.7 /server/routes/legal.ts — public endpoint uses DB-first engine
// FILE: /server/routes/legal.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import { assembleDoc1Terms } from "../legal/termsEngineDb";

export const legalRouter = Router();

// GET /api/legal/doc1?country=KE
legalRouter.get("/doc1", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const country = String(req.query.country || "");
    const assembled = assembleDoc1Terms(country);

    return res.json({
      ok: true,
      countryIso2: assembled.meta.countryIso2,
      regionKey: assembled.meta.regionKey,
      issuedAtMs: assembled.meta.issuedAtMs,

      global: assembled.global,
      addendum: assembled.addendum,

      combinedSha256: assembled.combined.sha256,
      token: assembled.token,
      text: assembled.combined.text,
    });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Failed to assemble terms." });
  }
});

2.8 /server/routes/adminLegalDocs.ts — admin APIs to load/replace/preview
// FILE: /server/routes/adminLegalDocs.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import { requireAdmin } from "../middleware/requireAdmin";
import { and, eq, desc } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../db";
import { legalDocuments, legalDocPointers, legalDocChangeAudit } from "../../shared/schema";
import { createDocumentVersion, getActiveDoc, listVersions, upsertPointer } from "../legal/legalDocsRepo";
import { appendLegalDocChangeAudit } from "../legal/legalDocChangeAuditService";
import { assembleDoc1Terms } from "../legal/termsEngineDb";
import { REGION_RULES_IN_ORDER } from "../legal/regionRules";

export const adminLegalDocsRouter = Router();
adminLegalDocsRouter.use(requireAdmin);

// Targets for dropdowns
adminLegalDocsRouter.get("/targets", (_req, res) => {
  const regionKeys = Array.from(new Set(REGION_RULES_IN_ORDER.map((r) => r.regionKey)));
  return res.json({
    ok: true,
    docSets: ["DOC1"],
    docTypes: ["GLOBAL_MASTER", "ADDENDUM"],
    jurisdictionTypes: ["DEFAULT", "COUNTRY", "REGION"],
    defaultKeys: ["GLOBAL", "ROW"],
    regionKeys,
  });
});

// List pointer for a target
adminLegalDocsRouter.get("/pointer", (req, res) => {
  const docSet = String(req.query.docSet || "DOC1");
  const docType = String(req.query.docType || "");
  const jurisdictionType = String(req.query.jurisdictionType || "");
  const jurisdictionKey = String(req.query.jurisdictionKey || "");

  const pointer = db
    .select()
    .from(legalDocPointers)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(legalDocPointers.docSet, docSet),
        eq(legalDocPointers.docType, docType),
        eq(legalDocPointers.jurisdictionType, jurisdictionType),
        eq(legalDocPointers.jurisdictionKey, jurisdictionKey)
      )
    )
    .get();

  return res.json({ ok: true, pointer: pointer ?? null });
});

// List versions for a target + active doc
adminLegalDocsRouter.get("/versions", (req, res) => {
  const docSet = String(req.query.docSet || "DOC1");
  const docType = String(req.query.docType || "");
  const jurisdictionType = String(req.query.jurisdictionType || "");
  const jurisdictionKey = String(req.query.jurisdictionKey || "");

  const target = {
    docSet,
    docType: docType as any,
    jurisdictionType: jurisdictionType as any,
    jurisdictionKey,
  };

  const versions = listVersions(target);
  const active = getActiveDoc(target);

  return res.json({
    ok: true,
    activeDocumentId: active.doc?.id ?? null,
    versions,
  });
});

// Get document content by id
adminLegalDocsRouter.get("/document/:id", (req, res) => {
  const id = Number(req.params.id);
  const doc = db.select().from(legalDocuments).where(eq(legalDocuments.id, id)).get();
  if (!doc) return res.status(404).json({ ok: false, error: "Not found." });
  return res.json({ ok: true, doc });
});

// Replace active: creates new version and activates it atomically + logs audit chain
adminLegalDocsRouter.post("/replace-active", (req, res) => {
  const body = req.body || {};
  const target = body.target as {
    docSet: string;
    docType: "GLOBAL_MASTER" | "ADDENDUM";
    jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT" | "COUNTRY" | "REGION";
    jurisdictionKey: string;
  };

  const version = String(body.version || "").trim();
  const content = String(body.content || "");
  const note = String(body.note || "");
  const adminUserId = Number((req as any).user?.id || 0) || null;

  if (!target?.docSet || !target?.docType || !target?.jurisdictionType || !target?.jurisdictionKey) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "Missing target." });
  }
  if (!version) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "Missing version." });
  if (!content || content.length < 50) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "Content too short." });

  try {
    const result = db.transaction((tx) => {
      const oldPointer = tx
        .select()
        .from(legalDocPointers)
        .where(
          and(
            eq(legalDocPointers.docSet, target.docSet),
            eq(legalDocPointers.docType, target.docType),
            eq(legalDocPointers.jurisdictionType, target.jurisdictionType),
            eq(legalDocPointers.jurisdictionKey, target.jurisdictionKey)
          )
        )
        .get();

      const oldActiveId = oldPointer?.activeDocumentId ?? null;

      // Create new doc version
      const newDoc = createDocumentVersion({
        target,
        version,
        content,
        notes: note || null,
        adminUserId,
      });

      // Activate it
      upsertPointer({
        target,
        activeDocumentId: Number(newDoc.id),
        adminUserId,
      });

      // Audit chain
      const audit = appendLegalDocChangeAudit({
        adminUserId,
        action: "REPLACE_ACTIVE",
        docSet: target.docSet,
        docType: target.docType,
        jurisdictionType: target.jurisdictionType,
        jurisdictionKey: target.jurisdictionKey,
        oldActiveDocumentId: oldActiveId,
        newActiveDocumentId: Number(newDoc.id),
        note: note || null,
      });

      return { newDoc, audit, oldActiveId };
    });

    return res.json({ ok: true, ...result });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Replace failed." });
  }
});

// Set active to an existing version (rollback or switch)
adminLegalDocsRouter.post("/set-active", (req, res) => {
  const { target, documentId, note } = req.body || {};
  const adminUserId = Number((req as any).user?.id || 0) || null;

  if (!target || !documentId) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "Missing target/documentId." });

  const doc = db.select().from(legalDocuments).where(eq(legalDocuments.id, Number(documentId))).get();
  if (!doc) return res.status(404).json({ ok: false, error: "Document not found." });

  try {
    const result = db.transaction((tx) => {
      const oldPointer = tx
        .select()
        .from(legalDocPointers)
        .where(
          and(
            eq(legalDocPointers.docSet, target.docSet),
            eq(legalDocPointers.docType, target.docType),
            eq(legalDocPointers.jurisdictionType, target.jurisdictionType),
            eq(legalDocPointers.jurisdictionKey, target.jurisdictionKey)
          )
        )
        .get();

      const oldActiveId = oldPointer?.activeDocumentId ?? null;

      upsertPointer({ target, activeDocumentId: Number(documentId), adminUserId });

      const audit = appendLegalDocChangeAudit({
        adminUserId,
        action: oldActiveId ? "SET_ACTIVE" : "SET_ACTIVE",
        docSet: target.docSet,
        docType: target.docType,
        jurisdictionType: target.jurisdictionType,
        jurisdictionKey: target.jurisdictionKey,
        oldActiveDocumentId: oldActiveId,
        newActiveDocumentId: Number(documentId),
        note: String(note || "") || null,
      });

      return { oldActiveId, newActiveId: Number(documentId), audit };
    });

    return res.json({ ok: true, ...result });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Set active failed." });
  }
});

// Assemble preview for a country (admin QA)
adminLegalDocsRouter.post("/preview-assemble", (req, res) => {
  const { countryIso2 } = req.body || {};
  try {
    const assembled = assembleDoc1Terms(String(countryIso2 || ""));
    return res.json({
      ok: true,
      countryIso2: assembled.meta.countryIso2,
      regionKey: assembled.meta.regionKey,
      combinedSha256: assembled.combined.sha256,
      global: assembled.global,
      addendum: assembled.addendum,
      token: assembled.token,
      text: assembled.combined.text,
    });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Preview failed." });
  }
});

// (Optional) View change audit chain
adminLegalDocsRouter.get("/change-audit", (req, res) => {
  const limit = Math.min(200, Math.max(1, Number(req.query.limit || 50)));
  const rows = db.select().from(legalDocChangeAudit).orderBy(desc(legalDocChangeAudit.seq)).limit(limit).all();
  return res.json({ ok: true, rows });
});
