3) Bootstrap: import your existing text files into DB once (optional but recommended)

If you already have your word-for-word text pack in the repo, this seeds legalDocuments + pointers so the system runs immediately.

3.1 /server/legal/bootstrapDoc1FromFiles.ts
// FILE: /server/legal/bootstrapDoc1FromFiles.ts
import fs from "fs";
import path from "path";
import { db } from "../db";
import { legalDocuments, legalDocPointers } from "../../shared/schema";
import { and, eq, desc } from "drizzle-orm";
import { sha256Hex } from "./cryptoUtils";

function read(rel: string): string {
  return fs.readFileSync(path.resolve(process.cwd(), rel), "utf8");
}

async function ensureDocAndPointer(args: {
  target: { docSet: string; docType: string; jurisdictionType: string; jurisdictionKey: string };
  version: string;
  filePath: string;
}) {
  const content = read(args.filePath);
  const sha = sha256Hex(content);
  const now = Date.now();

  const existing = db
    .select()
    .from(legalDocuments)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(legalDocuments.docSet, args.target.docSet),
        eq(legalDocuments.docType, args.target.docType),
        eq(legalDocuments.jurisdictionType, args.target.jurisdictionType),
        eq(legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey, args.target.jurisdictionKey),
        eq(legalDocuments.sha256, sha)
      )
    )
    .get();

  let docId: number;
  if (existing) {
    docId = Number(existing.id);
  } else {
    db.insert(legalDocuments)
      .values({
        docSet: args.target.docSet,
        docType: args.target.docType,
        jurisdictionType: args.target.jurisdictionType,
        jurisdictionKey: args.target.jurisdictionKey,
        version: args.version,
        sha256: sha,
        content,
        notes: "BOOTSTRAP_IMPORT",
        createdAt: new Date(now),
        createdByAdminUserId: null,
      })
      .run();

    const inserted = db
      .select()
      .from(legalDocuments)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(legalDocuments.docSet, args.target.docSet),
          eq(legalDocuments.docType, args.target.docType),
          eq(legalDocuments.jurisdictionType, args.target.jurisdictionType),
          eq(legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey, args.target.jurisdictionKey),
          eq(legalDocuments.sha256, sha)
        )
      )
      .orderBy(desc(legalDocuments.createdAt))
      .limit(1)
      .get();

    if (!inserted) throw new Error("Bootstrap insert failed.");
    docId = Number(inserted.id);
  }

  const pointer = db
    .select()
    .from(legalDocPointers)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(legalDocPointers.docSet, args.target.docSet),
        eq(legalDocPointers.docType, args.target.docType),
        eq(legalDocPointers.jurisdictionType, args.target.jurisdictionType),
        eq(legalDocPointers.jurisdictionKey, args.target.jurisdictionKey)
      )
    )
    .get();

  if (!pointer) {
    db.insert(legalDocPointers)
      .values({
        docSet: args.target.docSet,
        docType: args.target.docType,
        jurisdictionType: args.target.jurisdictionType,
        jurisdictionKey: args.target.jurisdictionKey,
        activeDocumentId: docId,
        updatedAt: new Date(now),
        updatedByAdminUserId: null,
      })
      .run();
  }
}

export async function bootstrapDoc1FromFilesIfEmpty() {
  const any = db.select().from(legalDocuments).limit(1).get();
  if (any) return;

  // Adjust these file paths to match where you store the text pack in repo:
  await ensureDocAndPointer({
    target: { docSet: "DOC1", docType: "GLOBAL_MASTER", jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT", jurisdictionKey: "GLOBAL" },
    version: "1.0",
    filePath: "server/legal/doc1/DOC1_GLOBAL_v1.0.txt",
  });

  // Default addendum ROW
  await ensureDocAndPointer({
    target: { docSet: "DOC1", docType: "ADDENDUM", jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT", jurisdictionKey: "ROW" },
    version: "1.0",
    filePath: "server/legal/doc1/addenda/ADD-ROW_v1.0.txt",
  });

  // Countries (repeat as needed)
  const countries = ["US","AE","IN","KE","NG","BD","TH","PH","SG","HK","CN","BR","AR","CO","MX","ET","GH","AO","ZA","UG","TZ","RU","UA","GB"];
  for (const iso2 of countries) {
    const fp = `server/legal/doc1/addenda/ADD-${iso2}_v1.0.txt`;
    if (fs.existsSync(path.resolve(process.cwd(), fp))) {
      await ensureDocAndPointer({
        target: { docSet: "DOC1", docType: "ADDENDUM", jurisdictionType: "COUNTRY", jurisdictionKey: iso2 },
        version: "1.0",
        filePath: fp,
      });
    }
  }

  // Regions (repeat as needed)
  const regions = ["WEST_EUROPE","EAST_EUROPE","SE_ASIA","MIDDLE_EAST","NORTH_AFRICA","WEST_AFRICA","EAST_AFRICA","SOUTHERN_AFRICA","CENTRAL_AFRICA","SOUTH_AMERICA","CENTRAL_AMERICA","NORTH_AMERICA","CENTRAL_ASIA","JAPAN","SOUTH_KOREA","PAKISTAN","AUSTRALASIA","PACIFIC_ISLANDS","INDIAN_OCEAN_ISLANDS"];
  for (const rk of regions) {
    const fp = `server/legal/doc1/addenda/ADD-${rk}_v1.0.txt`;
    if (fs.existsSync(path.resolve(process.cwd(), fp))) {
      await ensureDocAndPointer({
        target: { docSet: "DOC1", docType: "ADDENDUM", jurisdictionType: "REGION", jurisdictionKey: rk },
        version: "1.0",
        filePath: fp,
      });
    }
  }
}

3.2 Call bootstrap once at server startup

Add near app initialization:

// FILE: /server/index.ts
import { bootstrapDoc1FromFilesIfEmpty } from "./legal/bootstrapDoc1FromFiles";

// call before routes (best effort)
bootstrapDoc1FromFilesIfEmpty().catch((e) => {
  // log only; do not crash prod
  console.error("DOC1 bootstrap failed:", e?.message || e);
});

4) Frontend: System Config minitab “Legal Docs” (Admin UI)
4.1 /client/src/pages/AdminLegalDocs.tsx
// FILE: /client/src/pages/AdminLegalDocs.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

type Target = {
  docSet: string;
  docType: "GLOBAL_MASTER" | "ADDENDUM";
  jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT" | "COUNTRY" | "REGION";
  jurisdictionKey: string;
};

type VersionRow = {
  id: number;
  docSet: string;
  docType: string;
  jurisdictionType: string;
  jurisdictionKey: string;
  version: string;
  sha256: string;
  notes?: string | null;
  createdAt: number;
  createdByAdminUserId?: number | null;
};

function bumpPatch(v: string): string {
  const m = String(v || "").match(/^(\d+)\.(\d+)$/);
  if (!m) return "1.0";
  const major = Number(m[1]);
  const minor = Number(m[2]);
  return `${major}.${minor + 1}`;
}

export default function AdminLegalDocs() {
  const [targetsMeta, setTargetsMeta] = useState<any>(null);

  const [docSet, setDocSet] = useState("DOC1");
  const [docType, setDocType] = useState<Target["docType"]>("GLOBAL_MASTER");
  const [jurisdictionType, setJurisdictionType] = useState<Target["jurisdictionType"]>("DEFAULT");
  const [jurisdictionKey, setJurisdictionKey] = useState("GLOBAL");

  const [versions, setVersions] = useState<VersionRow[]>([]);
  const [activeDocumentId, setActiveDocumentId] = useState<number | null>(null);

  const [selectedDocId, setSelectedDocId] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [selectedDocContent, setSelectedDocContent] = useState<string>("");

  const [editorVersion, setEditorVersion] = useState("1.0");
  const [editorNote, setEditorNote] = useState("");
  const [editorContent, setEditorContent] = useState("");

  const [previewCountry, setPreviewCountry] = useState("KE");
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState<any>(null);

  const target: Target = useMemo(
    () => ({ docSet, docType, jurisdictionType, jurisdictionKey }),
    [docSet, docType, jurisdictionType, jurisdictionKey]
  );

  async function loadTargets() {
    const res = await fetch("/api/admin/legal-docs/targets");
    const data = await res.json();
    if (data?.ok) setTargetsMeta(data);
  }

  async function loadVersions() {
    const qs = new URLSearchParams({
      docSet,
      docType,
      jurisdictionType,
      jurisdictionKey,
    });
    const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/legal-docs/versions?${qs.toString()}`);
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!data?.ok) {
      alert(data?.error || "Failed to load versions.");
      return;
    }
    setVersions(data.versions || []);
    setActiveDocumentId(data.activeDocumentId ?? null);

    // set editor version default based on active
    const active = (data.versions || []).find((v: VersionRow) => v.id === data.activeDocumentId);
    if (active?.version) setEditorVersion(bumpPatch(active.version));
    else setEditorVersion("1.0");

    // convenience: auto-select active doc
    if (data.activeDocumentId) {
      setSelectedDocId(data.activeDocumentId);
      await loadDocContent(data.activeDocumentId);
    } else {
      setSelectedDocId(null);
      setSelectedDocContent("");
      setEditorContent("");
    }
  }

  async function loadDocContent(id: number) {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/legal-docs/document/${id}`);
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!data?.ok) {
      alert(data?.error || "Failed to load document.");
      return;
    }
    setSelectedDocContent(String(data.doc?.content || ""));
  }

  async function onSelectDoc(id: number) {
    setSelectedDocId(id);
    await loadDocContent(id);
  }

  async function onLoadActiveIntoEditor() {
    if (!selectedDocContent) return;
    setEditorContent(selectedDocContent);
  }

  async function onReplaceActive() {
    if (!editorContent || editorContent.length < 50) {
      alert("Editor content too short.");
      return;
    }
    const ok = window.confirm(
      `Replace ACTIVE document for:\n${docSet}/${docType}/${jurisdictionType}/${jurisdictionKey}\n\nThis will activate version ${editorVersion}. Proceed?`
    );
    if (!ok) return;

    const res = await fetch("/api/admin/legal-docs/replace-active", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        target,
        version: editorVersion,
        content: editorContent,
        note: editorNote,
      }),
    });

    const data = await res.json();
    if (!data?.ok) {
      alert(data?.error || "Replace active failed.");
      return;
    }

    setEditorNote("");
    await loadVersions();
    alert(`Activated new doc id ${data.newDoc?.id} (sha ${data.newDoc?.sha256?.slice(0, 12)}...)`);
  }

  async function onSetActive(docId: number) {
    const ok = window.confirm(`Set doc id ${docId} as ACTIVE for this target?`);
    if (!ok) return;

    const res = await fetch("/api/admin/legal-docs/set-active", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        target,
        documentId: docId,
        note: `Set active via admin UI`,
      }),
    });

    const data = await res.json();
    if (!data?.ok) {
      alert(data?.error || "Set active failed.");
      return;
    }

    await loadVersions();
  }

  async function onPreviewAssemble() {
    const res = await fetch("/api/admin/legal-docs/preview-assemble", {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ countryIso2: previewCountry }),
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!data?.ok) {
      alert(data?.error || "Preview failed.");
      return;
    }
    setPreview(data);
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    loadTargets();
  }, []);

  useEffect(() => {
    loadVersions();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [docSet, docType, jurisdictionType, jurisdictionKey]);

  function suggestKeys() {
    if (!targetsMeta?.ok) return [];
    if (jurisdictionType === "DEFAULT") return targetsMeta.defaultKeys || ["GLOBAL", "ROW"];
    if (jurisdictionType === "REGION") return targetsMeta.regionKeys || [];
    return []; // COUNTRY is free-form ISO2
  }

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 12 }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "baseline" }}>
        <div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 700 }}>Legal Docs</div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>
            DB-first document management. Replace active docs per country/region without redeploy.
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>
          Tip: Use DEFAULT/GLOBAL for the master, DEFAULT/ROW for fallback addendum.
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Target selector */}
      <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 10, gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(4, minmax(180px, 1fr))" }}>
        <Field label="Doc Set">
          <select value={docSet} onChange={(e) => setDocSet(e.target.value)} style={input}>
            <option value="DOC1">DOC1</option>
          </select>
        </Field>

        <Field label="Doc Type">
          <select value={docType} onChange={(e) => setDocType(e.target.value as any)} style={input}>
            <option value="GLOBAL_MASTER">GLOBAL_MASTER</option>
            <option value="ADDENDUM">ADDENDUM</option>
          </select>
        </Field>

        <Field label="Jurisdiction Type">
          <select value={jurisdictionType} onChange={(e) => setJurisdictionType(e.target.value as any)} style={input}>
            <option value="DEFAULT">DEFAULT</option>
            <option value="COUNTRY">COUNTRY</option>
            <option value="REGION">REGION</option>
          </select>
        </Field>

        <Field label="Jurisdiction Key">
          {jurisdictionType === "COUNTRY" ? (
            <input
              value={jurisdictionKey}
              onChange={(e) => setJurisdictionKey(e.target.value.toUpperCase())}
              placeholder="ISO2 (e.g., KE, US)"
              style={input}
            />
          ) : (
            <select value={jurisdictionKey} onChange={(e) => setJurisdictionKey(e.target.value)} style={input}>
              {suggestKeys().map((k: string) => (
                <option key={k} value={k}>
                  {k}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          )}
        </Field>
      </div>

      <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 12, gridTemplateColumns: "1.2fr 1fr" }}>
        {/* Versions list */}
        <div style={card}>
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 8 }}>Versions</div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75, marginBottom: 10 }}>
            Active document id: <strong>{activeDocumentId ?? "None"}</strong>
          </div>

          <div style={{ border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)", borderRadius: 10, overflow: "hidden" }}>
            <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "90px 90px 1fr 140px 120px", padding: 10, fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.04)" }}>
              <div>ID</div>
              <div>Ver</div>
              <div>SHA256</div>
              <div>Created</div>
              <div>Actions</div>
            </div>

            <div style={{ maxHeight: 300, overflow: "auto" }}>
              {versions.map((v) => {
                const isActive = v.id === activeDocumentId;
                return (
                  <div
                    key={v.id}
                    style={{
                      display: "grid",
                      gridTemplateColumns: "90px 90px 1fr 140px 120px",
                      padding: 10,
                      fontSize: 12,
                      borderTop: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.08)",
                      background: isActive ? "rgba(0, 120, 0, 0.06)" : "transparent",
                    }}
                  >
                    <div>
                      <button style={linkBtn} onClick={() => onSelectDoc(v.id)}>
                        {v.id}
                      </button>
                      {isActive && <span style={{ marginLeft: 6, fontWeight: 700 }}>(active)</span>}
                    </div>
                    <div>{v.version}</div>
                    <div style={{ fontFamily: "monospace" }}>{v.sha256}</div>
                    <div>{new Date(v.createdAt).toLocaleString()}</div>
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
                      <button style={smallBtn} onClick={() => onSelectDoc(v.id)}>View</button>
                      {!isActive && <button style={smallBtn} onClick={() => onSetActive(v.id)}>Set Active</button>}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
              {versions.length === 0 && <div style={{ padding: 10, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.7 }}>No versions found for this target.</div>}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ marginTop: 10, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>
            Selected doc id: <strong>{selectedDocId ?? "None"}</strong>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Viewer + Editor */}
        <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 12 }}>
          <div style={card}>
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 8 }}>Selected Document (read-only)</div>
            <textarea value={selectedDocContent} readOnly style={{ ...textarea, height: 180 }} />
            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, marginTop: 8 }}>
              <button style={smallBtn} onClick={onLoadActiveIntoEditor} disabled={!selectedDocContent}>
                Load Into Editor
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={card}>
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 8 }}>Replace Active (create new version + activate)</div>

            <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 10 }}>
              <Field label="New Version">
                <input value={editorVersion} onChange={(e) => setEditorVersion(e.target.value)} style={input} />
              </Field>
              <Field label="Note (optional)">
                <input value={editorNote} onChange={(e) => setEditorNote(e.target.value)} style={input} />
              </Field>
            </div>

            <Field label="New Content">
              <textarea value={editorContent} onChange={(e) => setEditorContent(e.target.value)} style={{ ...textarea, height: 220 }} />
            </Field>

            <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10 }}>
              <button style={primaryBtn} onClick={onReplaceActive}>
                Replace Active
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Assemble preview */}
      <div style={card}>
        <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 8 }}>Assemble Preview (QA what signup will load)</div>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10, alignItems: "center" }}>
          <Field label="Country ISO2">
            <input value={previewCountry} onChange={(e) => setPreviewCountry(e.target.value.toUpperCase())} style={input} />
          </Field>
          <button style={primaryBtn} onClick={onPreviewAssemble}>Preview Assemble</button>
        </div>

        {preview?.ok && (
          <div style={{ marginTop: 10, display: "grid", gap: 6, fontSize: 12 }}>
            <div>Combined SHA256: <span style={{ fontFamily: "monospace" }}>{preview.combinedSha256}</span></div>
            <div>Global Doc ID: {preview.global?.documentId} (v{preview.global?.version})</div>
            <div>Addendum Doc ID: {preview.addendum?.documentId} (v{preview.addendum?.version})</div>
            <div>Region Used: {preview.regionKey ?? "none"}</div>
            <details style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
              <summary style={{ cursor: "pointer" }}>Show Rendered Text</summary>
              <pre style={{ whiteSpace: "pre-wrap", marginTop: 8, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)", padding: 10, borderRadius: 10 }}>
                {preview.text}
              </pre>
            </details>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Field(props: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <label style={{ display: "grid", gap: 6, fontSize: 12 }}>
      <div style={{ opacity: 0.8 }}>{props.label}</div>
      {props.children}
    </label>
  );
}

const card: React.CSSProperties = {
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)",
  borderRadius: 12,
  padding: 12,
  background: "white",
};

const input: React.CSSProperties = {
  width: "100%",
  padding: "10px 12px",
  borderRadius: 10,
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.2)",
};

const textarea: React.CSSProperties = {
  width: "100%",
  padding: "10px 12px",
  borderRadius: 10,
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.2)",
  fontFamily: "monospace",
  fontSize: 12,
  lineHeight: 1.35,
};

const smallBtn: React.CSSProperties = {
  padding: "8px 10px",
  borderRadius: 10,
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.2)",
  background: "white",
  cursor: "pointer",
  fontSize: 12,
};

const primaryBtn: React.CSSProperties = {
  padding: "10px 12px",
  borderRadius: 10,
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.25)",
  background: "white",
  cursor: "pointer",
  fontWeight: 700,
};

const linkBtn: React.CSSProperties = {
  padding: 0,
  border: "none",
  background: "transparent",
  cursor: "pointer",
  textDecoration: "underline",
  fontSize: 12,
};

Result

With this, the Admin “Legal Docs” minitab can:

Load any country/region target

See active doc and all versions

Replace active doc (create new version + activate)

Roll back (set active to an older version)

Preview the assembled signup terms for any ISO2

Maintain a hash-chained legal-doc change audit trail

When you are ready, the next build-out is the Admin UI mini-tab “Legal Acceptances” to:

Query acceptances by user / country / date

Validate the acceptance ledger chain

Export acceptance snapshots for compliance (CSV/JSON)

build next the Admin UI mini-tab “Legal Acceptances"
Thought for 34s

Below is a complete, copy/paste Admin UI mini-tab: “Legal Acceptances” (backend + frontend) that:

Lists acceptance records with filters (country, userId, email contains, date range)

Opens a full detail viewer (includes the exact rendered combinedText)

Validates the acceptance ledger hash-chain end-to-end (optionally last N or by seq range)

Validates combinedSha256 == SHA256(combinedText) for each row

Exports CSV (filtered)

This assumes you already have:

legalAcceptances table (with ledgerSeq, prevLedgerHash, ledgerHash, combinedText, etc.)

/server/legal/cryptoUtils.ts with sha256Hex + stableStringify

/server/middleware/requireAdmin.ts

1) Backend: Admin Legal Acceptances Routes
1.1 server/routes/adminLegalAcceptances.ts
// FILE: /server/routes/adminLegalAcceptances.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import { and, asc, desc, eq, like, gte, lte, sql } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../db";
import { requireAdmin } from "../middleware/requireAdmin";
import { legalAcceptances } from "../../shared/schema";
import { sha256Hex, stableStringify } from "../legal/cryptoUtils";

export const adminLegalAcceptancesRouter = Router();
adminLegalAcceptancesRouter.use(requireAdmin);

function toMs(value: any): number | null {
  if (value == null || value === "") return null;
  const n = Number(value);
  if (!Number.isFinite(n)) return null;
  return n;
}

function normalizeIso2(v: any): string | null {
  const s = String(v || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!s) return null;
  if (!/^[A-Z]{2}$/.test(s)) return null;
  return s;
}

function buildWhere(params: {
  countryIso2?: any;
  userId?: any;
  email?: any;
  fromMs?: any;
  toMs?: any;
}) {
  const clauses: any[] = [];

  const iso2 = normalizeIso2(params.countryIso2);
  if (iso2) clauses.push(eq(legalAcceptances.countryIso2, iso2));

  const uid = params.userId != null && params.userId !== "" ? Number(params.userId) : null;
  if (uid && Number.isFinite(uid)) clauses.push(eq(legalAcceptances.userId, uid));

  const email = String(params.email || "").trim();
  if (email) clauses.push(like(legalAcceptances.emailAtAcceptance, `%${email}%`));

  const fromMs = toMs(params.fromMs);
  const toMsVal = toMs(params.toMs);

  if (fromMs != null) clauses.push(gte(legalAcceptances.acceptedAt, new Date(fromMs)));
  if (toMsVal != null) clauses.push(lte(legalAcceptances.acceptedAt, new Date(toMsVal)));

  if (clauses.length === 0) return undefined;
  return and(...clauses);
}

// GET /api/admin/legal-acceptances/list?limit=50&offset=0&countryIso2=KE&email=gmail&userId=12&fromMs=...&toMs=...
adminLegalAcceptancesRouter.get("/list", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const limit = Math.min(200, Math.max(1, Number(req.query.limit || 50)));
    const offset = Math.max(0, Number(req.query.offset || 0));

    const where = buildWhere({
      countryIso2: req.query.countryIso2,
      userId: req.query.userId,
      email: req.query.email,
      fromMs: req.query.fromMs,
      toMs: req.query.toMs,
    });

    const totalRow = db
      .select({ count: sql<number>`count(*)` })
      .from(legalAcceptances)
      .where(where as any)
      .get();

    const rows = db
      .select({
        id: legalAcceptances.id,
        userId: legalAcceptances.userId,
        emailAtAcceptance: legalAcceptances.emailAtAcceptance,
        countryIso2: legalAcceptances.countryIso2,
        regionKey: legalAcceptances.regionKey,

        globalDocId: legalAcceptances.globalDocId,
        globalDocVersion: legalAcceptances.globalDocVersion,
        globalDocSha256: legalAcceptances.globalDocSha256,

        addendumId: legalAcceptances.addendumId,
        addendumVersion: legalAcceptances.addendumVersion,
        addendumSha256: legalAcceptances.addendumSha256,

        combinedSha256: legalAcceptances.combinedSha256,
        acceptedAt: legalAcceptances.acceptedAt,

        ipAddress: legalAcceptances.ipAddress,
        userAgent: legalAcceptances.userAgent,

        ledgerSeq: legalAcceptances.ledgerSeq,
        prevLedgerHash: legalAcceptances.prevLedgerHash,
        ledgerHash: legalAcceptances.ledgerHash,
      })
      .from(legalAcceptances)
      .where(where as any)
      .orderBy(desc(legalAcceptances.acceptedAt))
      .limit(limit)
      .offset(offset)
      .all();

    return res.json({
      ok: true,
      total: Number(totalRow?.count || 0),
      limit,
      offset,
      rows,
    });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Failed to list acceptances." });
  }
});

// GET /api/admin/legal-acceptances/:id (includes combinedText)
adminLegalAcceptancesRouter.get("/:id", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const id = Number(req.params.id);
    if (!id) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "Bad id." });

    const row = db.select().from(legalAcceptances).where(eq(legalAcceptances.id, id)).get();
    if (!row) return res.status(404).json({ ok: false, error: "Not found." });

    return res.json({ ok: true, row });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Failed to fetch acceptance." });
  }
});

// POST /api/admin/legal-acceptances/validate-chain
// body: { mode: "lastN", n?: number } OR { mode: "range", fromSeq: number, toSeq: number }
adminLegalAcceptancesRouter.post("/validate-chain", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const body = req.body || {};
    const mode = String(body.mode || "lastN");

    let rows: any[] = [];

    if (mode === "range") {
      const fromSeq = Number(body.fromSeq);
      const toSeq = Number(body.toSeq);
      if (!Number.isFinite(fromSeq) || !Number.isFinite(toSeq) || fromSeq <= 0 || toSeq < fromSeq) {
        return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "Invalid seq range." });
      }

      rows = db
        .select()
        .from(legalAcceptances)
        .where(and(gte(legalAcceptances.ledgerSeq, fromSeq), lte(legalAcceptances.ledgerSeq, toSeq)) as any)
        .orderBy(asc(legalAcceptances.ledgerSeq))
        .all();
    } else {
      const n = Math.min(5000, Math.max(1, Number(body.n || 500)));
      rows = db
        .select()
        .from(legalAcceptances)
        .orderBy(desc(legalAcceptances.ledgerSeq))
        .limit(n)
        .all()
        .reverse(); // ascending
    }

    if (rows.length === 0) return res.json({ ok: true, summary: { rows: 0, ok: true }, issues: [] });

    const issues: any[] = [];
    let prevHash = "GENESIS";
    let prevSeq = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < rows.length; i++) {
      const r = rows[i];

      const ledgerSeq = Number(r.ledgerSeq);
      const prevLedgerHash = String(r.prevLedgerHash);
      const ledgerHash = String(r.ledgerHash);

      // acceptedAt may be Date or number depending on drizzle config
      const acceptedAtMs =
        r.acceptedAt instanceof Date ? r.acceptedAt.getTime() : Number(r.acceptedAt);

      // 1) Sequence continuity (within the requested set)
      if (prevSeq > 0 && ledgerSeq !== prevSeq + 1) {
        issues.push({
          type: "SEQ_GAP",
          id: r.id,
          ledgerSeq,
          expected: prevSeq + 1,
          found: ledgerSeq,
        });
      }

      // 2) prev hash link correctness
      if (prevLedgerHash !== prevHash) {
        issues.push({
          type: "PREV_HASH_MISMATCH",
          id: r.id,
          ledgerSeq,
          expectedPrevHash: prevHash,
          foundPrevHash: prevLedgerHash,
        });
      }

      // 3) Recompute ledger hash
      const payloadForLedger = {
        ledgerSeq,
        prevLedgerHash,

        userId: r.userId ?? null,
        emailAtAcceptance: r.emailAtAcceptance ?? null,
        countryIso2: r.countryIso2,
        regionKey: r.regionKey ?? null,

        global: { id: r.globalDocId, version: r.globalDocVersion, sha256: r.globalDocSha256 },
        addendum: { id: r.addendumId, version: r.addendumVersion, sha256: r.addendumSha256 },

        combinedSha256: r.combinedSha256,

        acceptedAtMs,
        ipAddress: r.ipAddress ?? null,
        userAgent: r.userAgent ?? null,
        sessionId: r.sessionId ?? null,

        termsToken: r.termsToken,
      };

      const ledgerPayloadStr = stableStringify(payloadForLedger);
      const expectedLedgerHash = sha256Hex(`${prevLedgerHash}|${ledgerPayloadStr}`);

      if (expectedLedgerHash !== ledgerHash) {
        issues.push({
          type: "LEDGER_HASH_MISMATCH",
          id: r.id,
          ledgerSeq,
          expected: expectedLedgerHash,
          found: ledgerHash,
        });
      }

      // 4) Verify combinedSha256 matches the stored combinedText bytes
      const combinedText = String(r.combinedText || "");
      const expectedCombinedSha = sha256Hex(combinedText);
      if (expectedCombinedSha !== String(r.combinedSha256)) {
        issues.push({
          type: "COMBINED_TEXT_SHA_MISMATCH",
          id: r.id,
          ledgerSeq,
          expected: expectedCombinedSha,
          found: String(r.combinedSha256),
        });
      }

      prevHash = ledgerHash;
      prevSeq = ledgerSeq;
    }

    const summary = {
      rows: rows.length,
      ok: issues.length === 0,
      firstSeq: Number(rows[0].ledgerSeq),
      lastSeq: Number(rows[rows.length - 1].ledgerSeq),
    };

    return res.json({ ok: true, summary, issues });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Validation failed." });
  }
});

// GET /api/admin/legal-acceptances/export.csv?countryIso2=KE&fromMs=...&toMs=...&email=...
adminLegalAcceptancesRouter.get("/export.csv", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const where = buildWhere({
      countryIso2: req.query.countryIso2,
      userId: req.query.userId,
      email: req.query.email,
      fromMs: req.query.fromMs,
      toMs: req.query.toMs,
    });

    const limit = Math.min(5000, Math.max(1, Number(req.query.limit || 2000)));

    const rows = db
      .select({
        id: legalAcceptances.id,
        userId: legalAcceptances.userId,
        emailAtAcceptance: legalAcceptances.emailAtAcceptance,
        countryIso2: legalAcceptances.countryIso2,
        regionKey: legalAcceptances.regionKey,
        globalDocId: legalAcceptances.globalDocId,
        globalDocVersion: legalAcceptances.globalDocVersion,
        globalDocSha256: legalAcceptances.globalDocSha256,
        addendumId: legalAcceptances.addendumId,
        addendumVersion: legalAcceptances.addendumVersion,
        addendumSha256: legalAcceptances.addendumSha256,
        combinedSha256: legalAcceptances.combinedSha256,
        acceptedAt: legalAcceptances.acceptedAt,
        ipAddress: legalAcceptances.ipAddress,
        userAgent: legalAcceptances.userAgent,
        ledgerSeq: legalAcceptances.ledgerSeq,
        prevLedgerHash: legalAcceptances.prevLedgerHash,
        ledgerHash: legalAcceptances.ledgerHash,
      })
      .from(legalAcceptances)
      .where(where as any)
      .orderBy(desc(legalAcceptances.acceptedAt))
      .limit(limit)
      .all();

    const header = [
      "id","userId","emailAtAcceptance","countryIso2","regionKey",
      "globalDocId","globalDocVersion","globalDocSha256",
      "addendumId","addendumVersion","addendumSha256",
      "combinedSha256","acceptedAtMs","ipAddress","userAgent",
      "ledgerSeq","prevLedgerHash","ledgerHash"
    ];

    const escape = (v: any) => {
      const s = v == null ? "" : String(v);
      if (s.includes('"') || s.includes(",") || s.includes("\n") || s.includes("\r")) {
        return `"${s.replaceAll('"', '""')}"`;
      }
      return s;
    };

    const lines = [header.join(",")];

    for (const r of rows) {
      const acceptedAtMs =
        (r.acceptedAt as any) instanceof Date ? (r.acceptedAt as any).getTime() : Number(r.acceptedAt);

      lines.push(
        [
          r.id,
          r.userId ?? "",
          r.emailAtAcceptance ?? "",
          r.countryIso2 ?? "",
          r.regionKey ?? "",
          r.globalDocId ?? "",
          r.globalDocVersion ?? "",
          r.globalDocSha256 ?? "",
          r.addendumId ?? "",
          r.addendumVersion ?? "",
          r.addendumSha256 ?? "",
          r.combinedSha256 ?? "",
          acceptedAtMs || "",
          r.ipAddress ?? "",
          r.userAgent ?? "",
          r.ledgerSeq ?? "",
          r.prevLedgerHash ?? "",
          r.ledgerHash ?? "",
        ].map(escape).join(",")
      );
    }

    const csv = lines.join("\n");
    res.setHeader("content-type", "text/csv; charset=utf-8");
    res.setHeader("content-disposition", `attachment; filename="legal_acceptances_${Date.now()}.csv"`);
    return res.send(csv);
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Export failed." });
  }
});

1.2 Wire the route in server/index.ts
// FILE: /server/index.ts
import { adminLegalAcceptancesRouter } from "./routes/adminLegalAcceptances";

// ...

2) Frontend: Admin mini-tab component “Legal Acceptances”
2.1 client/src/pages/AdminLegalAcceptances.tsx
// FILE: /client/src/pages/AdminLegalAcceptances.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

type ListRow = {
  id: number;
  userId: number | null;
  emailAtAcceptance: string | null;
  countryIso2: string;
  regionKey: string | null;

  globalDocId: string;
  globalDocVersion: string;
  globalDocSha256: string;

  addendumId: string;
  addendumVersion: string;
  addendumSha256: string;

  combinedSha256: string;
  acceptedAt: any;

  ipAddress: string | null;
  userAgent: string | null;

  ledgerSeq: number;
  prevLedgerHash: string;
  ledgerHash: string;
};

export default function AdminLegalAcceptances() {
  const [countryIso2, setCountryIso2] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [userId, setUserId] = useState("");

  const [fromDate, setFromDate] = useState(""); // yyyy-mm-dd
  const [toDate, setToDate] = useState(""); // yyyy-mm-dd

  const [limit, setLimit] = useState(50);
  const [offset, setOffset] = useState(0);

  const [total, setTotal] = useState(0);
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<ListRow[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const [detailOpen, setDetailOpen] = useState(false);
  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<any>(null);

  const [validateOpen, setValidateOpen] = useState(false);
  const [validateResult, setValidateResult] = useState<any>(null);
  const [validateN, setValidateN] = useState(500);

  const queryParams = useMemo(() => {
    const qs = new URLSearchParams();
    qs.set("limit", String(limit));
    qs.set("offset", String(offset));

    if (countryIso2.trim()) qs.set("countryIso2", countryIso2.trim().toUpperCase());
    if (email.trim()) qs.set("email", email.trim());
    if (userId.trim()) qs.set("userId", userId.trim());

    const toMs = (d: string, endOfDay: boolean) => {
      if (!d) return "";
      const dt = new Date(d + "T00:00:00");
      if (endOfDay) dt.setHours(23, 59, 59, 999);
      return String(dt.getTime());
    };

    if (fromDate) qs.set("fromMs", toMs(fromDate, false));
    if (toDate) qs.set("toMs", toMs(toDate, true));

    return qs.toString();
  }, [countryIso2, email, userId, fromDate, toDate, limit, offset]);

  async function load() {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/legal-acceptances/list?${queryParams}`);
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!data?.ok) throw new Error(data?.error || "Failed to load.");

      setTotal(Number(data.total || 0));
      setRows(data.rows || []);
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(e?.message || "Load failed.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [queryParams]);

  function fmtTime(v: any) {
    try {
      const d = v instanceof Date ? v : new Date(v);
      return d.toLocaleString();
    } catch {
      return String(v ?? "");
    }
  }

  async function openDetail(id: number) {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/legal-acceptances/${id}`);
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!data?.ok) {
      alert(data?.error || "Failed to load detail.");
      return;
    }
    setDetail(data.row);
    setDetailOpen(true);
  }

  async function runValidate() {
    setValidateResult(null);
    const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/legal-acceptances/validate-chain`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ mode: "lastN", n: validateN }),
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!data?.ok) {
      alert(data?.error || "Validation failed.");
      return;
    }
    setValidateResult(data);
  }

  function exportCsv() {
    const qs = new URLSearchParams(queryParams);
    qs.delete("limit");
    qs.delete("offset");
    qs.set("limit", "2000");

    const url = `/api/admin/legal-acceptances/export.csv?${qs.toString()}`;
    window.open(url, "_blank");
  }

  const page = Math.floor(offset / limit) + 1;
  const pageCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / limit));

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 12 }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "baseline" }}>
        <div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 700 }}>Legal Acceptances</div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>
            Hedge-fund-grade acceptance ledger: filter, inspect, validate chain, export.
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
          <button style={btn} onClick={() => { setValidateOpen(true); runValidate(); }}>
            Validate Ledger
          </button>
          <button style={btn} onClick={exportCsv}>
            Export CSV
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Filters */}
      <div style={{ ...card, display: "grid", gap: 10, gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(6, minmax(140px, 1fr))" }}>
        <Field label="Country ISO2">
          <input value={countryIso2} onChange={(e) => { setOffset(0); setCountryIso2(e.target.value.toUpperCase()); }} style={input} placeholder="KE" />
        </Field>

        <Field label="Email contains">
          <input value={email} onChange={(e) => { setOffset(0); setEmail(e.target.value); }} style={input} placeholder="gmail" />
        </Field>

        <Field label="User ID">
          <input value={userId} onChange={(e) => { setOffset(0); setUserId(e.target.value); }} style={input} placeholder="123" />
        </Field>

        <Field label="From (date)">
          <input type="date" value={fromDate} onChange={(e) => { setOffset(0); setFromDate(e.target.value); }} style={input} />
        </Field>

        <Field label="To (date)">
          <input type="date" value={toDate} onChange={(e) => { setOffset(0); setToDate(e.target.value); }} style={input} />
        </Field>

        <Field label="Limit">
          <select value={limit} onChange={(e) => { setOffset(0); setLimit(Number(e.target.value)); }} style={input}>
            {[25, 50, 100, 200].map((n) => (
              <option key={n} value={n}>{n}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </Field>
      </div>

      {/* Table */}
      <div style={card}>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", marginBottom: 8 }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>
            {loading ? "Loading..." : `Total: ${total} | Page ${page}/${pageCount}`}
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
            <button style={btnSmall} disabled={offset <= 0} onClick={() => setOffset(Math.max(0, offset - limit))}>Prev</button>
            <button style={btnSmall} disabled={offset + limit >= total} onClick={() => setOffset(offset + limit)}>Next</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)", borderRadius: 10, overflow: "hidden" }}>
          <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "70px 110px 220px 80px 170px 210px 120px 120px", padding: 10, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.04)", fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700 }}>
            <div>ID</div>
            <div>Accepted</div>
            <div>Email</div>
            <div>ISO2</div>
            <div>Addendum</div>
            <div>Combined SHA</div>
            <div>Ledger Seq</div>
            <div>Actions</div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ maxHeight: 420, overflow: "auto" }}>
            {rows.map((r) => (
              <div key={r.id} style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "70px 110px 220px 80px 170px 210px 120px 120px", padding: 10, borderTop: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.08)", fontSize: 12, alignItems: "center" }}>
                <div>{r.id}</div>
                <div>{fmtTime(r.acceptedAt)}</div>
                <div title={r.emailAtAcceptance || ""} style={{ overflow: "hidden", textOverflow: "ellipsis", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>
                  {r.emailAtAcceptance || "(none)"}
                </div>
                <div>{r.countryIso2}</div>
                <div title={`${r.addendumId} v${r.addendumVersion}`}>
                  {r.addendumId} v{r.addendumVersion}
                </div>
                <div style={{ fontFamily: "monospace" }} title={r.combinedSha256}>
                  {r.combinedSha256.slice(0, 18)}…
                </div>
                <div>{r.ledgerSeq}</div>
                <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
                  <button style={btnSmall} onClick={() => openDetail(r.id)}>View</button>
                </div>
              </div>
            ))}
            {rows.length === 0 && <div style={{ padding: 10, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.7 }}>No rows found.</div>}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Detail modal */}
      <Modal open={detailOpen} onClose={() => setDetailOpen(false)} title={`Acceptance #${detail?.id ?? ""}`}>
        {detail && (
          <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 10, fontSize: 12 }}>
            <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 10 }}>
              <Info label="User ID" value={detail.userId} />
              <Info label="Email" value={detail.emailAtAcceptance} />
              <Info label="Country" value={detail.countryIso2} />
              <Info label="Region Key" value={detail.regionKey ?? "—"} />
              <Info label="Accepted At" value={new Date(detail.acceptedAt).toLocaleString()} />
              <Info label="IP" value={detail.ipAddress ?? "—"} />
              <Info label="Ledger Seq" value={detail.ledgerSeq} />
              <Info label="Ledger Hash" value={detail.ledgerHash} mono />
              <Info label="Prev Ledger Hash" value={detail.prevLedgerHash} mono />
              <Info label="Combined SHA256" value={detail.combinedSha256} mono />
              <Info label="Global Version" value={`${detail.globalDocId} v${detail.globalDocVersion}`} />
              <Info label="Addendum Version" value={`${detail.addendumId} v${detail.addendumVersion}`} />
            </div>

            <details>
              <summary style={{ cursor: "pointer" }}>Terms Token (HMAC)</summary>
              <pre style={pre}>{String(detail.termsToken || "")}</pre>
            </details>

            <details open>
              <summary style={{ cursor: "pointer" }}>Rendered Combined Text (exact snapshot)</summary>
              <pre style={pre}>{String(detail.combinedText || "")}</pre>
            </details>
          </div>
        )}
      </Modal>

      {/* Validate modal */}
      <Modal open={validateOpen} onClose={() => setValidateOpen(false)} title="Validate Acceptance Ledger Chain">
        <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 10, fontSize: 12 }}>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10, alignItems: "end" }}>
            <Field label="Validate last N rows">
              <input value={String(validateN)} onChange={(e) => setValidateN(Number(e.target.value || 0))} style={input} />
            </Field>
            <button style={btn} onClick={runValidate}>Run</button>
          </div>

          {validateResult?.summary && (
            <div style={{ padding: 10, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)", borderRadius: 10 }}>
              <div><strong>Rows:</strong> {validateResult.summary.rows}</div>
              <div><strong>Seq range:</strong> {validateResult.summary.firstSeq} → {validateResult.summary.lastSeq}</div>
              <div>
                <strong>Status:</strong>{" "}
                <span style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>
                  {validateResult.summary.ok ? "OK" : "ISSUES FOUND"}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {Array.isArray(validateResult?.issues) && validateResult.issues.length > 0 && (
            <div style={{ maxHeight: 320, overflow: "auto", border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)", borderRadius: 10 }}>
              <div style={{ padding: 10, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.04)", fontWeight: 700 }}>Issues</div>
              {validateResult.issues.slice(0, 500).map((iss: any, idx: number) => (
                <div key={idx} style={{ padding: 10, borderTop: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.08)", fontFamily: "monospace" }}>
                  {JSON.stringify(iss)}
                </div>
              ))}
              {validateResult.issues.length > 500 && (
                <div style={{ padding: 10, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>
                  Showing first 500 issues.
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {validateResult?.summary && validateResult.summary.ok && (
            <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>
              No issues detected in the requested validation range.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </Modal>
    </div>
  );
}

function Field(props: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <label style={{ display: "grid", gap: 6 }}>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.8 }}>{props.label}</div>
      {props.children}
    </label>
  );
}

function Info(props: { label: string; value: any; mono?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)", borderRadius: 10, padding: 10 }}>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>{props.label}</div>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 12, fontFamily: props.mono ? "monospace" : "inherit", wordBreak: "break-word" }}>
        {props.value == null || props.value === "" ? "—" : String(props.value)}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Modal(props: { open: boolean; onClose: () => void; title: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  if (!props.open) return null;
  return (
    <div style={overlay} onMouseDown={props.onClose}>
      <div style={modal} onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", gap: 12 }}>
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>{props.title}</div>
          <button style={btn} onClick={props.onClose}>Close</button>
        </div>
        <div style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>{props.children}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

const card: React.CSSProperties = {
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)",
  borderRadius: 12,
  padding: 12,
  background: "white",
};

const input: React.CSSProperties = {
  width: "100%",
  padding: "10px 12px",
  borderRadius: 10,
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.2)",
  fontSize: 12,
};

const btn: React.CSSProperties = {
  padding: "10px 12px",
  borderRadius: 10,
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.25)",
  background: "white",
  cursor: "pointer",
  fontWeight: 700,
  fontSize: 12,
};

const btnSmall: React.CSSProperties = {
  padding: "8px 10px",
  borderRadius: 10,
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.2)",
  background: "white",
  cursor: "pointer",
  fontSize: 12,
};

const overlay: React.CSSProperties = {
  position: "fixed",
  inset: 0,
  background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.35)",
  display: "grid",
  placeItems: "center",
  padding: 16,
  zIndex: 1000,
};

const modal: React.CSSProperties = {
  width: "min(1100px, 96vw)",
  background: "white",
  borderRadius: 12,
  padding: 14,
  boxShadow: "0 12px 30px rgba(0,0,0,0.25)",
  maxHeight: "92vh",
  overflow: "auto",
};

const pre: React.CSSProperties = {
  whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)",
  borderRadius: 10,
  padding: 10,
  maxHeight: 420,
  overflow: "auto",
  marginTop: 8,
};

4) Notes on performance and correctness

The list endpoint intentionally does not return combinedText (heavy). Detail endpoint returns it.

Chain validation recomputes:

ledgerHash from stored fields

combinedSha256 from stored combinedText

prevLedgerHash linkage across the range

Export defaults to metadata only (no giant text blobs).

If you want the acceptance tab to also “resolve” the exact legal doc version currently stored in legalDocuments (by matching sha256 to a legalDocuments record and linking to the “Legal Docs” minitab), I can add a Resolve Doc Links button and a server endpoint that returns {globalDocumentId, addendumDocumentId} for each acceptance row.

resolve
Thought for 34s

Understood. Below is the “Resolve Doc Links” implementation that maps each legal acceptance row to the corresponding Legal Docs DB record(s) using sha256 matching, with:

Backend endpoint: POST /api/admin/legal-acceptances/resolve-doc-links

UI button: “Resolve Doc Links” to resolve the currently loaded page of rows

Detail modal enhancement: shows resolved Global/Addendum doc IDs and allows viewing the resolved doc content inline

This works even after future doc replacements, because the linkage is based on the exact sha256 stored at acceptance time.

1) Backend: add resolve endpoint
1.1 Update server/routes/adminLegalAcceptances.ts

Add the new route near the other admin routes (e.g., after /list), keeping existing code intact.

// FILE: /server/routes/adminLegalAcceptances.ts
// ADD THESE IMPORTS at top with others:
import { inArray } from "drizzle-orm";
import { legalDocuments } from "../../shared/schema";

// ... existing code ...

// POST /api/admin/legal-acceptances/resolve-doc-links
// body: { ids: number[] }
// returns: { [acceptanceId]: { global: {best, matches[]}, addendum: {best, matches[]}} }
adminLegalAcceptancesRouter.post("/resolve-doc-links", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const idsRaw = (req.body || {}).ids;
    if (!Array.isArray(idsRaw)) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "ids must be an array." });

    const ids = idsRaw
      .map((x) => Number(x))
      .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0)
      .slice(0, 200);

    if (ids.length === 0) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "No valid ids provided." });

    // Pull acceptance rows for those IDs
    const acceptances = db
      .select({
        id: legalAcceptances.id,
        globalDocSha256: legalAcceptances.globalDocSha256,
        addendumSha256: legalAcceptances.addendumSha256,
      })
      .from(legalAcceptances)
      .where(inArray(legalAcceptances.id, ids))
      .all();

    // Collect unique shas
    const globalShas = Array.from(
      new Set(acceptances.map((a) => String(a.globalDocSha256 || "")).filter(Boolean))
    );
    const addendumShas = Array.from(
      new Set(acceptances.map((a) => String(a.addendumSha256 || "")).filter(Boolean))
    );

    // Fetch candidate legalDocuments by sha
    const globalDocs =
      globalShas.length === 0
        ? []
        : db
            .select({
              id: legalDocuments.id,
              docSet: legalDocuments.docSet,
              docType: legalDocuments.docType,
              jurisdictionType: legalDocuments.jurisdictionType,
              jurisdictionKey: legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey,
              version: legalDocuments.version,
              sha256: legalDocuments.sha256,
              createdAt: legalDocuments.createdAt,
            })
            .from(legalDocuments)
            .where(
              and(
                eq(legalDocuments.docSet, "DOC1"),
                eq(legalDocuments.docType, "GLOBAL_MASTER"),
                inArray(legalDocuments.sha256, globalShas)
              ) as any
            )
            .orderBy(desc(legalDocuments.createdAt))
            .all();

    const addendumDocs =
      addendumShas.length === 0
        ? []
        : db
            .select({
              id: legalDocuments.id,
              docSet: legalDocuments.docSet,
              docType: legalDocuments.docType,
              jurisdictionType: legalDocuments.jurisdictionType,
              jurisdictionKey: legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey,
              version: legalDocuments.version,
              sha256: legalDocuments.sha256,
              createdAt: legalDocuments.createdAt,
            })
            .from(legalDocuments)
            .where(
              and(
                eq(legalDocuments.docSet, "DOC1"),
                eq(legalDocuments.docType, "ADDENDUM"),
                inArray(legalDocuments.sha256, addendumShas)
              ) as any
            )
            .orderBy(desc(legalDocuments.createdAt))
            .all();

    // Build sha -> docs[] maps
    const globalMap = new Map<string, any[]>();
    for (const d of globalDocs) {
      const key = String(d.sha256);
      if (!globalMap.has(key)) globalMap.set(key, []);
      globalMap.get(key)!.push(d);
    }

    const addMap = new Map<string, any[]>();
    for (const d of addendumDocs) {
      const key = String(d.sha256);
      if (!addMap.has(key)) addMap.set(key, []);
      addMap.get(key)!.push(d);
    }

    // Choose best match:
    // - If single match => it
    // - If multiple => latest createdAt (already ordered desc)
    const chooseBest = (arr: any[] | undefined) => {
      if (!arr || arr.length === 0) return null;
      if (arr.length === 1) return arr[0];
      return arr[0]; // latest createdAt
    };

    const resolved: Record<
      number,
      {
        global: { best: any | null; matches: any[] };
        addendum: { best: any | null; matches: any[] };
      }
    > = {};

    for (const a of acceptances) {
      const gSha = String(a.globalDocSha256 || "");
      const adSha = String(a.addendumSha256 || "");

      const gMatches = globalMap.get(gSha) || [];
      const aMatches = addMap.get(adSha) || [];

      resolved[Number(a.id)] = {
        global: { best: chooseBest(gMatches), matches: gMatches },
        addendum: { best: chooseBest(aMatches), matches: aMatches },
      };
    }

    return res.json({ ok: true, resolved });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Resolve failed." });
  }
});


No additional wiring is needed if your router is already mounted at:
app.use("/api/admin/legal-acceptances", adminLegalAcceptancesRouter);

2) Frontend: add “Resolve Doc Links” UI + display
2.1 Update client/src/pages/AdminLegalAcceptances.tsx

Replace the file with the version below (it includes your existing tab plus the resolve functionality). If you prefer patch-style edits, you can still copy/paste and diff.

// FILE: /client/src/pages/AdminLegalAcceptances.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

type ListRow = {
  id: number;
  userId: number | null;
  emailAtAcceptance: string | null;
  countryIso2: string;
  regionKey: string | null;

  globalDocId: string;
  globalDocVersion: string;
  globalDocSha256: string;

  addendumId: string;
  addendumVersion: string;
  addendumSha256: string;

  combinedSha256: string;
  acceptedAt: any;

  ipAddress: string | null;
  userAgent: string | null;

  ledgerSeq: number;
  prevLedgerHash: string;
  ledgerHash: string;
};

type DocLite = {
  id: number;
  docSet: string;
  docType: string;
  jurisdictionType: string;
  jurisdictionKey: string;
  version: string;
  sha256: string;
  createdAt: any;
};

type ResolvedMap = Record<
  number,
  {
    global: { best: DocLite | null; matches: DocLite[] };
    addendum: { best: DocLite | null; matches: DocLite[] };
  }
>;

export default function AdminLegalAcceptances() {
  const [countryIso2, setCountryIso2] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [userId, setUserId] = useState("");

  const [fromDate, setFromDate] = useState("");
  const [toDate, setToDate] = useState("");

  const [limit, setLimit] = useState(50);
  const [offset, setOffset] = useState(0);

  const [total, setTotal] = useState(0);
  const [rows, setRows] = useState<ListRow[]>([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const [detailOpen, setDetailOpen] = useState(false);
  const [detail, setDetail] = useState<any>(null);

  const [validateOpen, setValidateOpen] = useState(false);
  const [validateResult, setValidateResult] = useState<any>(null);
  const [validateN, setValidateN] = useState(500);

  // NEW: resolved doc links (by acceptanceId)
  const [resolved, setResolved] = useState<ResolvedMap>({});
  const [resolving, setResolving] = useState(false);

  // NEW: quick doc-viewer inside the acceptance modal
  const [docViewOpen, setDocViewOpen] = useState(false);
  const [docView, setDocView] = useState<{ title: string; content: string } | null>(null);

  const queryParams = useMemo(() => {
    const qs = new URLSearchParams();
    qs.set("limit", String(limit));
    qs.set("offset", String(offset));

    if (countryIso2.trim()) qs.set("countryIso2", countryIso2.trim().toUpperCase());
    if (email.trim()) qs.set("email", email.trim());
    if (userId.trim()) qs.set("userId", userId.trim());

    const toMs = (d: string, endOfDay: boolean) => {
      if (!d) return "";
      const dt = new Date(d + "T00:00:00");
      if (endOfDay) dt.setHours(23, 59, 59, 999);
      return String(dt.getTime());
    };

    if (fromDate) qs.set("fromMs", toMs(fromDate, false));
    if (toDate) qs.set("toMs", toMs(toDate, true));

    return qs.toString();
  }, [countryIso2, email, userId, fromDate, toDate, limit, offset]);

  async function load() {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/legal-acceptances/list?${queryParams}`);
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!data?.ok) throw new Error(data?.error || "Failed to load.");

      setTotal(Number(data.total || 0));
      setRows(data.rows || []);
      // Clear resolved for new page (prevents showing stale mappings)
      setResolved({});
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(e?.message || "Load failed.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [queryParams]);

  function fmtTime(v: any) {
    try {
      const d = v instanceof Date ? v : new Date(v);
      return d.toLocaleString();
    } catch {
      return String(v ?? "");
    }
  }

  async function openDetail(id: number) {
    const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/legal-acceptances/${id}`);
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!data?.ok) {
      alert(data?.error || "Failed to load detail.");
      return;
    }
    setDetail(data.row);
    setDetailOpen(true);
  }

  async function runValidate() {
    setValidateResult(null);
    const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/legal-acceptances/validate-chain`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({ mode: "lastN", n: validateN }),
    });
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!data?.ok) {
      alert(data?.error || "Validation failed.");
      return;
    }
    setValidateResult(data);
  }

  function exportCsv() {
    const qs = new URLSearchParams(queryParams);
    qs.delete("limit");
    qs.delete("offset");
    qs.set("limit", "2000");
    const url = `/api/admin/legal-acceptances/export.csv?${qs.toString()}`;
    window.open(url, "_blank");
  }

  // NEW: resolve doc links for currently loaded rows
  async function resolveDocLinksForPage() {
    if (!rows.length) return;
    setResolving(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/legal-acceptances/resolve-doc-links`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ ids: rows.map((r) => r.id) }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!data?.ok) throw new Error(data?.error || "Resolve failed.");
      setResolved(data.resolved || {});
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(e?.message || "Resolve failed.");
    } finally {
      setResolving(false);
    }
  }

  async function openLegalDocById(docId: number) {
    // Uses the Legal Docs admin endpoint you already added:
    // GET /api/admin/legal-docs/document/:id
    const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/legal-docs/document/${docId}`);
    const data = await res.json();
    if (!data?.ok) {
      alert(data?.error || "Failed to load legal doc.");
      return;
    }
    const d = data.doc;
    setDocView({
      title: `${d.docSet}/${d.docType}/${d.jurisdictionType}/${d.jurisdictionKey} v${d.version} (#${d.id})`,
      content: String(d.content || ""),
    });
    setDocViewOpen(true);
  }

  const page = Math.floor(offset / limit) + 1;
  const pageCount = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(total / limit));

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 12 }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "baseline" }}>
        <div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 700 }}>Legal Acceptances</div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>
            Filter, inspect, validate acceptance ledger, export. Resolve links to Legal Docs versions by SHA.
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
          <button style={btn} onClick={resolveDocLinksForPage} disabled={resolving || rows.length === 0}>
            {resolving ? "Resolving..." : "Resolve Doc Links"}
          </button>
          <button style={btn} onClick={() => { setValidateOpen(true); runValidate(); }}>
            Validate Ledger
          </button>
          <button style={btn} onClick={exportCsv}>
            Export CSV
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Filters */}
      <div style={{ ...card, display: "grid", gap: 10, gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(6, minmax(140px, 1fr))" }}>
        <Field label="Country ISO2">
          <input value={countryIso2} onChange={(e) => { setOffset(0); setCountryIso2(e.target.value.toUpperCase()); }} style={input} placeholder="KE" />
        </Field>

        <Field label="Email contains">
          <input value={email} onChange={(e) => { setOffset(0); setEmail(e.target.value); }} style={input} placeholder="gmail" />
        </Field>

        <Field label="User ID">
          <input value={userId} onChange={(e) => { setOffset(0); setUserId(e.target.value); }} style={input} placeholder="123" />
        </Field>

        <Field label="From (date)">
          <input type="date" value={fromDate} onChange={(e) => { setOffset(0); setFromDate(e.target.value); }} style={input} />
        </Field>

        <Field label="To (date)">
          <input type="date" value={toDate} onChange={(e) => { setOffset(0); setToDate(e.target.value); }} style={input} />
        </Field>

        <Field label="Limit">
          <select value={limit} onChange={(e) => { setOffset(0); setLimit(Number(e.target.value)); }} style={input}>
            {[25, 50, 100, 200].map((n) => (
              <option key={n} value={n}>{n}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </Field>
      </div>

      {/* Table */}
      <div style={card}>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", marginBottom: 8 }}>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>
            {loading ? "Loading..." : `Total: ${total} | Page ${page}/${pageCount}`}
          </div>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
            <button style={btnSmall} disabled={offset <= 0} onClick={() => setOffset(Math.max(0, offset - limit))}>Prev</button>
            <button style={btnSmall} disabled={offset + limit >= total} onClick={() => setOffset(offset + limit)}>Next</button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)", borderRadius: 10, overflow: "hidden" }}>
          <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "70px 110px 220px 80px 210px 220px 120px 130px", padding: 10, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.04)", fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 700 }}>
            <div>ID</div>
            <div>Accepted</div>
            <div>Email</div>
            <div>ISO2</div>
            <div>Resolved Docs</div>
            <div>Combined SHA</div>
            <div>Ledger Seq</div>
            <div>Actions</div>
          </div>

          <div style={{ maxHeight: 420, overflow: "auto" }}>
            {rows.map((r) => {
              const rr = resolved[r.id];
              const gBest = rr?.global?.best || null;
              const aBest = rr?.addendum?.best || null;

              const resolvedLabel =
                rr
                  ? `G#${gBest?.id ?? "—"} / A#${aBest?.id ?? "—"}`
                  : "—";

              const ambiguity =
                rr
                  ? `${rr.global.matches.length > 1 ? `G(${rr.global.matches.length}) ` : ""}${rr.addendum.matches.length > 1 ? `A(${rr.addendum.matches.length})` : ""}`.trim()
                  : "";

              return (
                <div key={r.id} style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "70px 110px 220px 80px 210px 220px 120px 130px", padding: 10, borderTop: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.08)", fontSize: 12, alignItems: "center" }}>
                  <div>{r.id}</div>
                  <div>{fmtTime(r.acceptedAt)}</div>
                  <div title={r.emailAtAcceptance || ""} style={{ overflow: "hidden", textOverflow: "ellipsis", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>
                    {r.emailAtAcceptance || "(none)"}
                  </div>
                  <div>{r.countryIso2}</div>

                  <div style={{ fontFamily: "monospace" }} title={ambiguity ? `Ambiguous: ${ambiguity}` : ""}>
                    {resolvedLabel}{ambiguity ? `  ${ambiguity}` : ""}
                  </div>

                  <div style={{ fontFamily: "monospace" }} title={r.combinedSha256}>
                    {r.combinedSha256.slice(0, 18)}…
                  </div>

                  <div>{r.ledgerSeq}</div>

                  <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
                    <button style={btnSmall} onClick={() => openDetail(r.id)}>View</button>
                    {resolved[r.id]?.global?.best?.id && (
                      <button style={btnSmall} onClick={() => openLegalDocById(resolved[r.id].global.best!.id)}>
                        Open Global
                      </button>
                    )}
                    {resolved[r.id]?.addendum?.best?.id && (
                      <button style={btnSmall} onClick={() => openLegalDocById(resolved[r.id].addendum.best!.id)}>
                        Open Addendum
                      </button>
                    )}
                  </div>
                </div>
              );
            })}
            {rows.length === 0 && <div style={{ padding: 10, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.7 }}>No rows found.</div>}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ marginTop: 10, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>
          Resolution notes: matches are resolved by SHA256 against `legalDocuments` (DOC1/GLOBAL_MASTER and DOC1/ADDENDUM).
          If multiple matches exist, the most recently created doc is selected as “best”.
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Detail modal */}
      <Modal open={detailOpen} onClose={() => setDetailOpen(false)} title={`Acceptance #${detail?.id ?? ""}`}>
        {detail && (
          <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 10, fontSize: 12 }}>
            <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 10 }}>
              <Info label="User ID" value={detail.userId} />
              <Info label="Email" value={detail.emailAtAcceptance} />
              <Info label="Country" value={detail.countryIso2} />
              <Info label="Region Key" value={detail.regionKey ?? "—"} />
              <Info label="Accepted At" value={new Date(detail.acceptedAt).toLocaleString()} />
              <Info label="IP" value={detail.ipAddress ?? "—"} />
              <Info label="Ledger Seq" value={detail.ledgerSeq} />
              <Info label="Ledger Hash" value={detail.ledgerHash} mono />
              <Info label="Prev Ledger Hash" value={detail.prevLedgerHash} mono />
              <Info label="Combined SHA256" value={detail.combinedSha256} mono />

              <Info label="Global (snapshot)" value={`${detail.globalDocId} v${detail.globalDocVersion}`} />
              <Info label="Addendum (snapshot)" value={`${detail.addendumId} v${detail.addendumVersion}`} />
            </div>

            {resolved?.[detail.id] && (
              <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 8, padding: 10, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)", borderRadius: 10 }}>
                <div style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>Resolved Legal Docs (by SHA)</div>
                <div style={{ display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "1fr 1fr", gap: 10 }}>
                  <ResolvedBox title="Global Best Match" doc={resolved[detail.id].global.best} onOpen={openLegalDocById} />
                  <ResolvedBox title="Addendum Best Match" doc={resolved[detail.id].addendum.best} onOpen={openLegalDocById} />
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            <details>
              <summary style={{ cursor: "pointer" }}>Terms Token (HMAC)</summary>
              <pre style={pre}>{String(detail.termsToken || "")}</pre>
            </details>

            <details open>
              <summary style={{ cursor: "pointer" }}>Rendered Combined Text (exact snapshot)</summary>
              <pre style={pre}>{String(detail.combinedText || "")}</pre>
            </details>
          </div>
        )}
      </Modal>

      {/* Validate modal */}
      <Modal open={validateOpen} onClose={() => setValidateOpen(false)} title="Validate Acceptance Ledger Chain">
        <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 10, fontSize: 12 }}>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 10, alignItems: "end" }}>
            <Field label="Validate last N rows">
              <input value={String(validateN)} onChange={(e) => setValidateN(Number(e.target.value || 0))} style={input} />
            </Field>
            <button style={btn} onClick={runValidate}>Run</button>
          </div>

          {validateResult?.summary && (
            <div style={{ padding: 10, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)", borderRadius: 10 }}>
              <div><strong>Rows:</strong> {validateResult.summary.rows}</div>
              <div><strong>Seq range:</strong> {validateResult.summary.firstSeq} → {validateResult.summary.lastSeq}</div>
              <div>
                <strong>Status:</strong>{" "}
                <span style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>
                  {validateResult.summary.ok ? "OK" : "ISSUES FOUND"}
                </span>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}

          {Array.isArray(validateResult?.issues) && validateResult.issues.length > 0 && (
            <div style={{ maxHeight: 320, overflow: "auto", border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)", borderRadius: 10 }}>
              <div style={{ padding: 10, background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.04)", fontWeight: 700 }}>Issues</div>
              {validateResult.issues.slice(0, 500).map((iss: any, idx: number) => (
                <div key={idx} style={{ padding: 10, borderTop: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.08)", fontFamily: "monospace" }}>
                  {JSON.stringify(iss)}
                </div>
              ))}
              {validateResult.issues.length > 500 && (
                <div style={{ padding: 10, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>
                  Showing first 500 issues.
                </div>
              )}
            </div>
          )}

          {validateResult?.summary && validateResult.summary.ok && (
            <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>
              No issues detected in the requested validation range.
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </Modal>

      {/* Inline Legal Doc viewer modal */}
      <Modal open={docViewOpen} onClose={() => setDocViewOpen(false)} title={docView?.title || "Legal Doc"}>
        <pre style={pre}>{docView?.content || ""}</pre>
      </Modal>
    </div>
  );
}

function ResolvedBox(props: { title: string; doc: DocLite | null; onOpen: (id: number) => void }) {
  const d = props.doc;
  return (
    <div style={{ border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)", borderRadius: 10, padding: 10 }}>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>{props.title}</div>
      {!d ? (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 6, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.7 }}>No match found.</div>
      ) : (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 6, display: "grid", gap: 6, fontSize: 12 }}>
          <div><strong>ID:</strong> {d.id}</div>
          <div><strong>Target:</strong> {d.docSet}/{d.docType}/{d.jurisdictionType}/{d.jurisdictionKey}</div>
          <div><strong>Version:</strong> {d.version}</div>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "monospace" }}><strong>SHA:</strong> {d.sha256}</div>
          <button style={btnSmall} onClick={() => props.onOpen(d.id)}>Open Doc</button>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function Field(props: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <label style={{ display: "grid", gap: 6 }}>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.8 }}>{props.label}</div>
      {props.children}
    </label>
  );
}

function Info(props: { label: string; value: any; mono?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)", borderRadius: 10, padding: 10 }}>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>{props.label}</div>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 12, fontFamily: props.mono ? "monospace" : "inherit", wordBreak: "break-word" }}>
        {props.value == null || props.value === "" ? "—" : String(props.value)}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Modal(props: { open: boolean; onClose: () => void; title: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  if (!props.open) return null;
  return (
    <div style={overlay} onMouseDown={props.onClose}>
      <div style={modal} onMouseDown={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "center", gap: 12 }}>
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>{props.title}</div>
          <button style={btn} onClick={props.onClose}>Close</button>
        </div>
        <div style={{ marginTop: 12 }}>{props.children}</div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
