3) What “Resolve” does operationally

For each acceptance row:

globalDocSha256 is matched to legalDocuments where docSet=DOC1 and docType=GLOBAL_MASTER

addendumSha256 is matched to legalDocuments where docSet=DOC1 and docType=ADDENDUM

If multiple matches exist (rare but possible if identical content was inserted for different targets), the UI:

Shows G(n) / A(n) counts

Picks “best” as most recently created doc (deterministic)

4) Minimal AdminDashboard wiring reminder

If you haven’t already, ensure the mini-tab renders:

import AdminLegalAcceptances from "./AdminLegalAcceptances";

// ...
if (activeSystemConfigTab === "legalAcceptances") return <AdminLegalAcceptances />;


If you want the “best match” selection rule to be stricter (e.g., for addenda prefer COUNTRY/{ISO2} over REGION/* over DEFAULT/ROW when multiple matches exist), I can adjust the backend to pick best deterministically based on jurisdictionType priority rather than recency.

Yes
Thought for 11s

Below is the stricter, jurisdiction-aware “best match” selection rule for resolve-doc-links.

What changes:

For GLOBAL_MASTER matches, prefer DEFAULT/GLOBAL (then other DEFAULT, then anything else).

For ADDENDUM matches, prefer in this order:

COUNTRY/{acceptance.countryIso2}

REGION/{acceptance.regionKey} (if present)

DEFAULT/ROW

otherwise fallback to the newest match

When ties exist at the same priority, pick the most recently created doc.

Patch: replace the resolve handler in server/routes/adminLegalAcceptances.ts

Replace your existing POST /resolve-doc-links handler with this version:

// FILE: /server/routes/adminLegalAcceptances.ts
// Ensure these imports exist at top:
import { inArray } from "drizzle-orm";
import { legalDocuments } from "../../shared/schema";

// ... keep other code ...

// POST /api/admin/legal-acceptances/resolve-doc-links
// body: { ids: number[] }
// returns: { [acceptanceId]: { global: {best, matches[]}, addendum: {best, matches[]}} }
adminLegalAcceptancesRouter.post("/resolve-doc-links", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const idsRaw = (req.body || {}).ids;
    if (!Array.isArray(idsRaw)) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "ids must be an array." });

    const ids = idsRaw
      .map((x) => Number(x))
      .filter((n) => Number.isFinite(n) && n > 0)
      .slice(0, 200);

    if (ids.length === 0) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "No valid ids provided." });

    // Pull acceptance rows for those IDs (NEED country/region for strict selection)
    const acceptances = db
      .select({
        id: legalAcceptances.id,
        countryIso2: legalAcceptances.countryIso2,
        regionKey: legalAcceptances.regionKey,
        globalDocSha256: legalAcceptances.globalDocSha256,
        addendumSha256: legalAcceptances.addendumSha256,
      })
      .from(legalAcceptances)
      .where(inArray(legalAcceptances.id, ids))
      .all();

    // Collect unique shas
    const globalShas = Array.from(
      new Set(acceptances.map((a) => String(a.globalDocSha256 || "")).filter(Boolean))
    );
    const addendumShas = Array.from(
      new Set(acceptances.map((a) => String(a.addendumSha256 || "")).filter(Boolean))
    );

    // Fetch candidate legalDocuments by sha
    const globalDocs =
      globalShas.length === 0
        ? []
        : db
            .select({
              id: legalDocuments.id,
              docSet: legalDocuments.docSet,
              docType: legalDocuments.docType,
              jurisdictionType: legalDocuments.jurisdictionType,
              jurisdictionKey: legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey,
              version: legalDocuments.version,
              sha256: legalDocuments.sha256,
              createdAt: legalDocuments.createdAt,
            })
            .from(legalDocuments)
            .where(
              and(
                eq(legalDocuments.docSet, "DOC1"),
                eq(legalDocuments.docType, "GLOBAL_MASTER"),
                inArray(legalDocuments.sha256, globalShas)
              ) as any
            )
            .orderBy(desc(legalDocuments.createdAt))
            .all();

    const addendumDocs =
      addendumShas.length === 0
        ? []
        : db
            .select({
              id: legalDocuments.id,
              docSet: legalDocuments.docSet,
              docType: legalDocuments.docType,
              jurisdictionType: legalDocuments.jurisdictionType,
              jurisdictionKey: legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey,
              version: legalDocuments.version,
              sha256: legalDocuments.sha256,
              createdAt: legalDocuments.createdAt,
            })
            .from(legalDocuments)
            .where(
              and(
                eq(legalDocuments.docSet, "DOC1"),
                eq(legalDocuments.docType, "ADDENDUM"),
                inArray(legalDocuments.sha256, addendumShas)
              ) as any
            )
            .orderBy(desc(legalDocuments.createdAt))
            .all();

    // Build sha -> docs[] maps
    const globalMap = new Map<string, any[]>();
    for (const d of globalDocs) {
      const key = String(d.sha256);
      if (!globalMap.has(key)) globalMap.set(key, []);
      globalMap.get(key)!.push(d);
    }

    const addMap = new Map<string, any[]>();
    for (const d of addendumDocs) {
      const key = String(d.sha256);
      if (!addMap.has(key)) addMap.set(key, []);
      addMap.get(key)!.push(d);
    }

    const createdAtMs = (d: any) => {
      const v = d?.createdAt;
      if (!v) return 0;
      return v instanceof Date ? v.getTime() : Number(v) || 0;
    };

    // Strict chooser for GLOBAL: prefer DEFAULT/GLOBAL
    const chooseBestGlobal = (docs: any[] | undefined) => {
      if (!docs || docs.length === 0) return null;

      const score = (d: any) => {
        const jt = String(d.jurisdictionType || "");
        const jk = String(d.jurisdictionKey || "");
        if (jt === "DEFAULT" && jk === "GLOBAL") return 3;
        if (jt === "DEFAULT") return 2;
        return 1;
      };

      return docs
        .slice()
        .sort((a, b) => {
          const sa = score(a);
          const sb = score(b);
          if (sb !== sa) return sb - sa;
          const ta = createdAtMs(a);
          const tb = createdAtMs(b);
          if (tb !== ta) return tb - ta;
          return Number(b.id) - Number(a.id);
        })[0];
    };

    // Strict chooser for ADDENDUM: COUNTRY/{ISO2} > REGION/{regionKey} > DEFAULT/ROW
    const chooseBestAddendum = (docs: any[] | undefined, iso2: string, regionKey: string | null) => {
      if (!docs || docs.length === 0) return null;

      const ISO2 = String(iso2 || "").toUpperCase().trim();
      const RK = regionKey ? String(regionKey).trim() : null;

      const score = (d: any) => {
        const jt = String(d.jurisdictionType || "");
        const jk = String(d.jurisdictionKey || "");
        if (jt === "COUNTRY" && jk === ISO2) return 3;
        if (RK && jt === "REGION" && jk === RK) return 2;
        if (jt === "DEFAULT" && jk === "ROW") return 1;
        return 0;
      };

      return docs
        .slice()
        .sort((a, b) => {
          const sa = score(a);
          const sb = score(b);
          if (sb !== sa) return sb - sa;
          const ta = createdAtMs(a);
          const tb = createdAtMs(b);
          if (tb !== ta) return tb - ta;
          return Number(b.id) - Number(a.id);
        })[0];
    };

    const resolved: Record<
      number,
      {
        global: { best: any | null; matches: any[] };
        addendum: { best: any | null; matches: any[] };
      }
    > = {};

    for (const a of acceptances) {
      const gSha = String(a.globalDocSha256 || "");
      const adSha = String(a.addendumSha256 || "");

      const gMatches = globalMap.get(gSha) || [];
      const aMatches = addMap.get(adSha) || [];

      const bestGlobal = chooseBestGlobal(gMatches);
      const bestAddendum = chooseBestAddendum(aMatches, String(a.countryIso2 || ""), a.regionKey ? String(a.regionKey) : null);

      resolved[Number(a.id)] = {
        global: { best: bestGlobal, matches: gMatches },
        addendum: { best: bestAddendum, matches: aMatches },
      };
    }

    return res.json({ ok: true, resolved });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Resolve failed." });
  }
});

1) Backend: return bestReason + bestScore
1.1 Patch POST /resolve-doc-links in server/routes/adminLegalAcceptances.ts

In the handler you just updated, replace the chooser functions with the versions below and update the resolved[...] object to include reason/score.

// Inside POST /resolve-doc-links handler (same file)

// helper
const pickWithReason = (docs: any[], scorer: (d: any) => { score: number; reason: string }) => {
  if (!docs || docs.length === 0) return { best: null, bestReason: "NO_MATCH", bestScore: -1 };

  const sorted = docs
    .slice()
    .sort((a, b) => {
      const sa = scorer(a);
      const sb = scorer(b);
      if (sb.score !== sa.score) return sb.score - sa.score;

      const ta = createdAtMs(a);
      const tb = createdAtMs(b);
      if (tb !== ta) return tb - ta;

      return Number(b.id) - Number(a.id);
    });

  const best = sorted[0];
  const bestMeta = scorer(best);
  return { best, bestReason: bestMeta.reason, bestScore: bestMeta.score };
};

// GLOBAL scorer: prefer DEFAULT/GLOBAL
const scoreGlobal = (d: any) => {
  const jt = String(d.jurisdictionType || "");
  const jk = String(d.jurisdictionKey || "");
  if (jt === "DEFAULT" && jk === "GLOBAL") return { score: 3, reason: "DEFAULT/GLOBAL" };
  if (jt === "DEFAULT") return { score: 2, reason: "DEFAULT/*" };
  return { score: 1, reason: "OTHER" };
};

// ADDENDUM scorer: COUNTRY/{ISO2} > REGION/{regionKey} > DEFAULT/ROW
const scoreAddendum = (iso2: string, regionKey: string | null) => {
  const ISO2 = String(iso2 || "").toUpperCase().trim();
  const RK = regionKey ? String(regionKey).trim() : null;

  return (d: any) => {
    const jt = String(d.jurisdictionType || "");
    const jk = String(d.jurisdictionKey || "");

    if (jt === "COUNTRY" && jk === ISO2) return { score: 3, reason: `COUNTRY/${ISO2}` };
    if (RK && jt === "REGION" && jk === RK) return { score: 2, reason: `REGION/${RK}` };
    if (jt === "DEFAULT" && jk === "ROW") return { score: 1, reason: "DEFAULT/ROW" };
    return { score: 0, reason: "OTHER" };
  };
};

// ... inside for-loop over acceptances:
for (const a of acceptances) {
  const gSha = String(a.globalDocSha256 || "");
  const adSha = String(a.addendumSha256 || "");

  const gMatches = globalMap.get(gSha) || [];
  const aMatches = addMap.get(adSha) || [];

  const gPicked = pickWithReason(gMatches, scoreGlobal);
  const aPicked = pickWithReason(
    aMatches,
    scoreAddendum(String(a.countryIso2 || ""), a.regionKey ? String(a.regionKey) : null)
  );

  resolved[Number(a.id)] = {
    global: { best: gPicked.best, matches: gMatches, bestReason: gPicked.bestReason, bestScore: gPicked.bestScore },
    addendum: { best: aPicked.best, matches: aMatches, bestReason: aPicked.bestReason, bestScore: aPicked.bestScore },
  };
}

Add.
Thought for 1m 5s

Below is the deep-link hint + auto-prefill for your Legal Docs minitab, plus “Go to Legal Docs” buttons in Legal Acceptances.

Implementation approach (robust, minimal coupling):

Clicking Go Docs (Global/Addendum) stores a prefill payload in localStorage and dispatches a non-breaking navigation event.

AdminLegalDocs reads the prefill once, auto-selects the target, loads versions, and auto-opens the selected document.

A) Add shared helper: Deep-link prefill (frontend)
1) Create client/src/lib/adminDeepLink.ts
// FILE: /client/src/lib/adminDeepLink.ts
export const ADMIN_LEGAL_DOCS_PREFILL_KEY = "adminLegalDocs_prefill_v1";

export type LegalDocsPrefill = {
  docSet: string;
  docType: "GLOBAL_MASTER" | "ADDENDUM";
  jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT" | "COUNTRY" | "REGION";
  jurisdictionKey: string;
  openDocumentId?: number | null;
};

export function setLegalDocsPrefill(prefill: LegalDocsPrefill) {
  try {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
    window.localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_LEGAL_DOCS_PREFILL_KEY, JSON.stringify(prefill));
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

export function getLegalDocsPrefillOnce(): LegalDocsPrefill | null {
  try {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(ADMIN_LEGAL_DOCS_PREFILL_KEY);
    if (!raw) return null;
    window.localStorage.removeItem(ADMIN_LEGAL_DOCS_PREFILL_KEY);
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!parsed?.docSet || !parsed?.docType || !parsed?.jurisdictionType || !parsed?.jurisdictionKey) return null;
    return parsed as LegalDocsPrefill;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

// FILE: /client/src/lib/adminDeepLink.ts

export const ADMIN_LEGAL_DOCS_PREFILL_KEY = "adminLegalDocs_prefill_v2";
export const ADMIN_LEGAL_ACCEPTANCES_PREFILL_KEY = "adminLegalAcceptances_prefill_v2";

export type AdminNavigateRequest = {
  // use stable keys (no spaces) so you can map consistently
  topTabKey?: "userManagement" | "viewAsTrader" | "tradeSettings" | "instruments" | "data" | "tradeAudit" | "systemConfig";
  systemConfigTabKey?: string; // e.g., "legalDocs" | "legalAcceptances" | "migration" etc.
  userManagementTabKey?: string;
};

export type LegalDocsPrefill = {
  docSet: string;
  docType: "GLOBAL_MASTER" | "ADDENDUM";
  jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT" | "COUNTRY" | "REGION";
  jurisdictionKey: string;
  openDocumentId?: number | null;

  // return path (so Legal Docs can show "Back" button)
  returnTo?: {
    kind: "LEGAL_ACCEPTANCE";
    acceptanceId: number;
  } | null;
};

export type LegalAcceptancesPrefill = {
  // Optional filter prefill
  countryIso2?: string | null;
  emailContains?: string | null;
  userId?: number | null;
  fromMs?: number | null;
  toMs?: number | null;

  // Auto-open
  openAcceptanceId?: number | null;
};

export function setLegalDocsPrefill(prefill: LegalDocsPrefill) {
  try {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
    window.localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_LEGAL_DOCS_PREFILL_KEY, JSON.stringify(prefill));
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

export function getLegalDocsPrefillOnce(): LegalDocsPrefill | null {
  try {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return null;
    const raw = window.localStorage.getItem(ADMIN_LEGAL_DOCS_PREFILL_KEY);
    if (!raw) return null;
    window.localStorage.removeItem(ADMIN_LEGAL_DOCS_PREFILL_KEY);
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (!parsed?.docSet || !parsed?.docType || !parsed?.jurisdictionType || !parsed?.jurisdictionKey) return null;
    return parsed as LegalDocsPrefill;
  } catch {
    return null;
  }
}

export function setLegalAcceptancesPrefill(prefill: LegalAcceptancesPrefill) {
  try {
    if (typeof window === "undefined") return;
    window.localStorage.setItem(ADMIN_LEGAL_ACCEPTANCES_PREFILL_KEY, JSON.stringify(prefill));
  } catch {
    // ignore
  }
}

This assumes your legalDocuments table holds the canonical doc text and you treat “current” as the latest created doc per target (the most reliable default). If you later add an explicit isActive flag, you only change the getCurrentDoc() query.

1) Backend: Diff utility (new file)
server/legal/diffUtils.ts
// FILE: /server/legal/diffUtils.ts

type Op = { type: "equal" | "insert" | "delete"; line: string };

function splitLines(s: string): string[] {
  // Preserve empty last line semantics by not dropping trailing newline
  // but also avoid massive memory from \r\n mismatches.
  return String(s || "").replace(/\r\n/g, "\n").split("\n");
}

/**
 * Myers diff (line-based) -> ops
 * Returns full op stream, not hunks. Suitable for admin/legal diffing.
 */
export function myersDiffLines(oldText: string, newText: string): Op[] {
  const a = splitLines(oldText);
  const b = splitLines(newText);

  const N = a.length;
  const M = b.length;
  const MAX = N + M;

  // V is indexed by diagonal k (x - y), store furthest x
  let v = new Map<number, number>();
  v.set(1, 0);

  const trace: Map<number, number>[] = [];

  const getV = (map: Map<number, number>, k: number) => map.get(k) ?? -Infinity;

  for (let d = 0; d <= MAX; d++) {
    // snapshot
    trace.push(new Map(v));

    for (let k = -d; k <= d; k += 2) {
      let x: number;

      // Choose down or right
      if (k === -d || (k !== d && getV(v, k - 1) < getV(v, k + 1))) {
        // Down: insertion in a -> take from k+1
        x = getV(v, k + 1);
      } else {
        // Right: deletion from a -> take from k-1 + 1
        x = getV(v, k - 1) + 1;
      }

      let y = x - k;

      // Follow diagonal (equal lines)
      while (x < N && y < M && a[x] === b[y]) {
        x++;
        y++;
      }

      v.set(k, x);

      if (x >= N && y >= M) {
        return backtrack(trace, a, b);
      }
    }
  }

  // Fallback (should never happen)
  return fallbackAllChanged(a, b);
}

function backtrack(trace: Map<number, number>[], a: string[], b: string[]): Op[] {
  const ops: Op[] = [];
  let x = a.length;
  let y = b.length;

  for (let d = trace.length - 1; d >= 0; d--) {
    const v = trace[d];
    const k = x - y;

    // Determine the previous diagonal
    let prevK: number;
    const vKMinus = v.get(k - 1) ?? -Infinity;
    const vKPlus = v.get(k + 1) ?? -Infinity;

    const isInsertion = k === -d || (k !== d && vKMinus < vKPlus);
    prevK = isInsertion ? k + 1 : k - 1;

    const prevX = v.get(prevK) ?? 0;
    const prevY = prevX - prevK;

    // Emit equal lines on the diagonal
    while (x > prevX && y > prevY) {
      ops.push({ type: "equal", line: a[x - 1] });
      x--;
      y--;
    }

    if (d === 0) break;

    // Emit the edit step
    if (isInsertion) {
      // Insert b[prevY] (we moved down: y decreased)
      ops.push({ type: "insert", line: b[prevY] });
      y = prevY;
      x = prevX;
    } else {
      // Delete a[prevX] (we moved right: x decreased)
      ops.push({ type: "delete", line: a[prevX] });
      x = prevX;
      y = prevY;
    }
  }

  ops.reverse();
  return ops;
}

function fallbackAllChanged(a: string[], b: string[]): Op[] {
  const ops: Op[] = [];
  for (const line of a) ops.push({ type: "delete", line });
  for (const line of b) ops.push({ type: "insert", line });
  return ops;
}

export function renderFullDiff(oldText: string, newText: string) {
  const ops = myersDiffLines(oldText, newText);

  let inserted = 0;
  let deleted = 0;
  let equal = 0;

  const lines: string[] = [];
  lines.push("--- accepted");
  lines.push("+++ current");

  for (const op of ops) {
    if (op.type === "equal") {
      equal++;
      lines.push(` ${op.line}`);
    } else if (op.type === "delete") {
      deleted++;
      lines.push(`-${op.line}`);
    } else {
      inserted++;
      lines.push(`+${op.line}`);
    }
  }

  return {
    text: lines.join("\n"),
    stats: { inserted, deleted, equal, total: ops.length },
  };
}

2) Backend: Add diff endpoint to Admin Legal Acceptances router
Patch server/routes/adminLegalAcceptances.ts

Add imports near top:

import { renderFullDiff } from "../legal/diffUtils";
import { legalDocuments } from "../../shared/schema";


Then add this route (anywhere in the router file, recommended after /:id getter):

// GET /api/admin/legal-acceptances/:id/diff-current
adminLegalAcceptancesRouter.get("/:id/diff-current", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const id = Number(req.params.id);
    if (!id) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "Bad id." });

    const acc = db.select().from(legalAcceptances).where(eq(legalAcceptances.id, id)).get();
    if (!acc) return res.status(404).json({ ok: false, error: "Not found." });

    // Helper: current doc for a target = newest createdAt for that target.
    // If later you add explicit isActive, modify this query accordingly.
    const getCurrentDoc = (target: {
      docSet: string;
      docType: string;
      jurisdictionType: string;
      jurisdictionKey: string;
    }) => {
      return db
        .select({
          id: legalDocuments.id,
          docSet: legalDocuments.docSet,
          docType: legalDocuments.docType,
          jurisdictionType: legalDocuments.jurisdictionType,
          jurisdictionKey: legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey,
          version: legalDocuments.version,
          sha256: legalDocuments.sha256,
          content: legalDocuments.content,
          createdAt: legalDocuments.createdAt,
        })
        .from(legalDocuments)
        .where(
          and(
            eq(legalDocuments.docSet, target.docSet),
            eq(legalDocuments.docType, target.docType),
            eq(legalDocuments.jurisdictionType, target.jurisdictionType),
            eq(legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey, target.jurisdictionKey)
          ) as any
        )
        .orderBy(desc(legalDocuments.createdAt))
        .limit(1)
        .get();
    };

    // Resolve current Global (usually DEFAULT/GLOBAL)
    const currentGlobal =
      getCurrentDoc({
        docSet: "DOC1",
        docType: "GLOBAL_MASTER",
        jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT",
        jurisdictionKey: "GLOBAL",
      }) ||
      // fallback (if you used a different key)
      getCurrentDoc({
        docSet: "DOC1",
        docType: "GLOBAL_MASTER",
        jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT",
        jurisdictionKey: "ROW",
      });

    // Resolve current Addendum with same precedence you use in signup assembly:
    const ISO2 = String(acc.countryIso2 || "").toUpperCase();
    const RK = acc.regionKey ? String(acc.regionKey) : null;

    const currentAddendum =
      (ISO2
        ? getCurrentDoc({
            docSet: "DOC1",
            docType: "ADDENDUM",
            jurisdictionType: "COUNTRY",
            jurisdictionKey: ISO2,
          })
        : null) ||
      (RK
        ? getCurrentDoc({
            docSet: "DOC1",
            docType: "ADDENDUM",
            jurisdictionType: "REGION",
            jurisdictionKey: RK,
          })
        : null) ||
      getCurrentDoc({
        docSet: "DOC1",
        docType: "ADDENDUM",
        jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT",
        jurisdictionKey: "ROW",
      });

    const acceptedCombinedText = String(acc.combinedText || "");
    const acceptedCombinedSha = sha256Hex(acceptedCombinedText);

    const currentGlobalText = String(currentGlobal?.content || "");
    const currentAddendumText = String(currentAddendum?.content || "");

    // IMPORTANT: choose a stable delimiter for "current combined".
    // Use the same delimiter your signup assembly uses.
    const DELIM = "\n\n";
    const currentCombinedText = [currentGlobalText, currentAddendumText].filter(Boolean).join(DELIM);
    const currentCombinedSha = sha256Hex(currentCombinedText);

    const diffCombined = renderFullDiff(acceptedCombinedText, currentCombinedText);

    return res.json({
      ok: true,
      acceptance: {
        id: acc.id,
        userId: acc.userId ?? null,
        emailAtAcceptance: acc.emailAtAcceptance ?? null,
        countryIso2: acc.countryIso2,
        regionKey: acc.regionKey ?? null,
        acceptedAt: acc.acceptedAt,
        combinedSha256_stored: acc.combinedSha256,
        combinedSha256_recomputed: acceptedCombinedSha,
      },
      current: {
        global: currentGlobal
          ? {
              id: currentGlobal.id,
              docSet: currentGlobal.docSet,
              docType: currentGlobal.docType,
              jurisdictionType: currentGlobal.jurisdictionType,
              jurisdictionKey: currentGlobal.jurisdictionKey,
              version: currentGlobal.version,
              sha256: currentGlobal.sha256,
              createdAt: currentGlobal.createdAt,
            }
          : null,
        addendum: currentAddendum
          ? {
              id: currentAddendum.id,
              docSet: currentAddendum.docSet,
              docType: currentAddendum.docType,
              jurisdictionType: currentAddendum.jurisdictionType,
              jurisdictionKey: currentAddendum.jurisdictionKey,
              version: currentAddendum.version,
              sha256: currentAddendum.sha256,
              createdAt: currentAddendum.createdAt,
            }
          : null,
        combinedSha256: currentCombinedSha,
      },
      diff: {
        combined: diffCombined,
        notes: {
          delimiter: DELIM,
          globalResolved: Boolean(currentGlobal),
          addendumResolved: Boolean(currentAddendum),
        },
      },
    });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Diff failed." });
  }
});

      setDiffOpen(true);
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(e?.message || "Diff failed.");

      setDiffLoading(false);
    }
  }

4) Operational behavior you get

If a user accepted older terms, you can immediately:

see what changed in Global and/or Addendum

demonstrate evidence of what they accepted (your stored combinedText)

show what the “current” terms are today

This is crucial for:

disputes

compliance reviews

enforcement decisions

migration/legal refresh cycles

Optional tightening (recommended next)

If you want the “current active doc” to be explicit rather than “latest created”, I’ll add:

legalDocTargets table: (docSet, docType, jurisdictionType, jurisdictionKey, activeDocId)

Admin UI sets active doc explicitly

Diff endpoint uses activeDocId deterministically

Say “Add targets table” if you want that next.

Add
Thought for 32s

Below is the targets table + “active doc” plumbing (backend + schema + frontend) so “current active doc” is explicit (not “latest created”). This is the deterministic foundation you want for legal ops, upgrades, and enforceability.

You will add:

legalDocTargets table (target → activeDocId)

Admin endpoints to get/list and set active (upsert)

Update Legal Docs minitab to show ACTIVE and allow Set Active

Update Diff vs Current endpoint to use targets.activeDocId (fallback to latest only if target missing)

0) Assumptions (aligned with your stack)

DB: SQLite + Drizzle

Existing table: legalDocuments (with id, docSet, docType, jurisdictionType, jurisdictionKey, version, sha256, content, createdAt)

Admin auth middleware: requireAdmin

If your timestamp pattern differs (e.g., mode: "timestamp" vs timestamp_ms), adjust only the updatedAt column mode.

1) Schema: add legalDocTargets
1.1 shared/schema.ts (ADD TABLE)
// FILE: /shared/schema.ts
// ADD these imports near the top with other drizzle sqlite imports
import { sqliteTable, text, integer, index, uniqueIndex } from "drizzle-orm/sqlite-core";

// Ensure legalDocuments is already defined above.
// Then ADD this table definition (place near legalDocuments for clarity).

export const legalDocTargets = sqliteTable(
  "legal_doc_targets",
  {
    id: integer("id").primaryKey({ autoIncrement: true }),

    // Which doc family this target belongs to (DOC1 for your signup terms)
    docSet: text("doc_set").notNull(), // "DOC1"
    docType: text("doc_type").notNull(), // "GLOBAL_MASTER" | "ADDENDUM"
    jurisdictionType: text("jurisdiction_type").notNull(), // "DEFAULT" | "COUNTRY" | "REGION"
    jurisdictionKey: text("jurisdiction_key").notNull(), // e.g., "GLOBAL" | "ROW" | "KE" | "SE_ASIA"

    // The explicitly active legalDocuments.id for this target
    activeDocId: integer("active_doc_id").notNull(),

    // Operational metadata
    updatedAt: integer("updated_at", { mode: "timestamp_ms" }).notNull(),
    updatedByAdminUserId: integer("updated_by_admin_user_id"), // nullable if you don't have admin user ids
    note: text("note"), // optional: "FTC update", "Kenya update", etc.
  },
  (t) => ({
    uqTarget: uniqueIndex("uq_legal_doc_target").on(t.docSet, t.docType, t.jurisdictionType, t.jurisdictionKey),
    idxActive: index("idx_legal_doc_target_active").on(t.activeDocId),
  })
);


After adding schema: run your usual migration/push (npm run db:push).

2) Backend: Targets router
2.1 server/routes/adminLegalDocTargets.ts (NEW FILE)
// FILE: /server/routes/adminLegalDocTargets.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import { and, desc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../db";
import { requireAdmin } from "../middleware/requireAdmin";
import { legalDocTargets, legalDocuments } from "../../shared/schema";

export const adminLegalDocTargetsRouter = Router();
adminLegalDocTargetsRouter.use(requireAdmin);

function normText(v: any) {
  return String(v || "").trim();
}

function validateTarget(body: any) {
  const docSet = normText(body.docSet);
  const docType = normText(body.docType);
  const jurisdictionType = normText(body.jurisdictionType);
  const jurisdictionKey = normText(body.jurisdictionKey);

  if (!docSet || !docType || !jurisdictionType || !jurisdictionKey) {
    throw new Error("docSet, docType, jurisdictionType, jurisdictionKey are required.");
  }
  return { docSet, docType, jurisdictionType, jurisdictionKey };
}

// GET /api/admin/legal-docs/targets/get?docSet=DOC1&docType=ADDENDUM&jurisdictionType=COUNTRY&jurisdictionKey=KE
adminLegalDocTargetsRouter.get("/get", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const docSet = normText(req.query.docSet);
    const docType = normText(req.query.docType);
    const jurisdictionType = normText(req.query.jurisdictionType);
    const jurisdictionKey = normText(req.query.jurisdictionKey);

    if (!docSet || !docType || !jurisdictionType || !jurisdictionKey) {
      return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "Missing target parameters." });
    }

    const row = db
      .select()
      .from(legalDocTargets)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(legalDocTargets.docSet, docSet),
          eq(legalDocTargets.docType, docType),
          eq(legalDocTargets.jurisdictionType, jurisdictionType),
          eq(legalDocTargets.jurisdictionKey, jurisdictionKey)
        ) as any
      )
      .get();

    return res.json({ ok: true, target: row || null });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Failed to get target." });
  }
});

// GET /api/admin/legal-docs/targets/list?docSet=DOC1&docType=ADDENDUM
adminLegalDocTargetsRouter.get("/list", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const docSet = normText(req.query.docSet);
    const docType = normText(req.query.docType);

    const where: any[] = [];
    if (docSet) where.push(eq(legalDocTargets.docSet, docSet));
    if (docType) where.push(eq(legalDocTargets.docType, docType));

    const rows = db
      .select()
      .from(legalDocTargets)
      .where(where.length ? (and(...(where as any)) as any) : undefined)
      .orderBy(desc(legalDocTargets.updatedAt))
      .limit(500)
      .all();

    return res.json({ ok: true, rows });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Failed to list targets." });
  }
});

// POST /api/admin/legal-docs/targets/set-active
// body: { docSet, docType, jurisdictionType, jurisdictionKey, activeDocId, note? }
adminLegalDocTargetsRouter.post("/set-active", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const body = req.body || {};
    const { docSet, docType, jurisdictionType, jurisdictionKey } = validateTarget(body);

    const activeDocId = Number(body.activeDocId);
    if (!Number.isFinite(activeDocId) || activeDocId <= 0) throw new Error("activeDocId must be a positive number.");

    // Ensure the document exists and matches target
    const doc = db
      .select({
        id: legalDocuments.id,
        docSet: legalDocuments.docSet,
        docType: legalDocuments.docType,
        jurisdictionType: legalDocuments.jurisdictionType,
        jurisdictionKey: legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey,
      })
      .from(legalDocuments)
      .where(eq(legalDocuments.id, activeDocId))
      .get();

    if (!doc) throw new Error("activeDocId does not exist in legalDocuments.");
    if (
      doc.docSet !== docSet ||
      doc.docType !== docType ||
      doc.jurisdictionType !== jurisdictionType ||
      doc.jurisdictionKey !== jurisdictionKey
    ) {
      throw new Error("activeDocId document does not match the specified target.");
    }

    const now = Date.now();
    const note = body.note != null ? String(body.note) : null;

    // If your auth middleware exposes admin identity, you can set this.
    // Otherwise it remains null.
    const adminUserId = (req as any)?.user?.id ? Number((req as any).user.id) : null;

    // Upsert into legal_doc_targets
    db.insert(legalDocTargets)
      .values({
        docSet,
        docType,
        jurisdictionType,
        jurisdictionKey,
        activeDocId,
        updatedAt: new Date(now),
        updatedByAdminUserId: adminUserId,
        note,
      } as any)
      .onConflictDoUpdate({
        target: [legalDocTargets.docSet, legalDocTargets.docType, legalDocTargets.jurisdictionType, legalDocTargets.jurisdictionKey],
        set: {
          activeDocId,
          updatedAt: new Date(now) as any,
          updatedByAdminUserId: adminUserId,
          note,
        },
      })
      .run();

    const saved = db
      .select()
      .from(legalDocTargets)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(legalDocTargets.docSet, docSet),
          eq(legalDocTargets.docType, docType),
          eq(legalDocTargets.jurisdictionType, jurisdictionType),
          eq(legalDocTargets.jurisdictionKey, jurisdictionKey)
        ) as any
      )
      .get();

    return res.json({ ok: true, target: saved });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Failed to set active target." });
  }
});

3) Backend: Update “Diff vs Current” to use targets (deterministic)
Patch server/routes/adminLegalAcceptances.ts

Inside your existing /:id/diff-current handler, replace the “current doc resolution” logic with this.

Add import if missing:

import { legalDocTargets, legalDocuments } from "../../shared/schema";


Then replace the “getCurrentDoc” helper and resolution block with:

// Deterministic: current doc = legal_doc_targets.active_doc_id
const getActiveDocByTarget = (target: {
  docSet: string;
  docType: string;
  jurisdictionType: string;
  jurisdictionKey: string;
}) => {
  const t = db
    .select()
    .from(legalDocTargets)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(legalDocTargets.docSet, target.docSet),
        eq(legalDocTargets.docType, target.docType),
        eq(legalDocTargets.jurisdictionType, target.jurisdictionType),
        eq(legalDocTargets.jurisdictionKey, target.jurisdictionKey)
      ) as any
    )
    .get();

  if (!t) return { target: null, doc: null, mode: "MISSING_TARGET" as const };

  const doc = db
    .select({
      id: legalDocuments.id,
      docSet: legalDocuments.docSet,
      docType: legalDocuments.docType,
      jurisdictionType: legalDocuments.jurisdictionType,
      jurisdictionKey: legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey,
      version: legalDocuments.version,
      sha256: legalDocuments.sha256,
      content: legalDocuments.content,
      createdAt: legalDocuments.createdAt,
    })
    .from(legalDocuments)
    .where(eq(legalDocuments.id, t.activeDocId))
    .get();

  if (!doc) return { target: t, doc: null, mode: "BROKEN_TARGET" as const };

  return { target: t, doc, mode: "ACTIVE_TARGET" as const };
};

// Optional safety fallback if target missing: latest doc for that target.
// Keeps the diff usable while you are rolling out targets across jurisdictions.
const getLatestDocFallback = (target: {
  docSet: string;
  docType: string;
  jurisdictionType: string;
  jurisdictionKey: string;
}) => {
  const doc = db
    .select({
      id: legalDocuments.id,
      docSet: legalDocuments.docSet,
      docType: legalDocuments.docType,
      jurisdictionType: legalDocuments.jurisdictionType,
      jurisdictionKey: legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey,
      version: legalDocuments.version,
      sha256: legalDocuments.sha256,
      content: legalDocuments.content,
      createdAt: legalDocuments.createdAt,
    })
    .from(legalDocuments)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(legalDocuments.docSet, target.docSet),
        eq(legalDocuments.docType, target.docType),
        eq(legalDocuments.jurisdictionType, target.jurisdictionType),
        eq(legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey, target.jurisdictionKey)
      ) as any
    )
    .orderBy(desc(legalDocuments.createdAt))
    .limit(1)
    .get();

  return doc || null;
};

// Current Global (usually DEFAULT/GLOBAL)
const globalRes = getActiveDocByTarget({
  docSet: "DOC1",
  docType: "GLOBAL_MASTER",
  jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT",
  jurisdictionKey: "GLOBAL",
});

const currentGlobal =
  globalRes.doc ||
  getLatestDocFallback({
    docSet: "DOC1",
    docType: "GLOBAL_MASTER",
    jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT",
    jurisdictionKey: "GLOBAL",
  });

// Current Addendum: COUNTRY/ISO2 > REGION/RK > DEFAULT/ROW (targets first; fallback if missing)
const ISO2 = String(acc.countryIso2 || "").toUpperCase();
const RK = acc.regionKey ? String(acc.regionKey) : null;

let addRes:
  | { target: any; doc: any; mode: string; chosenTarget: any }
  | { target: any; doc: any; mode: string; chosenTarget: any };

let currentAddendum: any = null;
let addendumMode = "ACTIVE_TARGET";
let chosenAddTarget: any = null;

const tryTargets = [
  ISO2
    ? { docSet: "DOC1", docType: "ADDENDUM", jurisdictionType: "COUNTRY", jurisdictionKey: ISO2 }
    : null,
  RK
    ? { docSet: "DOC1", docType: "ADDENDUM", jurisdictionType: "REGION", jurisdictionKey: RK }
    : null,
  { docSet: "DOC1", docType: "ADDENDUM", jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT", jurisdictionKey: "ROW" },
].filter(Boolean) as any[];

for (const t of tryTargets) {
  const r = getActiveDocByTarget(t);
  if (r.doc) {
    currentAddendum = r.doc;
    addendumMode = r.mode;
    chosenAddTarget = t;
    break;
  }
}

if (!currentAddendum) {
  // fallback latest doc in precedence order
  for (const t of tryTargets) {
    const doc = getLatestDocFallback(t);
    if (doc) {
      currentAddendum = doc;
      addendumMode = "FALLBACK_LATEST";
      chosenAddTarget = t;
      break;
    }
  }
}


Then when returning JSON, replace your current.global and current.addendum objects to include target mode for operator clarity:

current: {
  global: currentGlobal
    ? {
        id: currentGlobal.id,
        docSet: currentGlobal.docSet,
        docType: currentGlobal.docType,
        jurisdictionType: currentGlobal.jurisdictionType,
        jurisdictionKey: currentGlobal.jurisdictionKey,
        version: currentGlobal.version,
        sha256: currentGlobal.sha256,
        createdAt: currentGlobal.createdAt,
        resolveMode: globalRes.mode, // ACTIVE_TARGET / MISSING_TARGET / BROKEN_TARGET
      }
    : null,
  addendum: currentAddendum
    ? {
        id: currentAddendum.id,
        docSet: currentAddendum.docSet,
        docType: currentAddendum.docType,
        jurisdictionType: currentAddendum.jurisdictionType,
        jurisdictionKey: currentAddendum.jurisdictionKey,
        version: currentAddendum.version,
        sha256: currentAddendum.sha256,
        createdAt: currentAddendum.createdAt,
        resolveMode: addendumMode, // ACTIVE_TARGET or FALLBACK_LATEST
        chosenTarget: chosenAddTarget,
      }
    : null,
  combinedSha256: currentCombinedSha,
},


This makes “current active” legally defensible and audit-friendly.

  const [activeDocId, setActiveDocId] = useState<number | null>(null);

Add helper to load the target’s active doc:

  async function loadActiveTarget() {
    setActiveLoading(true);
    try {
      const qs = new URLSearchParams({
        docSet,
        docType,
        jurisdictionType,
        jurisdictionKey,
      });
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/legal-docs/targets/get?${qs.toString()}`);
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!data?.ok) throw new Error(data?.error || "Failed to load active target.");
      setActiveDocId(data.target?.activeDocId ? Number(data.target.activeDocId) : null);
    } catch {
      setActiveDocId(null);
    } finally {
      setActiveLoading(false);
    }
  }

          docType,
          jurisdictionType,
          jurisdictionKey,
          activeDocId: docId,
          note: "Set via AdminLegalDocs",
        }),
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (!data?.ok) throw new Error(data?.error || "Failed to set active doc.");
      setActiveDocId(Number(data.target.activeDocId));
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(e?.message || "Failed to set active doc.");
    } finally {
      setSetActiveLoadingId(null);
    }
  }

5) Operational requirement satisfied: “load/replace future documents”

Upload new versions anytime (your legalDocuments grows as an immutable history)

Choose the active version per target (country/region/global)

Diff “accepted vs current active” deterministically

Change active versions as law shifts, without breaking your audit trail

6) What to do immediately after adding this

Create targets for the initial set you care about (at minimum):

DOC1 / GLOBAL_MASTER / DEFAULT / GLOBAL

DOC1 / ADDENDUM / DEFAULT / ROW

plus any country addenda you have (e.g., COUNTRY/US, COUNTRY/KE, etc.)

Use Legal Docs UI to click Set Active for each target once.

Now your “Diff vs Current” is legally crisp.

If you want, I can also add an Admin “Target Coverage” panel that lists which ISO2/regions have (a) docs present and (b) targets set, and highlights gaps before you onboard those jurisdictions.

Do
Thought for 1m 0s

Done. Below is the Admin “Target Coverage” mini-tab (backend + frontend) that:

Computes coverage for DOC1:

Global Master (DEFAULT/GLOBAL)

Addendum Default (DEFAULT/ROW)

Addendum COUNTRY/{ISO2}

Addendum REGION/{regionKey}

Highlights gaps:

MISSING_DOC (no legalDocuments exist for that target)

MISSING_TARGET (docs exist but no active target set in legal_doc_targets)

BROKEN_TARGET (target exists but points to missing doc)

OK (target + doc are in good state)

Provides actions:

Open Target in Legal Docs (prefill selector)

Set Active → Latest (one click) when applicable

Open Active Doc and Open Latest Doc

This works with the legal_doc_targets table you just added.

1) Backend: Coverage endpoint
1.1 Create server/routes/adminLegalDocCoverage.ts
// FILE: /server/routes/adminLegalDocCoverage.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import { and, desc, eq, inArray } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../db";
import { requireAdmin } from "../middleware/requireAdmin";
import { legalDocuments, legalDocTargets } from "../../shared/schema";

export const adminLegalDocCoverageRouter = Router();
adminLegalDocCoverageRouter.use(requireAdmin);

function splitList(raw: any): string[] {
  const s = String(raw || "").trim();
  if (!s) return [];
  return s
    .split(/[\s,;]+/g)
    .map((x) => x.trim())
    .filter(Boolean);
}

function toUpper2(x: string) {
  const s = String(x || "").trim().toUpperCase();
  return /^[A-Z]{2}$/.test(s) ? s : null;
}

function createdAtMs(v: any): number {
  if (!v) return 0;
  if (v instanceof Date) return v.getTime();
  const n = Number(v);
  return Number.isFinite(n) ? n : 0;
}

type DocLite = {
  id: number;
  docSet: string;
  docType: string;
  jurisdictionType: string;
  jurisdictionKey: string;
  version: string;
  sha256: string;
  createdAt: any;
};

type TargetLite = {
  id: number;
  docSet: string;
  docType: string;
  jurisdictionType: string;
  jurisdictionKey: string;
  activeDocId: number;
  updatedAt: any;
  note: string | null;
};

const DEFAULT_EXPECTED_ISO2 = [
  "US","AE","IN","KE","NG","BD","TH","PH","SG","HK","CN",
  "BR","AR","CO","MX",
  "ET","GH","AO","ZA","UG","TZ",
  "RU","UA","GB"
];

const DEFAULT_EXPECTED_REGIONS = [
  "SE_ASIA",
  "SOUTH_AMERICA",
  "WEST_EUROPE",
  "EAST_EUROPE",
  "MIDDLE_EAST",
  "WEST_AFRICA",
  "SOUTHERN_AFRICA",
  "CENTRAL_AFRICA",
  "NORTH_AFRICA",
  "CENTRAL_AMERICA",
  "NORTH_AMERICA",
  "CENTRAL_ASIA",
  "INDIAN_OCEAN_ISLANDS",
  "PACIFIC_ISLANDS",
  "AUSTRALASIA",
  "JAPAN",
  "SOUTH_KOREA",
  "PAKISTAN"
];

// GET /api/admin/legal-docs/coverage?docSet=DOC1&expectedIso2=US,KE&expectedRegions=SE_ASIA,MIDDLE_EAST
adminLegalDocCoverageRouter.get("/", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const docSet = String(req.query.docSet || "DOC1").trim() || "DOC1";

    // Expected inputs (optional). If not provided, use defaults.
    const expectedIso2Raw = splitList(req.query.expectedIso2);
    const expectedRegionsRaw = splitList(req.query.expectedRegions);

    const expectedIso2 =
      expectedIso2Raw.length > 0
        ? Array.from(new Set(expectedIso2Raw.map(toUpper2).filter(Boolean) as string[]))
        : DEFAULT_EXPECTED_ISO2.slice();

    const expectedRegions =
      expectedRegionsRaw.length > 0
        ? Array.from(new Set(expectedRegionsRaw.map((x) => String(x).trim()).filter(Boolean)))
        : DEFAULT_EXPECTED_REGIONS.slice();

    // Pull docs (DOC1: GLOBAL_MASTER + ADDENDUM)
    const docs = db
      .select({
        id: legalDocuments.id,
        docSet: legalDocuments.docSet,
        docType: legalDocuments.docType,
        jurisdictionType: legalDocuments.jurisdictionType,
        jurisdictionKey: legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey,
        version: legalDocuments.version,
        sha256: legalDocuments.sha256,
        createdAt: legalDocuments.createdAt,
      })
      .from(legalDocuments)
      .where(
        and(
          eq(legalDocuments.docSet, docSet),
          inArray(legalDocuments.docType, ["GLOBAL_MASTER", "ADDENDUM"])
        ) as any
      )
      .orderBy(desc(legalDocuments.createdAt))
      .limit(20000)
      .all() as unknown as DocLite[];

    // Pull targets for DOC1
    const targets = db
      .select({
        id: legalDocTargets.id,
        docSet: legalDocTargets.docSet,
        docType: legalDocTargets.docType,
        jurisdictionType: legalDocTargets.jurisdictionType,
        jurisdictionKey: legalDocTargets.jurisdictionKey,
        activeDocId: legalDocTargets.activeDocId,
        updatedAt: legalDocTargets.updatedAt,
        note: legalDocTargets.note,
      })
      .from(legalDocTargets)
      .where(eq(legalDocTargets.docSet, docSet))
      .limit(5000)
      .all() as unknown as TargetLite[];

    // Build doc map by id
    const docById = new Map<number, DocLite>();
    for (const d of docs) docById.set(Number(d.id), d);

    // Latest doc per (docType, jurisdictionType, jurisdictionKey)
    const latestByKey = new Map<string, DocLite>();
    const keyOf = (docType: string, jt: string, jk: string) => `${docType}|${jt}|${jk}`;

    for (const d of docs) {
      const k = keyOf(d.docType, d.jurisdictionType, d.jurisdictionKey);
      const prev = latestByKey.get(k);
      if (!prev || createdAtMs(d.createdAt) > createdAtMs(prev.createdAt)) {
        latestByKey.set(k, d);
      }
    }

    // Targets by (docType, jt, jk)
    const targetByKey = new Map<string, TargetLite>();
    for (const t of targets) {
      const k = keyOf(t.docType, t.jurisdictionType, t.jurisdictionKey);
      targetByKey.set(k, t);
    }

    // Discover keys present in docs/targets
    const discoveredIso2 = new Set<string>();
    const discoveredRegions = new Set<string>();

    for (const d of docs) {
      if (d.docType !== "ADDENDUM") continue;
      if (d.jurisdictionType === "COUNTRY") discoveredIso2.add(String(d.jurisdictionKey).toUpperCase());
      if (d.jurisdictionType === "REGION") discoveredRegions.add(String(d.jurisdictionKey));
    }
    for (const t of targets) {
      if (t.docType !== "ADDENDUM") continue;
      if (t.jurisdictionType === "COUNTRY") discoveredIso2.add(String(t.jurisdictionKey).toUpperCase());
      if (t.jurisdictionType === "REGION") discoveredRegions.add(String(t.jurisdictionKey));
    }

    // Union expected + discovered
    const allIso2 = Array.from(new Set([...expectedIso2, ...Array.from(discoveredIso2)])).sort();
    const allRegions = Array.from(new Set([...expectedRegions, ...Array.from(discoveredRegions)])).sort();

    // Helper to build a coverage row
    const buildRow = (docType: string, jt: string, jk: string) => {
      const k = keyOf(docType, jt, jk);
      const latest = latestByKey.get(k) || null;
      const target = targetByKey.get(k) || null;
      const activeDoc = target ? (docById.get(Number(target.activeDocId)) || null) : null;

      let status: "OK" | "MISSING_DOC" | "MISSING_TARGET" | "BROKEN_TARGET";
      if (!latest && !target) status = "MISSING_DOC";
      else if (latest && !target) status = "MISSING_TARGET";
      else if (target && !activeDoc) status = "BROKEN_TARGET";
      else status = "OK";

      return {
        target: { docSet, docType, jurisdictionType: jt, jurisdictionKey: jk },
        status,
        latest,
        targetRow: target,
        activeDoc,
      };
    };

    // Global / default coverage
    const global = buildRow("GLOBAL_MASTER", "DEFAULT", "GLOBAL");
    const addendumDefault = buildRow("ADDENDUM", "DEFAULT", "ROW");

    // Country and region coverage
    const countries = allIso2.map((iso2) => buildRow("ADDENDUM", "COUNTRY", iso2));
    const regions = allRegions.map((rk) => buildRow("ADDENDUM", "REGION", rk));

    // Summary
    const allRows = [global, addendumDefault, ...countries, ...regions];
    const summary = {
      total: allRows.length,
      ok: allRows.filter((r) => r.status === "OK").length,
      missingDoc: allRows.filter((r) => r.status === "MISSING_DOC").length,
      missingTarget: allRows.filter((r) => r.status === "MISSING_TARGET").length,
      brokenTarget: allRows.filter((r) => r.status === "BROKEN_TARGET").length,
      countries: countries.length,
      regions: regions.length,
    };

    return res.json({
      ok: true,
      docSet,
      expected: { iso2: expectedIso2, regions: expectedRegions },
      discovered: { iso2: Array.from(discoveredIso2).sort(), regions: Array.from(discoveredRegions).sort() },
      summary,
      global,
      addendumDefault,
      countries,
      regions,
    });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Coverage failed." });
  }
});

1.2 Mount route in server/index.ts
// FILE: /server/index.ts
import { adminLegalDocCoverageRouter } from "./routes/adminLegalDocCoverage";

// ...
app.use("/api/admin/legal-docs/coverage", adminLegalDocCoverageRouter);

2) Frontend: Admin mini-tab “Legal Coverage”
2.1 Create client/src/pages/AdminLegalCoverage.tsx
// FILE: /client/src/pages/AdminLegalCoverage.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { dispatchAdminNavigate, setLegalDocsPrefill } from "../lib/adminDeepLink";

type DocLite = {
  id: number;
  docSet: string;
  docType: string;
  jurisdictionType: string;
  jurisdictionKey: string;
  version: string;
  sha256: string;
  createdAt: any;
};

type TargetLite = {
  id: number;
  docSet: string;
  docType: string;
  jurisdictionType: string;
  jurisdictionKey: string;
  activeDocId: number;
  updatedAt: any;
  note: string | null;
};

type CoverageRow = {
  target: { docSet: string; docType: string; jurisdictionType: string; jurisdictionKey: string };
  status: "OK" | "MISSING_DOC" | "MISSING_TARGET" | "BROKEN_TARGET";
  latest: DocLite | null;
  targetRow: TargetLite | null;
  activeDoc: DocLite | null;
};

export default function AdminLegalCoverage() {
  const [docSet, setDocSet] = useState("DOC1");

  const [expectedIso2Text, setExpectedIso2Text] = useState(() =>
    (localStorage.getItem("legalCoverage_expectedIso2") || "US, AE, IN, KE, NG, BD, TH, PH, SG, HK, CN, BR, AR, CO, MX, ET, GH, AO, ZA, UG, TZ, RU, UA, GB")
  );
  const [expectedRegionsText, setExpectedRegionsText] = useState(() =>
    (localStorage.getItem("legalCoverage_expectedRegions") || "SE_ASIA, SOUTH_AMERICA, WEST_EUROPE, EAST_EUROPE, MIDDLE_EAST, WEST_AFRICA, SOUTHERN_AFRICA")
  );

  const [data, setData] = useState<any>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  const expectedIso2 = useMemo(() => expectedIso2Text, [expectedIso2Text]);
  const expectedRegions = useMemo(() => expectedRegionsText, [expectedRegionsText]);

  async function load() {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      localStorage.setItem("legalCoverage_expectedIso2", expectedIso2Text);
      localStorage.setItem("legalCoverage_expectedRegions", expectedRegionsText);

      const qs = new URLSearchParams({
        docSet,
        expectedIso2,
        expectedRegions,
      });

      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/legal-docs/coverage?${qs.toString()}`);
      const j = await res.json();
      if (!j?.ok) throw new Error(j?.error || "Failed to load coverage.");
      setData(j);
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(e?.message || "Coverage load failed.");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    load();
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [docSet]);

  function goLegalDocsTarget(target: CoverageRow["target"], openDocumentId?: number | null) {
    setLegalDocsPrefill({
      docSet: target.docSet,
      docType: target.docType as any,
      jurisdictionType: target.jurisdictionType as any,
      jurisdictionKey: target.jurisdictionKey,
      openDocumentId: openDocumentId ?? null,
      returnTo: null,
    });

    dispatchAdminNavigate({
      topTabKey: "systemConfig",
      systemConfigTabKey: "legalDocs",
    });
  }

  async function setActiveToLatest(row: CoverageRow) {
    if (!row.latest?.id) return;
    const t = row.target;

    const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/legal-docs/targets/set-active`, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        docSet: t.docSet,
        docType: t.docType,
        jurisdictionType: t.jurisdictionType,
        jurisdictionKey: t.jurisdictionKey,
        activeDocId: Number(row.latest.id),
        note: "Set Active to Latest via Coverage",
      }),
    });
    const j = await res.json();
    if (!j?.ok) {
      alert(j?.error || "Failed to set active.");
      return;
    }
    await load();
  }

  const summary = data?.summary;

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 12 }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "baseline" }}>
        <div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 18, fontWeight: 700 }}>Legal Coverage</div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>
            Coverage of docs and active targets per jurisdiction. Use this before onboarding new countries/regions.
          </div>
        </div>
        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8 }}>
          <button style={btn} onClick={load} disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? "Refreshing..." : "Refresh"}
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div style={{ ...card, display: "grid", gap: 10, gridTemplateColumns: "220px 1fr 1fr" }}>
        <Field label="Doc Set">
          <select value={docSet} onChange={(e) => setDocSet(e.target.value)} style={input}>
            {["DOC1"].map((x) => (
              <option key={x} value={x}>{x}</option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </Field>

        <Field label="Expected Countries (ISO2, comma/space separated)">
          <textarea
            value={expectedIso2Text}
            onChange={(e) => setExpectedIso2Text(e.target.value)}
            style={{ ...input, height: 74, resize: "vertical" }}
          />
        </Field>

        <Field label="Expected Regions (regionKey, comma/space separated)">
          <textarea
            value={expectedRegionsText}
            onChange={(e) => setExpectedRegionsText(e.target.value)}
            style={{ ...input, height: 74, resize: "vertical" }}
          />
        </Field>
      </div>

      {summary && (
        <div style={{ ...card, display: "grid", gridTemplateColumns: "repeat(7, 1fr)", gap: 10 }}>
          <Stat label="Total Targets" value={summary.total} />
          <Stat label="OK" value={summary.ok} />
          <Stat label="Missing Doc" value={summary.missingDoc} />
          <Stat label="Missing Target" value={summary.missingTarget} />
          <Stat label="Broken Target" value={summary.brokenTarget} />
          <Stat label="Countries" value={summary.countries} />
          <Stat label="Regions" value={summary.regions} />
        </div>
      )}

      {data && (
        <>
          <div style={card}>
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 8 }}>Core Targets</div>
            <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 8 }}>
              <CoverageRowView row={data.global as CoverageRow} onGo={goLegalDocsTarget} onSetActive={setActiveToLatest} />
              <CoverageRowView row={data.addendumDefault as CoverageRow} onGo={goLegalDocsTarget} onSetActive={setActiveToLatest} />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={card}>
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 8 }}>Countries</div>
            <div style={{ maxHeight: 420, overflow: "auto", border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.10)", borderRadius: 10 }}>
              <CoverageHeader />
              {(data.countries as CoverageRow[]).map((row) => (
                <CoverageRowLine key={`C:${row.target.jurisdictionKey}`} row={row} onGo={goLegalDocsTarget} onSetActive={setActiveToLatest} />
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>

          <div style={card}>
            <div style={{ fontWeight: 700, marginBottom: 8 }}>Regions</div>
            <div style={{ maxHeight: 420, overflow: "auto", border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.10)", borderRadius: 10 }}>
              <CoverageHeader />
              {(data.regions as CoverageRow[]).map((row) => (
                <CoverageRowLine key={`R:${row.target.jurisdictionKey}`} row={row} onGo={goLegalDocsTarget} onSetActive={setActiveToLatest} />
              ))}
            </div>
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

function CoverageHeader() {
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "grid",
        gridTemplateColumns: "90px 120px 260px 180px 180px 280px",
        padding: 10,
        background: "rgba(0,0,0,0.04)",
        fontSize: 12,
        fontWeight: 700,
      }}
    >
      <div>Status</div>
      <div>Type</div>
      <div>Target</div>
      <div>Latest Doc</div>
      <div>Active Doc</div>
      <div>Actions</div>
    </div>
  );
}

function CoverageRowView(props: {
  row: CoverageRow;
  onGo: (target: CoverageRow["target"], openDocumentId?: number | null) => void;
  onSetActive: (row: CoverageRow) => void;
}) {
  const r = props.row;
  return (
    <div style={{ border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.10)", borderRadius: 10, padding: 10 }}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 10, alignItems: "center" }}>
        <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 4 }}>
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>
            {r.target.docType} / {r.target.jurisdictionType}/{r.target.jurisdictionKey}{" "}
            <StatusBadge status={r.status} />
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.8 }}>
            Latest: {r.latest ? `#${r.latest.id} v${r.latest.version}` : "—"} | Active: {r.activeDoc ? `#${r.activeDoc.id} v${r.activeDoc.version}` : "—"}
          </div>
        </div>

        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
          <button style={btnSmall} onClick={() => props.onGo(r.target, null)}>Open Target</button>
          {r.latest?.id && <button style={btnSmall} onClick={() => props.onGo(r.target, r.latest!.id)}>Open Latest</button>}
          {r.activeDoc?.id && <button style={btnSmall} onClick={() => props.onGo(r.target, r.activeDoc!.id)}>Open Active</button>}
          {(r.status === "MISSING_TARGET" || r.status === "BROKEN_TARGET") && r.latest?.id && (
            <button style={btnSmall} onClick={() => props.onSetActive(r)}>Set Active → Latest</button>
          )}
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function CoverageRowLine(props: {
  row: CoverageRow;
  onGo: (target: CoverageRow["target"], openDocumentId?: number | null) => void;
  onSetActive: (row: CoverageRow) => void;
}) {
  const r = props.row;

  const targetLabel = `${r.target.jurisdictionType}/${r.target.jurisdictionKey}`;
  const latestLabel = r.latest ? `#${r.latest.id} v${r.latest.version}` : "—";
  const activeLabel = r.activeDoc ? `#${r.activeDoc.id} v${r.activeDoc.version}` : "—";

  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "grid",
        gridTemplateColumns: "90px 120px 260px 180px 180px 280px",
        padding: 10,
        borderTop: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.08)",
        fontSize: 12,
        alignItems: "center",
      }}
    >
      <div><StatusBadge status={r.status} /></div>
      <div>{r.target.docType}</div>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "monospace" }}>{targetLabel}</div>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "monospace" }}>{latestLabel}</div>
      <div style={{ fontFamily: "monospace" }}>{activeLabel}</div>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, flexWrap: "wrap" }}>
        <button style={btnSmall} onClick={() => props.onGo(r.target, null)}>Open Target</button>
        {r.latest?.id && <button style={btnSmall} onClick={() => props.onGo(r.target, r.latest!.id)}>Open Latest</button>}
        {r.activeDoc?.id && <button style={btnSmall} onClick={() => props.onGo(r.target, r.activeDoc!.id)}>Open Active</button>}
        {(r.status === "MISSING_TARGET" || r.status === "BROKEN_TARGET") && r.latest?.id && (
          <button style={btnSmall} onClick={() => props.onSetActive(r)}>Set Active → Latest</button>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

function StatusBadge(props: { status: CoverageRow["status"] }) {
  const s = props.status;
  return (
    <span
      style={{
        display: "inline-block",
        padding: "2px 8px",
        borderRadius: 999,
        border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.18)",
        fontSize: 11,
        fontWeight: 700,
      }}
      title={s}
    >
      {s}
    </span>
  );
}

function Stat(props: { label: string; value: any }) {
  return (
    <div style={{ border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.10)", borderRadius: 10, padding: 10 }}>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>{props.label}</div>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 16, fontWeight: 800 }}>{String(props.value)}</div>
    </div>
  );
}

function Field(props: { label: string; children: React.ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <label style={{ display: "grid", gap: 6 }}>
      <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.8 }}>{props.label}</div>
      {props.children}
    </label>
  );
}

const card: React.CSSProperties = {
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.12)",
  borderRadius: 12,
  padding: 12,
  background: "white",
};

const input: React.CSSProperties = {
  width: "100%",
  padding: "10px 12px",
  borderRadius: 10,
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.2)",
  fontSize: 12,
};

const btn: React.CSSProperties = {
  padding: "10px 12px",
  borderRadius: 10,
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.25)",
  background: "white",
  cursor: "pointer",
  fontWeight: 700,
  fontSize: 12,
};

const btnSmall: React.CSSProperties = {
  padding: "8px 10px",
  borderRadius: 10,
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.2)",
  background: "white",
  cursor: "pointer",
  fontSize: 12,
};

3.3 Render

4) Optional: add coverage deep-linking via admin:navigate

If you want the event bus to be able to open this panel, ensure your AdminDashboard’s admin:navigate listener can map:

topTabKey: "systemConfig"

systemConfigTabKey: "legalCoverage"

What this enables immediately

A single page that tells you, per jurisdiction, whether you can legally onboard:

Do we have docs?

Is “active” explicitly set?

Are targets broken?

One-click remediation for the common gap: docs exist but no target set.

If you want the next upgrade, say “Add onboarding gate” and I’ll add a backend function that refuses signup for jurisdictions where ADDENDUM coverage is not OK (or allows a strict fallback policy you control in System Config).

Add but let it be that I can turning on or off in the backend 
Thought for 58s

Below adds an onboarding jurisdiction coverage gate that you can turn on/off from the backend (Admin → System Config).

What it does when ENFORCED:

Blocks signup/legal-doc resolution for a country/region unless:

DOC1 / GLOBAL_MASTER / DEFAULT / GLOBAL has an active target set and that active doc exists, and

the resolved addendum target (COUNTRY → REGION → DEFAULT/ROW) has an active target set and that active doc exists.

When NOT enforced:

Never blocks; resolves by active target where present; otherwise falls back to latest doc for that target (and returns warnings).

This is the correct operational posture: you can run globally while you are still filling gaps, then flip enforcement on when ready.

1) Backend: System Config get/set (Admin toggle)
1.1 server/routes/adminSystemConfig.ts (NEW FILE)
// FILE: /server/routes/adminSystemConfig.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import { and, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../db";
import { requireAdmin } from "../middleware/requireAdmin";
import { systemConfig } from "../../shared/schema";

export const adminSystemConfigRouter = Router();
adminSystemConfigRouter.use(requireAdmin);

function normKey(x: any) {
  return String(x || "").trim();
}

// GET /api/admin/system-config/get?key=legal.coverage.enforce
adminSystemConfigRouter.get("/get", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const key = normKey(req.query.key);
    if (!key) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "key is required" });

    const row = db.select().from(systemConfig).where(eq(systemConfig.key, key)).get();
    return res.json({ ok: true, row: row || null });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Failed to get config." });
  }
});

// POST /api/admin/system-config/set
// body: { key: string, value: string }
adminSystemConfigRouter.post("/set", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const key = normKey(req.body?.key);
    const value = req.body?.value == null ? "" : String(req.body.value);

    if (!key) return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "key is required" });

    const now = Date.now();

    db.insert(systemConfig)
      .values({
        key,
        value,
        updatedAt: new Date(now) as any,
      } as any)
      .onConflictDoUpdate({
        target: [systemConfig.key],
        set: { value, updatedAt: new Date(now) as any },
      })
      .run();

    const saved = db.select().from(systemConfig).where(eq(systemConfig.key, key)).get();
    return res.json({ ok: true, row: saved });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Failed to set config." });
  }
});
