2) Backend: Coverage gate resolver (targets-first with fallback when off)
2.1 server/legal/coverageGate.ts (NEW FILE)
// FILE: /server/legal/coverageGate.ts
import { and, desc, eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../db";
import { legalDocuments, legalDocTargets } from "../../shared/schema";

export const CONFIG_KEYS = {
  LEGAL_COVERAGE_ENFORCE: "legal.coverage.enforce",
};

type TargetKey = {
  docSet: string;
  docType: "GLOBAL_MASTER" | "ADDENDUM";
  jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT" | "COUNTRY" | "REGION";
  jurisdictionKey: string;
};

type Doc = {
  id: number;
  docSet: string;
  docType: string;
  jurisdictionType: string;
  jurisdictionKey: string;
  version: string;
  sha256: string;
  content: string;
  createdAt: any;
};

type ResolveResult = {
  ok: boolean;
  enforce: boolean;
  blocked?: boolean;
  reason?: string;

  selected: {
    globalTarget: TargetKey;
    addendumTarget: TargetKey;
  };

  current: {
    global: { mode: "ACTIVE_TARGET" | "FALLBACK_LATEST" | "MISSING"; doc: Doc | null; activeDocId?: number | null };
    addendum: { mode: "ACTIVE_TARGET" | "FALLBACK_LATEST" | "MISSING"; doc: Doc | null; activeDocId?: number | null };
  };

  warnings: string[];
};

function normIso2(x: any) {
  return String(x || "").trim().toUpperCase();
}

function normRegion(x: any) {
  const s = String(x || "").trim();
  return s || null;
}

function getActiveDocForTarget(target: TargetKey): { activeDocId: number | null; doc: Doc | null } {
  const t = db
    .select()
    .from(legalDocTargets)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(legalDocTargets.docSet, target.docSet),
        eq(legalDocTargets.docType, target.docType),
        eq(legalDocTargets.jurisdictionType, target.jurisdictionType),
        eq(legalDocTargets.jurisdictionKey, target.jurisdictionKey)
      ) as any
    )
    .get();

  if (!t) return { activeDocId: null, doc: null };

  const doc = db
    .select({
      id: legalDocuments.id,
      docSet: legalDocuments.docSet,
      docType: legalDocuments.docType,
      jurisdictionType: legalDocuments.jurisdictionType,
      jurisdictionKey: legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey,
      version: legalDocuments.version,
      sha256: legalDocuments.sha256,
      content: legalDocuments.content,
      createdAt: legalDocuments.createdAt,
    })
    .from(legalDocuments)
    .where(eq(legalDocuments.id, t.activeDocId))
    .get() as any;

  return { activeDocId: Number(t.activeDocId), doc: doc || null };
}

function getLatestDocForTarget(target: TargetKey): Doc | null {
  const doc = db
    .select({
      id: legalDocuments.id,
      docSet: legalDocuments.docSet,
      docType: legalDocuments.docType,
      jurisdictionType: legalDocuments.jurisdictionType,
      jurisdictionKey: legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey,
      version: legalDocuments.version,
      sha256: legalDocuments.sha256,
      content: legalDocuments.content,
      createdAt: legalDocuments.createdAt,
    })
    .from(legalDocuments)
    .where(
      and(
        eq(legalDocuments.docSet, target.docSet),
        eq(legalDocuments.docType, target.docType),
        eq(legalDocuments.jurisdictionType, target.jurisdictionType),
        eq(legalDocuments.jurisdictionKey, target.jurisdictionKey)
      ) as any
    )
    .orderBy(desc(legalDocuments.createdAt))
    .limit(1)
    .get() as any;

  return doc || null;
}

export function resolveDoc1ByJurisdiction(args: {
  countryIso2: string;
  regionKey?: string | null;
  enforce: boolean;
}): ResolveResult {
  const ISO2 = normIso2(args.countryIso2);
  const RK = normRegion(args.regionKey);

  const warnings: string[] = [];

  // Global is always DEFAULT/GLOBAL for DOC1
  const globalTarget: TargetKey = {
    docSet: "DOC1",
    docType: "GLOBAL_MASTER",
    jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT",
    jurisdictionKey: "GLOBAL",
  };

  // Addendum precedence: COUNTRY -> REGION -> DEFAULT/ROW
  const addendumTargets: TargetKey[] = [
    ISO2
      ? { docSet: "DOC1", docType: "ADDENDUM", jurisdictionType: "COUNTRY", jurisdictionKey: ISO2 }
      : null,
    RK
      ? { docSet: "DOC1", docType: "ADDENDUM", jurisdictionType: "REGION", jurisdictionKey: RK }
      : null,
    { docSet: "DOC1", docType: "ADDENDUM", jurisdictionType: "DEFAULT", jurisdictionKey: "ROW" },
  ].filter(Boolean) as any[];

  // Resolve GLOBAL
  const gActive = getActiveDocForTarget(globalTarget);
  let globalMode: ResolveResult["current"]["global"]["mode"] = "ACTIVE_TARGET";
  let globalDoc: Doc | null = gActive.doc;

  if (!globalDoc) {
    if (args.enforce) {
      return {
        ok: true,
        enforce: true,
        blocked: true,
        reason: "GLOBAL_MASTER_ACTIVE_TARGET_MISSING",
        selected: { globalTarget, addendumTarget: addendumTargets[0] },
        current: {
          global: { mode: "MISSING", doc: null, activeDocId: gActive.activeDocId },
          addendum: { mode: "MISSING", doc: null },
        },
        warnings: [],
      };
    }

    const latest = getLatestDocForTarget(globalTarget);
    if (latest) {
      globalMode = "FALLBACK_LATEST";
      globalDoc = latest;
      warnings.push("GLOBAL_MASTER missing active target; using latest doc fallback.");
    } else {
      globalMode = "MISSING";
      globalDoc = null;
      warnings.push("GLOBAL_MASTER missing both active target and any doc content.");
    }
  }

  // Resolve ADDENDUM by precedence
  let chosenAddendumTarget: TargetKey = addendumTargets[addendumTargets.length - 1];
  let addMode: ResolveResult["current"]["addendum"]["mode"] = "MISSING";
  let addDoc: Doc | null = null;
  let addActiveDocId: number | null = null;

  for (const t of addendumTargets) {
    const aActive = getActiveDocForTarget(t);
    if (aActive.doc) {
      chosenAddendumTarget = t;
      addMode = "ACTIVE_TARGET";
      addDoc = aActive.doc;
      addActiveDocId = aActive.activeDocId;
      break;
    }

    // If enforce is ON, we do not skip to fallback-latest; we require an active target on the first *resolved* target
    // But we also allow precedence: if COUNTRY target is absent, we may proceed to REGION, then DEFAULT/ROW.
    // Meaning: you must have an active target for whichever target resolves (COUNTRY if present, else REGION if present, else DEFAULT/ROW).
    if (args.enforce) {
      // Continue to next precedence target, but do not use latest fallback.
      continue;
    }

    // Not enforced: can fallback to latest for this target if any docs exist
    const latest = getLatestDocForTarget(t);
    if (latest) {
      chosenAddendumTarget = t;
      addMode = "FALLBACK_LATEST";
      addDoc = latest;
      warnings.push(`ADDENDUM ${t.jurisdictionType}/${t.jurisdictionKey} missing active target; using latest doc fallback.`);
      break;
    }
  }

  if (!addDoc) {
    if (args.enforce) {
      return {
        ok: true,
        enforce: true,
        blocked: true,
        reason: "ADDENDUM_ACTIVE_TARGET_MISSING_FOR_RESOLVED_TARGET",
        selected: { globalTarget, addendumTarget: chosenAddendumTarget },
        current: {
          global: { mode: globalMode, doc: globalDoc, activeDocId: gActive.activeDocId },
          addendum: { mode: "MISSING", doc: null, activeDocId: null },
        },
        warnings: [],
      };
    }

    warnings.push("ADDENDUM missing active targets and no docs found in fallback search.");
  }

  return {
    ok: true,
    enforce: args.enforce,
    selected: { globalTarget, addendumTarget: chosenAddendumTarget },
    current: {
      global: { mode: globalMode, doc: globalDoc, activeDocId: gActive.activeDocId },
      addendum: { mode: addMode, doc: addDoc, activeDocId: addActiveDocId },
    },
    warnings,
  };
}

3) Backend: Public resolver endpoint for signup (the gate)

This is the clean integration point for your signup embed + renderer engine.

3.1 server/routes/legalDoc1Resolve.ts (NEW FILE)
// FILE: /server/routes/legalDoc1Resolve.ts
import { Router } from "express";
import { eq } from "drizzle-orm";
import { db } from "../db";
import { systemConfig } from "../../shared/schema";
import { resolveDoc1ByJurisdiction, CONFIG_KEYS } from "../legal/coverageGate";
import crypto from "crypto";

export const legalDoc1ResolveRouter = Router();

function getBoolConfig(key: string, defaultValue: boolean) {
  const row = db.select().from(systemConfig).where(eq(systemConfig.key, key)).get() as any;
  if (!row || row.value == null) return defaultValue;
  const v = String(row.value).trim().toLowerCase();
  if (["1", "true", "yes", "on"].includes(v)) return true;
  if (["0", "false", "no", "off"].includes(v)) return false;
  return defaultValue;
}

function sha256Hex(s: string) {
  return crypto.createHash("sha256").update(s, "utf8").digest("hex");
}

// GET /api/legal/doc1/resolve?countryIso2=KE&regionKey=EAST_AFRICA
legalDoc1ResolveRouter.get("/resolve", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const countryIso2 = String(req.query.countryIso2 || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    const regionKey = req.query.regionKey != null ? String(req.query.regionKey).trim() : null;

    if (!countryIso2 || countryIso2.length !== 2) {
      return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "countryIso2 must be 2-letter ISO code." });
    }

    const enforce = getBoolConfig(CONFIG_KEYS.LEGAL_COVERAGE_ENFORCE, false);

    const resolved = resolveDoc1ByJurisdiction({
      countryIso2,
      regionKey,
      enforce,
    });

    if (resolved.blocked) {
      return res.status(409).json({
        ok: false,
        code: "LEGAL_COVERAGE_BLOCKED",
        reason: resolved.reason,
        enforce: true,
        selected: resolved.selected,
        remediation: "Admin must set active targets (System Config → Legal Docs / Legal Coverage) for GLOBAL_MASTER DEFAULT/GLOBAL and resolved ADDENDUM target.",
      });
    }

    const globalText = String(resolved.current.global.doc?.content || "");
    const addendumText = String(resolved.current.addendum.doc?.content || "");

    const DELIM = "\n\n";
    const combinedText = [globalText, addendumText].filter(Boolean).join(DELIM);
    const combinedSha256 = sha256Hex(combinedText);

    return res.json({
      ok: true,
      enforce,
      selected: resolved.selected,
      current: {
        global: resolved.current.global.doc
          ? {
              id: resolved.current.global.doc.id,
              version: resolved.current.global.doc.version,
              sha256: resolved.current.global.doc.sha256,
              mode: resolved.current.global.mode,
              jurisdiction: `${resolved.current.global.doc.jurisdictionType}/${resolved.current.global.doc.jurisdictionKey}`,
            }
          : null,
        addendum: resolved.current.addendum.doc
          ? {
              id: resolved.current.addendum.doc.id,
              version: resolved.current.addendum.doc.version,
              sha256: resolved.current.addendum.doc.sha256,
              mode: resolved.current.addendum.mode,
              jurisdiction: `${resolved.current.addendum.doc.jurisdictionType}/${resolved.current.addendum.doc.jurisdictionKey}`,
            }
          : null,
        delimiter: DELIM,
        combinedSha256,
      },
      docs: {
        globalText,
        addendumText,
        combinedText,
      },
      warnings: resolved.warnings,
    });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Resolve failed." });
  }
});

4) Frontend: Add toggle controls to “Legal Coverage” minitab

Update the coverage minitab so you can turn enforcement on/off from backend UI.

Patch client/src/pages/AdminLegalCoverage.tsx
4.1 Add state + load functions (inside component)

Add these near the top:

  const [enforceGate, setEnforceGate] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [enforceLoading, setEnforceLoading] = useState(false);

  async function loadEnforceGate() {
    setEnforceLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/system-config/get?key=legal.coverage.enforce`);
      const j = await res.json();
      if (!j?.ok) throw new Error(j?.error || "Failed to load gate config.");
      const v = String(j.row?.value ?? "false").trim().toLowerCase();
      setEnforceGate(["1", "true", "yes", "on"].includes(v));
    } catch {
      setEnforceGate(false);
    } finally {
      setEnforceLoading(false);
    }
  }

  async function setEnforceGateValue(next: boolean) {
    setEnforceLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/system-config/set`, {
        method: "POST",
        headers: { "content-type": "application/json" },
        body: JSON.stringify({ key: "legal.coverage.enforce", value: next ? "true" : "false" }),
      });
      const j = await res.json();
      if (!j?.ok) throw new Error(j?.error || "Failed to set gate config.");
      setEnforceGate(next);
    } catch (e: any) {
      alert(e?.message || "Failed to set gate config.");
    } finally {
      setEnforceLoading(false);
    }
  }

4.2 Call on mount

Add to your existing useEffect that loads coverage, or add a new one:

  useEffect(() => {
    loadEnforceGate().catch(() => {});
  }, []);

4.3 Add UI controls (near the top header actions)

In the header area (where you have Refresh), add:

        <div style={{ display: "flex", gap: 8, alignItems: "center" }}>
          <span style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>
            Gate:{" "}
            <strong>{enforceLoading ? "…" : (enforceGate ? "ENFORCED" : "OFF")}</strong>
          </span>
          <button
            style={btn}
            disabled={enforceLoading}
            onClick={() => setEnforceGateValue(!enforceGate)}
            title="When enforced, signup/legal resolution is blocked unless active targets exist for the resolved jurisdiction."
          >
            {enforceLoading ? "Saving..." : (enforceGate ? "Disable Gate" : "Enable Gate")}
          </button>
          <button style={btn} onClick={load} disabled={loading}>
            {loading ? "Refreshing..." : "Refresh"}
          </button>
        </div>

5) How you integrate this into signup (now or later)

Your signup embed/renderer engine should call:

GET /api/legal/doc1/resolve?countryIso2=XX&regionKey=...

If it gets:

409 with code: LEGAL_COVERAGE_BLOCKED → show user a clean message:

“We are not yet available in your jurisdiction. Please check back later.”

This gives you a controlled rollout by country/region.

6) Operational checklist (what you must set before enforcing)

Before you flip legal.coverage.enforce = true, ensure targets are set in Legal Docs (or Coverage panel “Set Active → Latest”):

DOC1 / GLOBAL_MASTER / DEFAULT / GLOBAL → active doc id set

DOC1 / ADDENDUM / DEFAULT / ROW → active doc id set

For any priority country you want to onboard:

DOC1 / ADDENDUM / COUNTRY / {ISO2} → active doc id set

or, if you use regions for that onboarding, set REGION/{regionKey}.

Once those are covered, enforcement is safe.

If you want, next step is to add a “Signup Availability” check on the public site that calls this resolver and returns a simple green/red status for the selected jurisdiction (same gate logic, no doc payload).

Adf
Thought for 12s

Interpreting “Adf” as “Add”: here is the lightweight Signup Availability check (backend endpoint + frontend widget) that uses your coverage gate toggle (legal.coverage.enforce) but does not ship the full document payload.

1) Backend: add /availability to your existing DOC1 router
Patch server/routes/legalDoc1Resolve.ts

Add this route below your existing /resolve route (or anywhere in the same router file):

// FILE: /server/routes/legalDoc1Resolve.ts
// Add below the existing /resolve handler:

// GET /api/legal/doc1/availability?countryIso2=KE&regionKey=EAST_AFRICA
legalDoc1ResolveRouter.get("/availability", (req, res) => {
  try {
    const countryIso2 = String(req.query.countryIso2 || "").trim().toUpperCase();
    const regionKey = req.query.regionKey != null ? String(req.query.regionKey).trim() : null;

    if (!countryIso2 || countryIso2.length !== 2) {
      return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: "countryIso2 must be 2-letter ISO code." });
    }

    const enforce = getBoolConfig(CONFIG_KEYS.LEGAL_COVERAGE_ENFORCE, false);

    const resolved = resolveDoc1ByJurisdiction({
      countryIso2,
      regionKey,
      enforce,
    });

    if (resolved.blocked) {
      return res.json({
        ok: true,
        available: false,
        enforce: true,
        code: "LEGAL_COVERAGE_BLOCKED",
        reason: resolved.reason,
        selected: resolved.selected,
      });
    }

    // If not enforced, we still may be “unavailable” if we truly have no usable docs at all.
    const hasGlobal = Boolean(resolved.current.global.doc?.content);
    const hasAddendum = Boolean(resolved.current.addendum.doc?.content);
    const available = hasGlobal && hasAddendum;

    return res.json({
      ok: true,
      available,
      enforce,
      selected: resolved.selected,
      modes: {
        global: resolved.current.global.mode,
        addendum: resolved.current.addendum.mode,
      },
      warnings: resolved.warnings,
      reason: available ? null : "MISSING_DOC_CONTENT",
    });
  } catch (e: any) {
    return res.status(400).json({ ok: false, error: e?.message || "Availability check failed." });
  }
});


No other backend changes are required (router is already mounted at /api/legal/doc1).

2) Frontend: Signup availability widget (disable proceed if not available)

Create a small reusable component. You can drop this into your signup page where the user selects country/region.

New file: client/src/components/SignupAvailabilityGate.tsx
// FILE: /client/src/components/SignupAvailabilityGate.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

type AvailabilityResp = {
  ok: boolean;
  available: boolean;
  enforce: boolean;
  code?: string;
  reason?: string | null;
  selected?: {
    globalTarget: { docSet: string; docType: string; jurisdictionType: string; jurisdictionKey: string };
    addendumTarget: { docSet: string; docType: string; jurisdictionType: string; jurisdictionKey: string };
  };
  modes?: { global: string; addendum: string };
  warnings?: string[];
};

export function SignupAvailabilityGate(props: {
  countryIso2: string;
  regionKey?: string | null;
  onAvailabilityChange?: (available: boolean, enforce: boolean, payload?: AvailabilityResp | null) => void;
}) {
  const countryIso2 = useMemo(() => String(props.countryIso2 || "").trim().toUpperCase(), [props.countryIso2]);
  const regionKey = useMemo(() => (props.regionKey ? String(props.regionKey).trim() : ""), [props.regionKey]);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [data, setData] = useState<AvailabilityResp | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const ISO2 = countryIso2;
    if (!ISO2 || ISO2.length !== 2) {
      setData(null);
      props.onAvailabilityChange?.(false, false, null);
      return;
    }

    let cancelled = false;

    (async () => {
      setLoading(true);
      try {
        const qs = new URLSearchParams({ countryIso2: ISO2 });
        if (regionKey) qs.set("regionKey", regionKey);

        const res = await fetch(`/api/legal/doc1/availability?${qs.toString()}`);
        const j = (await res.json()) as AvailabilityResp;
        if (cancelled) return;

        if (!j?.ok) throw new Error((j as any)?.error || "Availability check failed.");
        setData(j);
        props.onAvailabilityChange?.(Boolean(j.available), Boolean(j.enforce), j);
      } catch {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setData({
          ok: true,
          available: false,
          enforce: false,
          reason: "CHECK_FAILED",
        } as any);
        props.onAvailabilityChange?.(false, false, null);
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      }
    })();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [countryIso2, regionKey]);

  if (!countryIso2 || countryIso2.length !== 2) {
    return (
      <div style={boxMuted}>
        <div style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>Availability</div>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.8 }}>Select a valid country code to check availability.</div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const available = Boolean(data?.available);
  const enforce = Boolean(data?.enforce);

  return (
    <div style={available ? boxOk : boxWarn}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "baseline", gap: 10 }}>
        <div style={{ fontWeight: 800 }}>
          Availability:{" "}
          {loading ? "Checking…" : available ? "Available" : enforce ? "Not Available" : "Limited Availability"}
        </div>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.8 }}>
          Gate: <strong>{loading ? "…" : enforce ? "ENFORCED" : "OFF"}</strong>
        </div>
      </div>

      {data?.selected && (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 8, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.85 }}>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "monospace" }}>
            Global: {data.selected.globalTarget.jurisdictionType}/{data.selected.globalTarget.jurisdictionKey} (
            {data.modes?.global || "—"})
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "monospace" }}>
            Addendum: {data.selected.addendumTarget.jurisdictionType}/{data.selected.addendumTarget.jurisdictionKey} (
            {data.modes?.addendum || "—"})
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {!loading && !available && enforce && (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 8, fontSize: 12 }}>
          This jurisdiction is not yet supported. Please check back later.
        </div>
      )}

      {!loading && !available && !enforce && (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 8, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.9 }}>
          Terms coverage is not fully configured for this jurisdiction. Admin may allow fallback temporarily.
        </div>
      )}

      {!loading && Array.isArray(data?.warnings) && data!.warnings!.length > 0 && (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 8, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.85 }}>
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>Warnings</div>
          <ul style={{ margin: "6px 0 0 18px" }}>
            {data!.warnings!.slice(0, 6).map((w, i) => (
              <li key={i}>{w}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

const boxBase: React.CSSProperties = {
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.15)",
  borderRadius: 12,
  padding: 12,
  background: "white",
};

const boxOk: React.CSSProperties = {
  ...boxBase,
};

const boxWarn: React.CSSProperties = {
  ...boxBase,
};

const boxMuted: React.CSSProperties = {
  ...boxBase,
  opacity: 0.85,
};

2) Frontend: generic component location
Create file (generic path)

Use one of these common patterns—agent picks whichever matches your repo:

client/src/components/legal/SignupAvailabilityGate.tsx

OR client/src/components/SignupAvailabilityGate.tsx

File content (use exactly):

// FILE: client/src/components/legal/SignupAvailabilityGate.tsx
import React, { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from "react";

type AvailabilityResp = {
  ok: boolean;
  available: boolean;
  enforce: boolean;
  code?: string;
  reason?: string | null;
  selected?: {
    globalTarget: { docSet: string; docType: string; jurisdictionType: string; jurisdictionKey: string };
    addendumTarget: { docSet: string; docType: string; jurisdictionType: string; jurisdictionKey: string };
  };
  modes?: { global: string; addendum: string };
  warnings?: string[];
};

export function SignupAvailabilityGate(props: {
  countryIso2: string;
  regionKey?: string | null;
  onAvailabilityChange?: (available: boolean, enforce: boolean, payload?: AvailabilityResp | null) => void;
}) {
  const countryIso2 = useMemo(() => String(props.countryIso2 || "").trim().toUpperCase(), [props.countryIso2]);
  const regionKey = useMemo(() => (props.regionKey ? String(props.regionKey).trim() : ""), [props.regionKey]);

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);
  const [data, setData] = useState<AvailabilityResp | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => {
    const ISO2 = countryIso2;
    if (!ISO2 || ISO2.length !== 2) {
      setData(null);
      props.onAvailabilityChange?.(false, false, null);
      return;
    }

    let cancelled = false;

    (async () => {
      setLoading(true);
      try {
        const qs = new URLSearchParams({ countryIso2: ISO2 });
        if (regionKey) qs.set("regionKey", regionKey);

        const res = await fetch(`/api/legal/doc1/availability?${qs.toString()}`);
        const j = (await res.json()) as AvailabilityResp;
        if (cancelled) return;

        if (!j?.ok) throw new Error((j as any)?.error || "Availability check failed.");
        setData(j);
        props.onAvailabilityChange?.(Boolean(j.available), Boolean(j.enforce), j);
      } catch {
        if (cancelled) return;
        setData({ ok: true, available: false, enforce: false, reason: "CHECK_FAILED" } as any);
        props.onAvailabilityChange?.(false, false, null);
      } finally {
        if (!cancelled) setLoading(false);
      }
    })();

    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
    };
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [countryIso2, regionKey]);

  if (!countryIso2 || countryIso2.length !== 2) {
    return (
      <div style={boxMuted}>
        <div style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>Availability</div>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.8 }}>Select a country to check availability.</div>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const available = Boolean(data?.available);
  const enforce = Boolean(data?.enforce);

  return (
    <div style={available ? boxOk : boxWarn}>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "baseline", gap: 10 }}>
        <div style={{ fontWeight: 800 }}>
          Availability: {loading ? "Checking…" : available ? "Available" : enforce ? "Not Available" : "Limited Availability"}
        </div>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.8 }}>
          Gate: <strong>{loading ? "…" : enforce ? "ENFORCED" : "OFF"}</strong>
        </div>
      </div>

      {data?.selected && (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 8, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.85 }}>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "monospace" }}>
            Global: {data.selected.globalTarget.jurisdictionType}/{data.selected.globalTarget.jurisdictionKey} ({data.modes?.global || "—"})
          </div>
          <div style={{ fontFamily: "monospace" }}>
            Addendum: {data.selected.addendumTarget.jurisdictionType}/{data.selected.addendumTarget.jurisdictionKey} ({data.modes?.addendum || "—"})
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {!loading && !available && enforce && (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 8, fontSize: 12 }}>
          This jurisdiction is not yet supported. Please check back later.
        </div>
      )}

      {!loading && !available && !enforce && (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 8, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.9 }}>
          Coverage is not fully configured for this jurisdiction; the platform may be running in fallback mode.
        </div>
      )}

      {!loading && Array.isArray(data?.warnings) && data!.warnings!.length > 0 && (
        <div style={{ marginTop: 8, fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.85 }}>
          <div style={{ fontWeight: 700 }}>Warnings</div>
          <ul style={{ margin: "6px 0 0 18px" }}>
            {data!.warnings!.slice(0, 6).map((w, i) => (
              <li key={i}>{w}</li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

const boxBase: React.CSSProperties = {
  border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.15)",
  borderRadius: 12,
  padding: 12,
  background: "white",
};

const boxOk: React.CSSProperties = {
  ...boxBase,
};

const boxWarn: React.CSSProperties = {
  ...boxBase,
};

const boxMuted: React.CSSProperties = {
  ...boxBase,
  opacity: 0.85,
};

Minimal integration snippet (copy/paste)
import React, { useMemo, useState } from "react";
import { SignupAvailabilityGate } from "../components/legal/SignupAvailabilityGate";

export default function SignupPage() {
  const [countryIso2, setCountryIso2] = useState<string>("US");
  const [regionKey, setRegionKey] = useState<string | null>(null);

  // You can choose strict behavior:
  // Recommended: canProceed only when available=true (even if gate off).
  const [canProceed, setCanProceed] = useState(false);
  const [gateEnforced, setGateEnforced] = useState(false);

  const allowProceed = useMemo(() => {
    // strict policy:
    return canProceed;

    // loose policy alternative (NOT recommended):
    // return gateEnforced ? canProceed : true;
  }, [canProceed]);

  return (
    <div style={{ display: "grid", gap: 12 }}>
      {/* country selector */}
      <label style={{ display: "grid", gap: 6 }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>Country</div>
        <input
          value={countryIso2}
          onChange={(e) => setCountryIso2(e.target.value)}
          placeholder="US"
          style={{ padding: "10px 12px", borderRadius: 10, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.25)" }}
        />
      </label>

      {/* optional region selector */}
      <label style={{ display: "grid", gap: 6 }}>
        <div style={{ fontSize: 12, opacity: 0.75 }}>Region (optional)</div>
        <input
          value={regionKey || ""}
          onChange={(e) => setRegionKey(e.target.value ? e.target.value : null)}
          placeholder="SE_ASIA"
          style={{ padding: "10px 12px", borderRadius: 10, border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.25)" }}
        />
      </label>

      {/* availability gate */}
      <SignupAvailabilityGate
        countryIso2={countryIso2}
        regionKey={regionKey}
        onAvailabilityChange={(available, enforce) => {
          setCanProceed(available);
          setGateEnforced(enforce);
        }}
      />

      {/* continue */}
      <button
        disabled={!allowProceed}
        style={{
          padding: "12px 14px",
          borderRadius: 12,
          border: "1px solid rgba(0,0,0,0.25)",
          background: allowProceed ? "white" : "rgba(0,0,0,0.05)",
          cursor: allowProceed ? "pointer" : "not-allowed",
          fontWeight: 800,
        }}
        onClick={() => {
          // Next step will call /api/legal/doc1/resolve and render the doc + acceptance checkbox
          // For now: proceed to “Terms Preview” step
        }}
      >
        Continue
      </button>
    </div>
  );
}

E) SQL Verification Queries (SQLite)

Agent 3 should run these (or equivalent through drizzle):

-- Targets coverage
SELECT doc_set, doc_type, jurisdiction_type, jurisdiction_key, active_doc_id, updated_at
FROM legal_doc_targets
ORDER BY updated_at DESC;

-- Latest docs per target (sanity)
SELECT doc_set, doc_type, jurisdiction_type, jurisdiction_key, MAX(created_at) AS latest_created_at
FROM legal_documents
GROUP BY doc_set, doc_type, jurisdiction_type, jurisdiction_key;

-- Acceptance integrity sampling
SELECT id, user_id, email_at_acceptance, country_iso2, region_key, accepted_at,
       LENGTH(combined_text) AS combined_len, combined_sha256
FROM legal_acceptances
ORDER BY accepted_at DESC
LIMIT 25;

F) Common Failure Patterns to Check

Gate toggle exists in UI but backend key is never read in resolver

Resolver uses “latest created” even when targets exist (non-deterministic)

Acceptance stores only doc ids and hash, but not combinedText snapshot (weak evidence)

UI assembles combined text client-side without server canonicalization (risk of mismatch)

Deep link doesn’t actually switch minitabs (listener missing at AdminDashboard root)

Coverage uses hard-coded country list and ignores discovered keys

Phone validation is UI-only; backend accepts invalid phone or wrong country mapping
